## Gunshot reports become a real hearing channel — radius broadcast through SfxWaves
- **Why**: `OnShotHeard` (belief-write) existed but was wired 1:1 — only the declared `ThreatCharacter` heard the shot, unconditionally at any distance, and possess-fired shots reported to nobody. Now that the debug-wave + procedural-audio layers exist, the report is a real perception event per `PERCEPTION_AWARENESS.md`.
- **`SfxWaves.Heard`** (new static event) + **`Report(origin, range, refDist, tag, shooter, visualOrigin?)`** — the semantic hearing channel, fired for every real report **regardless of `showWaves`** (the old `Emit` early-out made hearing debug-dependent; `Emit` stays visual-only so HUD/test previews never reach AI). Propagation is same-tick — real sound crosses earshot in ~0.1 s at street scale, and synchronous invocation inside the fixed-step fire keeps it deterministic under F8.
- **`ProjectileSystem.PlayShotSound`** now calls `Report` instead of `Emit`: semantic origin = `mount.FeetCenter` (new `WeaponMount.FeetCenter` — same ground-level body-center convention as `CombatController.Feet`), display shell stays at `MuzzleWorld`, shooter ref = the mount (receivers resolve `GetComponentInParent<VoxelCharacter>`).
- **`CombatController.OnSfxHeard`** subscribes in `OnEnable`/`OnDisable`, self-filters per receiver: tag must be `gunshot`, shooter must resolve to a different actor, `AmplitudeAt(distance) > 0` (the authored audio rolloff is the audibility gate — `shotAudibleRange` 50 m default), and the shooter must be who the listener is already tracking (`ThreatCharacter`) or watching post-fight (`watchTarget`) — a stranger's bang is ignored for now; third-party intel belongs to the awareness table later. Matching reports flow into the existing `OnShotHeard` — belief channel updates (`ThreatPos`/`LastSeenTarget`/`lastConfirmed` → cover zones re-paint, live orbit re-anchors, post-fight watch re-enters combat) while `contactSeen`/`LastSeenAt`/`convergeSince` stay on the eye channel — **a report is intel, not a sighting**.
- **Replaces** the scoped `ThreatCharacter?.GetComponent<CombatController>()?.OnShotHeard(Feet())` call in `Fire()`. Behavioral deltas: any *engaged opponent* in earshot hears it (not just the declared target — his declared threat is the shooter, which is the filter), hearing is now range-gated (previously infinite), and player-fired shots inform AI for the first time. `shotAudioEnabled=false` still suppresses the whole channel by design.
- Headless compile clean. **Playtest**: fighter A fires from behind/at range → B's belief snaps to A's position and his search/orbit re-anchors even with no LOS; watch F8 determinism and whether 50 m earshot feels right (`shotAudibleRange` slider); stranger fighters should not turn toward a duel they aren't in.

## Reload animation states 13/14 — authored poses in the studio, tap driven by the sim
- **Why**: the solver-override reload (v1 pivot IK → forearm folded into the upper arm; v2 authored-FK quat overrides → still read wrong, per playtest + screenshot). User directive: *author it like AimSweep* — real animation states with studio-tunable params, not a runtime solver fighting the pose pipeline.
- **Two new anim states** (`CharacterAnimation.AnimState`): `Reloading = 13` (standing) and `ReloadCrouch = 14` (reload arms over the crouch body channel — legs/lean/lower come from the `crouching→crouchDeep` depth lerp, arms/head/twist from the `reloadCrouch` param block).
- **`reload`/`reloadCrouch` param blocks** — studio-authored (`character_animator.html` defaults + sliders + tap preview, exported in the anim JSON; `Missing()`-fill in `LoadFromAnimJson` so old JSONs get sane defaults; `AnimParamsData.reload/reloadCrouch` + `asp[27..34]` upload to the GPU static-params buffer). Fields per block: `armSwingL/R`, `shoulderReachL/R`, `elbowBendL/R`, `forearmTwistL/R`, `headYaw/Pitch/Tilt`, `torsoTwist`, `tapReachL/R`, `tapBendL/R` — `tap*` are DELTAS added per pulse.
- **Arm composition = the AimSweep convention exactly**: `arm = RotationY(reach) · RotationByAxis(axis, sign·swing) · RotationZ(restZ)`, `forearm = RotationByAxis(elbowAxis, sign·bend) · RotationX(twist)` — `reach` is the same model-space Y channel the sweep modulates for directional input. Evaluated in the SAME branches in JS (`computeGroupRotation`), CPU (`VoxelCharacterAnimator.ComputeGroupRotation`) and GPU (`CharacterPoseCompute.compute`).
- **`tapPulse` channel (0..1)** — `ReloadGestureSolver` is now a **tap driver only** (no IK writes): `RoundSeated` stamps `SimClock.Now` → fast-attack/exp-release envelope → `animRef.tapPulse` → `instancedHandle.tapPulse` → `animData[instIdx*4+3]` (buffer stride 3→4). The support arm adds `tapPulse·tapReach` yaw + `tapPulse·tapBend` elbow — the press lands on the same tick as `reload_click`. CPU eval sites (WeaponMount weld, joint/eye/silhouette queries) mirror `ic.tapPulse` into `JointAnimator.tapPulse` so the weld rides the same pulse the voxels pose. Latched test poses (no seat events) get a soft periodic preview pulse. Sim-clock → freezes under F8.
- **Eyes on the work** — reload head defaults now pitch the face DOWN at the gun (`headPitch` 0.55 + `headTilt` 0.08; the vision/gaze cone follows the posed head, so he literally watches the reload). A tap-synced head nod was tried and **removed** — the head stays fixed on the work; only the support arm taps.
- **Role-assigned arms (Unity L/R flip fix)** — in-game the flip put the *authored* "support" (L-column) params on the arm that actually holds the gun: the gun hand did the reload action while the free hand held still. Params are now applied by **role, not gid**: L column = support/work arm, R column = gun arm, with direction-sensitive angles (reach/twist) mirrored by `reloadGunSide` (±1, `animData[3].y`). `ReloadGestureSolver` resolves it live from `mount.HandGid`'s parent arm (`GetParentGroup` → gid 2/3); `WeaponMount` does the same for the CPU weld so the gun stays in the posed hand. `tap*R` keeps its semantic: gun-arm counter-press deltas (0 = holds still, as authored). Studio gains a **"Gun on right"** checkbox to preview both assignments.
- **Drivers**: `CombatController` stationary lane picks `reloading ? (duck>1.02 ? ReloadCrouch : Reloading)` ahead of the armed/dice choices; `PossessController` picks the same when `cc.Reloading` (manual R) and gains **Alt+R / Alt+E** latched test poses (PRIORITY_DEBUG); `CityMap3D.debugAnimStates` includes both. Transitions morph through the standard pose-transition path — the aim→reload lerp the playtest asked for comes free.
- **SECOND ARM-IK PAIR stays** (phase-4 plumbing shipped last session — stride-7 `_InstanceIKData`, nibble-packed gids, `ikPair2Enabled`) but nothing drives it now — it awaits the rifle support-arm work; `ArmAimSolver.ReleaseInstant` still clears it. The aim solver is disarmed during reload (`armOk` requires `!reloading`), so no IK fights the authored arms.
- Headless compile clean. **Playtest**: reload standing + crouched → gun arm draws to chest-front, support arm reaches center and presses once per click; tune the pose in `character_animator.html` (states 13/14), `tap*` sliders shape the press.

## .stsound schema: per-layer `delay` field (studio + C# parity) — true two-stage clicks
- **`RecipeLayer.delay`** (seconds, any layer type): silent until `t = delay` — pitch-scaled by `coupleDecay` like decays — then the layer runs on its OWN timeline (ring/glide/crack/AM/phase all start fresh). Implemented identically in `audio_studio.html render()` and `ProceduralAudio.SynthFromRecipe` (`lt = t - delay` inside every renderer; identical skip keeps mulberry32 consumption aligned = bit-exact parity). A `delay` knob sits on every layer's row (ms typing like the other time knobs).
- Why over `echo`: echo repeats the WHOLE buffer in one timbre; a delayed second `attack` is an independently adjustable click (own tone/toneQ/cutoff/crack/decay/amp) — the "Cu-Lick" seat can be sharper/duller than the contact. Delayed sweeps/partials also compose (glide starts at layer start on the recipe timeline).
- All six `sounds/LiveTesting/reload_click*` files rewritten as delay-based two-stage designs: served = dull contact strike + brighter seat strike at 34 ms; `_am` (45 ms + AM lobes), `_deep` (42 ms heavy ch-unk), `_crisp` (22 ms t-lik), `_chirp` (chirp press + 30 ms snap), `_mech` (crack'd seat at 40 ms). Preset synced to served; `PROCEDURAL_AUDIO.md` schema text documents the field.
- Headless compile clean. **Playtest result**: user's tuned Variant-Lab child (bright twin strikes 6.4k/7.4k, 22 ms delay, seed 1097609) promoted into the served slot — `reload_click.stsound` now carries their recipe; the other five delay-based alternates remain for reference.

## Reload seat-click wired (RoundSeated → ReloadAudio) + starter recipe staged in LiveTesting
- **`CombatController.RoundSeated`** (new STATIC `Action<CombatController>` event — one subscription hears every fighter's wheel) fires inside the reload seat tick right after `cylinder++`, before `Notify()`. Seats deferred by `beatHoldsReload` fire when they actually seat — the click follows the physical wheel across mode exits, same law as the HUD's cylinder dots.
- **`ReloadAudio`** (new `Assets/Scripts/Sim/ReloadAudio.cs`, `SurfaceImpactAudio` precedent: `GetOrCreate()` spawned from the emit site so no seat resolves unserved) subscribes to `RoundSeated`, plays `sounds/reload_click.stsound` at chest height via `GetClip`+`PlayAt` (hot-reloads on studio save), emits a display-only `"reload"` SfxWave (AI reacts to "gunshot" only, by design), honors `FileMix` sidecar volume trims. `reloadAudioEnabled` mute toggle; near-field defaults refDist 1.2 m / audible 10 m.
- **Sfx tab**: new RELOAD fold between GUNSHOT and SURFACE IMPACT — toggle, level/ref/range sliders, ▶ preview at the listener, `last:` readout.
- **Starter recipe** staged at `sounds/LiveTesting/reload_click.stsound` (shadows the not-yet-shipped `sounds/reload_click.stsound` — iterate in audio_studio, then move it out to ship): attack `tone` bandpass click + partials + whisper noise tail. **v2 after playtest** ("too pickaxe" → light-switch snap): `crack` off (fracture = the mineral quality), ring cut to ~40 ms, attack tightened to ~14 ms, plus a `sweep` micro-glide (5.2k→2.6k, ~55 ms) for the spring-release "flick". **v3 — "Cu-Lick" two-stage family**: six candidates in `sounds/LiveTesting/` (`reload_click` served = strike + 32 ms echo seat + AM-lobed slide; `_am` AM-gate, `_deep` ch-unk, `_crisp` t-lik, `_chirp` pressure exciter, `_mech` ragged double-action) — audition in studio (bind LiveTesting dir), Save-As over `reload_click.stsound` to swap the in-game pick.
- **Studio preset**: `reload_click` added to `audio_studio.html` PRESETS (weapon group, after `ricochet`) — identical numbers to the staged `.stsound`, so the preset is the same starting point the file carries.
- Headless compile clean. Needs a playtest: possess R reload → a click every 1.5 s per spent chamber; combat reloads click identically (dice/TopUp/auto all funnel through the same seat tick).
- `ArmAimSolver`'s torso assist now composes three rotations about fixed pre-torso axes: **yaw blade** (existing, plus a neck-clamp share-up rule: when residual yaw after the torso's share would still clamp the neck past ±77.2°, the torso absorbs more so head + torso together reach `F`), **stance blade** (`torsoBladeShare` 0.5 / `torsoBladeMaxDeg` 40° — waist yaw swinging the gun-side shoulder's azimuth toward the aim azimuth, the boxing-stance lead; `SignedAngle` is handedness-correct automatically), and **stance pitch** (`torsoPitchShare` 0.25 / `torsoPitchMaxDeg` 12° / `torsoPitchSign` — hunch with target elevation, posture not aim).
- **v1 reverted on sight**: the first cut rotated about the aim direction `d̂` — a roll axis, so the whole torso tipped laterally ("tilting to the left" per playtest). Replaced with the waist-yaw blade. Blading is yaw, not roll.
- Gates: suspended while WALKING UN-AIMED (root owns heading) and inside the deep-duck band (the yaw would un-fold the crouch lean); **AimWalk now keeps the stance** (`bladeWhileAimWalk`, default on — aiming at a live target while moving makes the upper body track while legs run the route; the old all-locomotion suspension is gone per playtest). Arm + head solves unchanged — they live in the pre-torso frame, so the enriched `torsoQuat` feeds them through `torsoEff` automatically.
- Headless compile clean. **Gate** (AIM_RIG.md phase 2): dice-trace `muz-eye(r u f)` lateral should drop from ~0.12–0.20 m toward ~0.05 m; fighters should visibly blade into shots rather than aiming with arms alone.

## AIM_RIG phase 1 — unified head-aim channel (CPU = GPU = JS), headYawOverride retired
- **One quaternion channel for gid 1**: `VoxelCharacter.SetHeadAim(q, blend)` writes the SAME model-space quat to the CPU poser (`VoxelCharacterAnimator.ikOverrides[1]` — the generic override slot that already existed) and to a new GPU slot (`InstancedCharacter.ikHeadEnabled/ikHeadQuat`). The old CPU-only `headYawOverride` (radians, yaw-only, applied inside the head pose layer) is gone — `ClearHeadAim()` removes the override.
- **GPU stride 4→5**: `_InstanceIKData` packs a 5th float4 — slots: armA, armB, torso, **head**, meta — meta flags gain bit 2 = head; `CharacterPoseCompute.compute` blends it onto gid-1 `ownRot` with the shared `ikBlend` (`Slerp(authored, override, blend)`, identical to the CPU apply path).
- **Two producers, one channel**: `ArmAimSolver.headTrack` (0..1, default 1) solves `qHead` = `BasisDelta(forward → clamped dir)` toward the aim target in the SAME pre-torso frame as the arm solve — yaw clamped ±1.35 rad, pitch ±0.9 — full yaw+pitch track while aiming. `CombatController` eyes-on-threat now writes a yaw-only `SetHeadAim` quat during `SeekingCover` instead of the old radian override, and **yields to the solver whenever it is aiming** (`solverOwns` guard — the solver owns every IK slot while engaged). JS `ikAim.headTrack` was already the reference implementation — parity by construction.
- Headless compile clean. **Runtime gate** (AIM_RIG.md phase 1): rendered head == CPU head (probe eye) — AIM & SIGHT `sight`/`bore`/`eye→tgt` beams + head visibly tracking while aiming and during cover-seek legs, both fighters.

## Surface impact audio wired (ShotResolved family) + runtime Variant-Lab modulation + SFX panel preview
- **SurfaceImpactAudio** (new `Assets/Scripts/Sim/SurfaceImpactAudio.cs`, same subscriber family as VoxelDamageSystem's glass path, spawned by `ProjectileSystem.Fire` before resolution): on every solid non-glass `ShotResolved` it computes incidence = angle(-shotDir, normal) and muzzle distance, then routes the hit to a file pool: grazing+close (`ricMinIncidenceDeg` default 65 deg vs normal, `ricMaxDistanceM` default 40 m) -> "ricochet"; otherwise -> "impact". Seeded pick from the shot seed; material tags prefer `<pool>_<tag>_NN` files (stone/wood/metal/flesh map; only stone has files today) else untagged generics. Plays via `ProceduralAudio.PlayAt` + emits an SfxWaves wave for the debug layer - AI reacts to "gunshot" only, by design.
- **Folder layout (user-renamed)**: ONE pool folder `sounds/Impacts/` holds both families; pools split by FILENAME - names containing "ricochet" (ricochet_*, ricochet_stone_*, Thud_ricochet, ricochet_dl) form the ricochet pool, everything else (Impact_01, ...) the impact pool. `sounds/Impacts/LiveTesting/` is the EXCLUSIVE pool while populated (currently `Impact_01.stsound` + `ricochet_01.stsound` - exactly one file per pool); `GetClip`/`LoadRecipe` also shadow per-file.
- **Runtime modulation** (`ProceduralAudio.MutateRecipe` - C# port of the studio Variant Lab's mutateRecipe): when `modEnabled`, the picked recipe is deep-cloned and mutated per hit - layer amp/decay jitter, partial drop/grow/freq shift, attack tone/crack/chirp, noise lowpass, sweep fields, layer drop/dup - with `modJitter` (.12 subtle / .30 med / .55 wild) and `modLockMaster` (freeze duration/master/env/eq/echo). Seeded from the shot seed -> replay-identical children. One authored file now yields a natural family of variations.
- **SFX panel**: "SURFACE IMPACT" fold - master toggle, incidence/distance gate sliders, mix sliders, modulation toggle+jitter+lock-master, a `last:` decision readout (`Stone inc 63 deg dist 12.4m -> ricochet/ricochet_stone_03`), and a preview group for `Impacts/` (LIVE/shipped/shadowed tags, the play button resolves the real clip at the listener).
- Headless compile clean. Needs a playtest: grazing hits within 40 m play mutated `ricochet_01` children; everything else plays mutated `Impact_01` children (both from LiveTesting, exclusive pools).

## Sound naming: filename is the single source of truth
- **Studio (save time)**: `audio_studio.html` Save-As now sets `recipe.name` to the chosen filename before writing (both the showSaveFilePicker path and the non-Chromium fallback), and the name field refreshes — internal name can no longer diverge from the file. (This was the user's `Thud_ricochet_10_02` saved as `ricochet_01.stsound` case; a library sweep shows every other file already matches.)
- **Game (load time)**: `ProceduralAudio.LoadRecipe` overrides `r.name` with the loaded file's basename — the clip label in logs/debug objects is always the FILE that was actually used, even for recipes saved before the studio fix.

- **LiveTesting override** (`ProceduralAudio.ResolveLiveTesting`, inside `GetClip` so every consumer inherits it): `sounds/<dir>/LiveTesting/<file>.stsound` shadows `sounds/<dir>/<file>.stsound` - drop a recipe in a folder's `LiveTesting/` and every request for that filename plays yours (in-game testing); delete it and the shipped one returns.
- The old `ricochet/ricochet_01.stsound` (+meta) deleted; `LiveTesting/ricochet_01.stsound` now serves that slot when something requests it. NOTE: the recipe's internal `name` field is `Thud_ricochet_10_02` (the clip label in logs/debug objects) - file name is what matters for lookup, the field only labels the clip.

## Deep-recon stops re-resolve under the live map + UNEMBED names the blocker
- **Why**: the last run's only `REFUSE` class was deep recon (9 in 262 s): stops are stored as WORLD points chosen under an older field build and an older feet seed; dispatch seconds later validated them against a REBUILT field (re-scan every 0.4 s re-snaps the origin, the flood re-seeds from the new feet after each hop) - stale-by-construction: `cell-of fail` (point left the window) and `map unreachable` (flood moved).
- **Fix**: `ResolveDeepPoint` re-resolves the stored point at dispatch under the CURRENT field+flood (nearest reachable, walkable, non-swept cell within `DeepResolveM` = 3 m, cheapest path on ties). Nothing reachable near the stop = the stop dies honestly and the fan pushes on (a skipped stop is still not a sweep). Deep stage cells now also skip vehicle-swept cells (`IsDyn`).
- **UNEMBED detail**: the UNEMBED line now carries the 9-column `ExplainSquare` verdict (`center=ok NW=waist:vol:<id>#<cell> ...`) with the blocking volume id resolved and `MOVING`/`parked` flagged when it is a vehicle - the next embed identifies the exact volume.
- Headless compile clean.


## CORRECTION: Vinny clipped into a PARKED car's cabin (step-up), not a moving car - vehicles are now solid for bodies
- **My previous diagnosis ("a moving car drove over him") was an assumption and wrong for this incident** (the user took cover on the car and clipped inside; the log cannot show car motion either way). The moving-car fixes (not-cover, swept-footprint mask, guard regression) stay - they are real - but they did not address this.
- **Measured cause** (`vehicle_490_touring.stasset`, 70x75x155 vox @0.01 m = 0.7x0.75x1.55 m, solid padding only 2-3 voxels): 2326 columns have a top <= 30 cm and the cabin/tub floor sits at 10-15 cm - inside the body's `stepUpMax` (0.15). `MoveWithCollision -> TryBodySquare -> TrySquareStepUp` therefore lets a body STEP UP onto the floor and stand in the open tub (the 5 body-column samples hit air). The map never sees it: `ProbeGround` casts from deck+1.6 m and reads the canopy/roof as ground (`walk=0`, flood 1 cell) - map and mover disagree, and since the square is legally free my embed check (`SquareFree`) did not fire either.
- **Fix at the shared primitive** (`VoxelCollisionWorld.ColumnFreeDetail`, the atomic body-occupancy law behind field cells, both movers, corridor sweeps and possess): a VEHICLE volume (`veh_*`) counts as solid for bodies across its WHOLE yawed volume, including open-cabin air (`PointKind.VolumeOpen` + vehicle id). Bodies can no longer occupy the tub nor step up onto running boards/floor (the raised square's waist/chest samples are inside the volume); cabin-side cover stands stop being walkable so they cannot be picked. Bullets/LOS (`Raycast`) unchanged - open cabin air still passes shots. Buildings unaffected (rooms stay walkable). Padding is 2-3 cm so outside cover is not eaten.
- **Pocket escape** (`TickUnembed` extended): triggers on EMBEDDED (square blocked, 0.4 s) OR SEALED POCKET (actor flood < 12 cells / no walkable cell, 1.0 s); returns the fighter to a breadcrumb (last position with a flood >= 200 cells and a walkable own cell, <= 15 m) else the nearest free square; clears the live leg/route; logs `UNEMBED (<reason>): <how>`.
- Offline selftest unaffected; headless compile clean. Needs a playtest: take cover on a parked touring car from the street and cabin sides.


## Moving vehicles: not cover, priced out of the MeMap, unembed escape + guard-reset regression fix
- **Run (2026-10-28 11:55, 148 s)**: reload-from-cover worked (Tan + Vinny reloaded behind `block_36_building` and parked cars; 32 shots), until Vinny's reload-cover stand was beside a MOVING test car (`veh_-895474`): he ended embedded in the car volume (`me walk=0`, flood = 1 cell) and sat at (2.1,-60.4) with `rchk=1 leg=1` for 40+ s.
- **REGRESSION (mine) fixed**: my "reset the stuck guard on re-route" change in `StepRouted` reset the guard whenever `BuildStamp` changed - but the field rebuilds EVERY `scanInterval` (0.4 s) while a threat exists, so the guard (0.6 s window) could never expire: any wedged dice leg lived forever. Now the guard resets only on a NEW leg (`!built`), never on a re-route.
- **Unembed**: `TickUnembed` (0.2 s cadence): if `world.SquareFree(Feet())` is false for 0.4 s the body slips to `TryNearestFreeSquare` (r <= 3 m; the possess wedge-escape primitive) and logs `UNEMBED` (Debug + trace). Cars re-register over standing bodies (vehicles collide with characters one-way: the body can't enter a car, a car can drive over a body).
- **Moving cars are never cover**: `VoxelVehicle` tracks motion (`IsMoving` = agent driving-and-not-parked, or moved within 1.5 s; `Velocity`), `IsVolumeMoving(volId)`. `SolidVolumeSet.ExcludeFromCover` hook (set by VoxelVehicle) makes `QueryCoverWalls` skip moving vehicles at scan time (no zones form on them; they stay obstacles); `StandReachable` also rejects them at pick time (covers the 0.4 s between scans) for COVER faces, deep-recon stops and reload cover. Parked cars (scenery, pulled over) remain valid cover.
- **Vehicles invalidate the MeMap dynamically**: `CoverField.dynBlock` (+ `ClearDynBlock/MarkDyn/IsDyn`) repainted every scan by `VoxelVehicle.PaintMovingFootprints` - cell p is marked iff `p - v*s` lies inside the car's current collision volume for some s in [0, 1.5 s] (answered by `VoxelCollisionWorld.SamplePoint`, so yaw/shape agree with the body blocker). `BuildDistMap` prices marked cells `DynBlockCostMult` (12x): routes avoid them but a body standing in one can still flee; stands and `DicePickStepCell` cells inside are rejected.
- Offline selftest (`Tools/dda_selftest/run.sh`) still ALL PASS (155732 checks).
- **Not done (proposal)**: cars still never yield to characters (one-way collision); a traffic-side "character in swept path -> brake" would remove the cause instead of the symptom.


## Reload from cover + point-blank convergence + pocket vision
- **Run (2026-10-28 11:44, 169 s)**: duel ended with both fighters 0.6 m apart, cylinders FULL (6/6), `see=1`, `aim=1`, `fire=losing lock` for 20+ s. The HUD's "reload check - spent 0/6" was the STAND-hold countdown (ramp pending), not a reload - there was no reload loop (6 reloads per fighter, all fine). Real causes: (1) `err` stuck at 20-37 deg with `tgtD=0.1 m` (aim point 10 cm from the muzzle): an ANGLE from a muzzle that close is meaningless (4 cm lateral = 22 deg), so `convergeSince` never held and the trigger never opened; (2) both stood in a 2-3 cell pocket (`MOVEFAIL ... reachable 2-3`, own cell `walk=0`) so every move resolved to STAND.
- **Convergence is range-aware** (`hitRadiusM`, default 0.1 m): converged = angle <= `aimGateDeg` OR the bore passes within `hitRadiusM` of the aim point (`missM = muzzle->point distance x tan(err)`).
- **Reload is a committed, vulnerable action -> cover first** (`diceReloadCoverMaxM`, default 8 m, 0 = off): when the ramp check says reload (or the wheel is dry) and the target is in sight (`contactSeen || contactHold`), `DiceReloadCoverSeek` picks the nearest flood-reachable Hidden stand (`StandReachable`, same gate as COVER faces; score = path cost + 2 x metres closer to the enemy; max 2 tries per need) and walks there FIRST; arrival starts the wheel with no extra roll (`DiceReloadNow`, then hidden-target -> shallow recon). No cover / not exposed -> reload in place as before. Opportunity fire rides the run (wheel not yet open).
- **Pocket vision**: `MOVEFAIL` now logs `flood=<reached cells>` and `grid=` (7x7: `@` me, `R` reached, `w` walkable-unreached, `#` unwalkable, `X` outside). New `RELOAD` trace line when no cover exists. HUD: ramp-pending while a hold timer runs reads `holding -> reload check`.
- **Still open**: how two fighters end up in a 2-3 cell pocket with own cell `walk=0` (mover enters cells the map calls unwalkable?) - the next run's `grid=` will show it.


## Stuck-guard state poisoned every next dice leg (the real "refused position" cause)
- **Found in the 2026-10-28 11:23 trace** (124 s duel, 35 shots, no wedge, 0 MOVEFAIL): 20 `REFUSE` lines, each stamped the SAME tick as its `DEST` with `short` = the full leg length - zero movement - while the map reported the destination reachable. Also deep recon exhausting in the same tick it started (Vinny x2, Tan, then Vinny again) and the fighter leaving combat.
- **Root cause**: `StepToward`'s per-leg stuck guard (`stuckBest`, `swingStuckT`, `stuckNudges`) was not reset when a dice micro-leg started, so a leg inherited the prior leg's exhausted budget / stale clock and aborted on its first tick; self-perpetuating until some SeekingCover transition happened to reset it. Explains the first run's 14 s wedge too.
- **Fix** (`CombatController`): `StepRouted` resets the guard whenever a leg is (re)materialized. `StandReachable` (same snap as `BuildRoute`) now gates `DiceCoverCands` and `DiceDeepSnap` - `ZoneReachable` only proved SOME Hidden member reachable; the dispatched stand cell could be unreachable (`reach=N`). Deep-exhaust verdict reports `swept N/3` (a skipped stop is not a sweep). `diceRunning` now carries `deep N/6` / `recon N/6` (REFUSE lines showed a stale `RELOAD 5/6`); `REFUSE` no longer prints a stale `routeFail` for routed legs.
- **Trace**: `BoreDiag` adds `off=<perp dist of muzzle from the eye->target line>(h v)` - the number that actually matters (body-frame `r` swings to +-0.65 m while the body is still turning in, so it isn't the shoulder offset).
- **Run findings (shootable set)**: 29 `muzzle blocked` episodes, median 0.08 s, max 0.48 s, 0 sustained verdicts; fire 35 shots in 124 s vs 11 in 68 s before; while aiming avg eye/muzzle/both visible = 38.7/35.7/33.7 (Vinny), 50.2/43.1/40.5 (Tan); both==0 on 20-25% of snaps.
- Bug file: `docs/known_issues/runtime/STUCK_GUARD_STATE_POISONS_NEXT_LEG.md`.


## Dice moves map-derived + shootable set (aware != shootable)
- **Issue**: first auto duel - STRAFE/ADVANCE picked raw geometry points (`feet + perp*2.5`, `feet + toward*step`) then asked the map; ~12 refusals/s while wedged. Separately, the eye saw slivers the muzzle could not (IK aims shoulder->target; muzzle sits ~12.5 cm to the gun side of the eye) -> `see=1` + `bore buried` livelock.
- **Moves**: `DicePickStepCell` picks the destination FROM the actor flood (`MeMap`): STRAFE/peek = reachable cell nearest the ideal lateral point (detour-filtered), ADVANCE = reachable cell with max GEODESIC progress toward the anchor (`DiceAdvMap`, flood from the anchor, cached per BuildStamp). Reachable by construction -> `BuildRoute` cannot refuse. No reachable cell serves the intent -> the face resolves to STAND (`DiceStandResolved`: posts `can't strafe/advance: <why> - stands`, `MOVEFAIL` trace line, STAND hold) - never a failed leg. Peek with no room = peek in place (rise).
- **Shootable set**: `CoverSensor.VisibleSet` gains `shootable` / `shootCentroid` / `muzzleGated` (samples visible from eye AND live muzzle). `CombatController.muzzleShootGate` (default on): while the gun is up (aim blend >= 0.7) the probe also rays from the muzzle; `canSee` stays AWARE (eye), new `canShoot` gates the trigger (`FireHold = "muzzle blocked"`), the aim point and fired point use the shootable centroid, and aware-but-unshootable counts toward the sustained "can't fight from here" verdict (dice posts `muzzle blocked - eye sees, gun can't`; authored mode relocates). Recon / TARGET SPOTTED still keys off awareness (`contactSeen`).
- **Spec**: `docs/systems/AIM_RIG.md` (focal-point torso/head/arm(s) rig - the structural fix for the eye/muzzle offset; phased, not started).
- **Watch**: stands from refused faces are now explicit (`MOVEFAIL`); `e/m/b` in SNAP is now live whenever aiming (not only under diceTrace). Authored (non-dice) mode also uses the muzzle gate.


## Dice trace — vision into refused legs + eye/muzzle/bore (diagnostics only, no behavior change)
- **Why**: first auto run showed Vinny wedged ~14s (ADVANCE/STRAFE "no corridor", `see=1` + `bore buried`, `e2e=BLOCKED`) with no way to tell WHY a leg was refused or why the bore was buried while the eye saw.
- **New trace lines** (`Logs/dice_trace.log`): `DEST` (pre-flight per STRAFE/ADVANCE/peek: dest cell + me cell: in-field, walkable, snapped cell, reachable@cost, field state, straightClear, anchorBlk) and `REFUSE` (on every dead dice leg and dead cover seek: short-by, noCorr, routedLeg, the `routeFail` string, dest/me cell diag, mapReach cells, straightClear).
- **SNAP + FIRE edge** (`bore buried` / `cone off`): `e/m/b` = samples visible from EYE / MUZZLE / BOTH (new `CoverSensor.HasMuzzleProbe`, `MuzzleProbeOrigin`, `LastMuzzleVisible`, `LastBothVisible` — extra rays only while `diceTrace` and a weapon is equipped), plus `muz-eye(r u f)` body-frame offset, `eye→t` / `muz→t` LOS to the aim target, `tgtD`.
- Muzzle probe origin is the CURRENT muzzle (hip when not aiming) — read e/m/b only while `aim=1`.


## Dice combat — turn-based scaffolding removed, decisions event-driven
- **Issue**: manual-mode scaffolding leaked into real-time auto: 5s cooldown between every decision (stacked ~15s per move), GUNS DOWN trigger-cold after reload, mimic lockstep - none in the Tab H design.
- **Change** (`CombatController`): `ArmRoll(label, autoHoldSec=0)` - auto is event-driven (next roll fires the tick the previous action lands); `diceRollCooldownSec` is MANUAL-only. New `diceStandHoldSec` (1.5s) gives STAND / refused-move-stands a real duration so a no-op face can't spin at tick rate. Removed `diceGunsDown` (+ fire gate + HUD text), `diceMimic` + `MimicSlave` + lockstep in `DiceClick`/HUD, and write-only `diceParkUntil` / `diceLastEvidence`. Sub-roll collapse was already in code (fixed `diceStepM`, nearest hidden cover).
- `DebugHUDManager`: dice-lab Fight preset now runs BOTH fighters on auto dice (was manual+mimic); MANUAL toggle/click/pin row kept for branch testing. `CoverMetricsPanel` label updated.
- Bug file: `docs/known_issues/runtime/DICE_TURN_BASED_SCAFFOLDING_VS_REALTIME.md`.
- **Watch**: chained rolls now fire at tick rate between legs (continuous motion); only STAND, reload, recon dwell and legs take time. Tune `diceStandHoldSec`.

## SimClock — combat decisions move to a fixed-step sim clock (0.08s)
- **Issue**: combat/dice clocks read `Time.unscaledTime`, so F8 (`timeScale=0`) froze bodies but not decisions (cooldowns, recon dwell, starve, reload pacing kept running); cadence also depended on frame rate.
- **Change**: new `Assets/Scripts/Sim/SimClock.cs` — static `Now`/`Dt`/`Ticks`, fixed `Step=0.08` (RE tickInterval), driven by scaled frame time x `Speed`, `Paused` + `StepOnce()`. Driver is a hidden DontDestroyOnLoad object at execution order -10000, auto-created BeforeSceneLoad. `Now` is offset by 1000s so -1 'never' sentinels still read as long-ago at boot.
- `CombatController`: 90 lines retargeted `Time.unscaledTime/unscaledDeltaTime` -> `SimClock.Now/Dt` (HUD yaw-monitor smoothing at the YawForce fold intentionally stays on frame time). `CoverMetricsPanel` (LastSeenAt/LastShotAt ages), `DebugHUDManager` MARK stamp and `DiceTrace` header now on the same clock.
- Body/pose easing (`Time.deltaTime`) untouched. Week-budget link deferred (expose `SimClock.Now`/`Ticks` only).
- Side effect to watch: fire cadence and holds now quantize to 0.08s steps.
- **F8 fix**: stress-test spawn + diagnostics rebound F8 -> F11 (they shared F8 with the `CityMap3D` freeze; no scene overrides existed). HUD hints updated. Pre-existing: `StressTestSpawner.beamCycleKey` F7 still collides with CityMap3D's F7 waypoints toggle.
- **Frozen step**: while F8-frozen, `.` (period) advances the world exactly one 0.08s tick (`SimClockDriver`: timeScale set for one frame, forced tick, back to 0). Free-cam authority lapses for that one frame. `diceManual` itself is unchanged (still click-driven).


## Combat beat lab — rebuilt as a real-asset 3D sterile field
- Gutted the 2D DOM sim entirely (walls[], tickVinny/tickTan, moveTo, search, fire, stamps, split logs, live chips) -- all removed; the lab is now a clean visualization + decision instrument to layer onto.
- Right rail is a Three.js viewport (three@0.160.0 importmap + OrbitControls -- same convention as VoxelAssetStudio): loads the REAL cover_arena.stasset via a JS port of StAssetReader.cs (16-byte header + LE uint16 X-major grid) + Civilian1.json as Vinny; surface-culled InstancedMesh per voxel; MobColors palette ported from C#.
- Arena mirrored X for Unity-true orientation (editor's 'UNITY VIEW' semantics). Vinny centered on real voxel bounds, feet on probed ground.
- Requires repo-root local server (same as VoxelAssetStudio): python -m http.server -> /docs/systems/COMBAT_BEAT_MACHINE.html. Status line shows asset stats; error message explains the server requirement.
- Scale note: NPCs are canonically 0.64m (CHAR_VOXEL_SIZE 0.01, 64 vox) -- Vinny's 62-voxel span is correct, not a bug.


## Combat beat lab — audit gaps 1+2 closed
- Orbit hops now claim UNVISITED HIDDEN COVER ZONES (per-scenario `zones` sets, evaluated live vs the belief anchor with losClear) sorted by bearing progress inside the +-arc -- replaces ring-point math. Arc exhaustion (not a hop cap) ends the orbit -> final look.
- PINNED implemented per CombatController.cs:1028: refused rise + no rim (swing dest inside wall) + relocate fails -> PIN -> DUCK -> NONE parked -> re-verdict on pinRecheckSec clock -> SETTLE->TOP-UP->RISE re-probe. Vinny gets it via swing-rim check; Tan (no swing verb) via direct wall-verdict refusal -- mirrors the real scriptedMode path.
- Both fighters: tryRelocate (arms search + claims fightable zone), pinFighter, pinned/pinUntil fields; flowchart NONE/PINNED node was already mapped.


## Combat beat lab — audit pass (believed-faithful mirror)
- Duel flipped to real layout: Vinny spawns bottom -> stand A south of the 1.9m wall, Tan spawns top -> stand B north of the 0.9m wall; refused/corner Tan flipped to match. Tan defaults to HIDE.
- Banner reports dead fighters as DOWN instead of retaining a stale last-beat label.
- Fidelity fixes found by auditing CombatController.cs: (1) swing-return seam now routes through DUCK + afterSettle=TopUp like the real EnterBeat chain so search.pending is consumed into the first hop (was SETTLE -> infinite re-swing loop); (2) orbit anchor now uses per-agent lastSeen (belief) instead of live foe position -- AimAnchor() is LastSeenTarget ?? ThreatPos; contact/hit/shot-heard sites update it via noteContact.

# Recent Changes — Steel City: Mob Sim

**Last Updated**: October 20, 2026 (Phase 3 — Engagement Beats IMPLEMENTED: authored rhythm — settle/top-up/rise/engaged — owns the arm inside cover)

---

- **Dice flight recorder — `Logs/dice_trace.log` (read-only diagnostics, no behavior change)** (user: manual/mimic still not in sync between Tan and Vinny; wants max visibility into both actors during drills): new `DiceTrace.cs` static writer (previous run rotates to `dice_trace_prev.log`; `Logs/` is gitignored) + `CombatController` recorder (`diceTrace` toggle, `diceTraceSnapSec` = 0.5s). Same `t{unscaledTime}` stamps as the SEAM panel history. **Edge lines** (the frame it happens): `STATE` state/beat · `SEE` raw contact gained/lost + vis voxels/cone · `HOLD` display-contact · `ARM` ExposureArmed · `AIM` solver armed/stowed · `POSE` · `DUCK` snaps (>0.4/frame) · `FIRE` gate transitions · `SHOT` · `FLAGS` all dice flags · `CLICK` OK/REFUSED with the reason (incl. mimic-lockstep partner gate) · `ROLL` which chain branch each consume took + posture at that instant · `DICE` mirror of every DicePost · `ENTER`/`EXIT`. **SNAP lines** every 0.5s per fighter: state, flags, seam countdown, see/hold/lastSeen age, vis/cone, **eye↔eye LOS (`e2e`, independent of silhouette sampling + cone)**, eye height, armed/aim/err/blend, fire gate, duck/targetDuck/pose, cylinder/shots, pos/yaw/faceErr/dist. HUD: SEAM header **MARK** button drops a flag + forced SNAP of both fighters. Labels `vinny`/`tan` set in StartPitDuel.
- **AimSweep↔Aiming bob — contact-hold on the DISPLAY channel, raw signal untouched** (user: "tan oscillating behind cover as vinny aimed and shot at him"; analysis found the real mechanism — not posture): `aimPointWorld = canSee ? vis.centroid : AimAnchor()` retargeted the solver between TWO world points (live centroid vs belief anchor) on every 1-frame voxel miss — head+gun whipping between them, the swing itself sweeping the cone off-target = self-exciting oscillation. New `contactHold` = `canSee || LastSeenAt < 0.45s` + `liveAimPoint` caches the last resolved centroid: the aim-source pick and the `Aiming`/`AimSweep` pose pick now read the debounced channel — live still supersedes, the anchor is a true fallback that only engages on genuine loss. `contactSeen` stays RAW for the fire gate, verdicts, and evidence feed (the trigger needs per-tick truth). Why Tan flapped while Vinny didn't: identical code, asymmetric inputs — Vinny's sightline to the exposed Tan resolved steadily, Tan's to Vinny crossed the cover-lip occlusion edge; whoever resolves first locks Aiming (steady cone stays resolved), the other keeps sweeping and self-excites.
- **runInBackground enabled** — play mode kept pausing when the editor window lost OS focus (alt-tab to report a bug = sim frozen, resume on refocus). `ProjectSettings.asset` `runInBackground: 1` — the sim ticks while you type reports.
- **Recon releases on sight — SHALLOW now matches DEEP** (user: "the recon step isn't releasing when target sighted, supposed to kick back to move roll — consistent with the other overrides"): the Update-level REACQUIRED interrupt covered `diceDeepActive` (hop leg + dwell) and the shallow dwell had its own `contactSeen` early-verdict — but the shallow PEEK LEG rode to arrival first, the only recon phase that didn't release on sight. The interrupt's condition is now `contactSeen && (diceDeepActive || diceReconCheck)` — a sighting cancels the peek mid-step or the dwell mid-sweep, restores lookScan, posts the spotted verdict, arms the next MOVE roll. (`diceDeepActive` still drives the `reacquired` label + `ResetSearch`; shallow posts `target spotted`.) NB the `AimSweep` pose seen parked-awaiting is the armed-blind sweep (`ExposureArmed && !contactSeen`), not a stuck recon.
- **GUNS-DOWN window — a dice reload's price is one reposition** (user rule: "the only time we gate shooting is directly after a reload segment, and before a movement roll — as soon as the movement roll executes, shooting is allowed again; this will lead to emergent dodging while firing"): `diceGunsDown` sets when the ramp verdict executes a reload (`DiceRampCheck` → `StartReload`) and clears the moment ANY face executes in `DiceExecFace` — STAND included, the die was consulted. `FireHold` gains `"guns down"` (DiceActive-scoped — authored combat untouched). The armed-roll label surfaces it: `next turn roll · GUNS DOWN` on all `ArmRoll("next turn roll")` sites via `DiceNextAwaitText`. Net cycle: `move → shoot → move → dry → reload → [trigger cold] → move roll → shoot` — reload forces one reposition, then the firefight resumes.
- **ENGAGEMENT ROLL — frame entry consumes the first face automatically + dry-cylinder auto-reload leak closed under dice** (user design: "when i activate the fight, that acts as a combination first roll and initiate Engagement — each actor will correctly roll to do Something (even a legit stand) but then come to conclusion after firing a total of 6 rounds; instead if both actors stay in view/empty mags on Round 1 it still comes to a definitive stop for the next manual roll"): `EnterCombatCore`'s dice branch now calls `DiceConsume(0)` after arming — the first face resolves instantly at activation (a real STAND is then an honest rolled outcome, not an unrolled standing default); the manual cadence takes over from the next armed decision. **Auto-reload leak**: the fire tick's `if (cylinder <= 0 && State != InCover) StartReload()` ran unguarded under `DiceActive` — a dry mag bypassed the ramp roll entirely (same bug class as the bore-buried relocate). Under dice, a dry bore now parks + arms `diceRampPending` as `reload check — cylinder DRY` — the definitive stop for the next manual roll; mid-execution (leg/dwell/seek) is skipped since the completion lands its own ramp arm, and the pending-chain order (ramp → recon → deep) keeps a queued recon alive behind the reload decision. Other `StartReload` sites audited — SeamEval's is `ArbActive`-gated, TopUp lives in `TickBeat` (early-returns under dice).
- **Dice MIMIC mode — Tan lockstepped to Vinny's click + seam timers visible everywhere + Fight-mode dice preset** (user request: "a toggle for tan called Mimic — when i click roll for vinny, it rolls for tan, step by step… the roll will be locked out until both are ready to roll again; for auto this lock step is disabled and they are truly unleashed"; and "set tan to Fight mode and then dice+manual+mimic"): `pitTanMode` defaults to `Fight`, and `StartPitDuel` now applies the dice-lab preset under Fight **before** `EnterCombat` reads `DiceActive` at frame entry: Vinny → `diceArbiter+diceManual` (driver), Tan → `diceArbiter+diceManual+diceMimic` (slave), with `seamArbiter`/`scriptedMode` cleared on both (a stale `scriptedMode` silently vetoes `DiceActive`). Hide/Dummy modes untouched — the preset is a Fight-only lab config; all flags stay runtime-live for post-start toggles. `CombatController.diceMimic` (per-fighter, toggled on TAN's row in the SEAM panel) slaves the fighter's rolls to the partner's click. `DiceClick` refactored: `DiceRollGateOpen()` is now THE shared gate predicate (armed + seam-elapsed + parked + not reloading — the click, the driver's lockstep check, and the auto tail all run it); `DiceConsume()` is THE shared post-gate chain (pendings → `DiceBoundary(forceFace)`). Driver-side: click refuses until `MimicSlave().DiceRollGateOpen()` — button shows `· wait {name}`; then consumes both — **mirrors the CLICK, never the face** (a slaved die still rolls its own 1d6). Slave-side: his own `DiceClick` hard-refuses when `diceMimic` — the 🎲 button reads `MIMIC — partner drives`, pin row hides. Auto never locks: `MimicSlave()` requires `diceManual` on the partner, and `diceMimic` is never consulted on the clock path — unleashed by construction. **Partner lookup**: `MimicSlave()` = `ThreatCharacter.GetComponent<CombatController>()` (the OnShotHeard relay's established pattern) — works because the duel's threat refs already point at each other. **Seam-timer visibility** (user: "the timers per seam/interval are hidden"): the await text + `DiceRollReadyIn` countdown was gated behind `cc.diceManual` on every surface — now rendered in BOTH modes: SEAM panel gains an `await {text} · {n}s / READY` status line for auto, the metrics overlay's dice line shows it unconditionally with a `· READY` marker when elapsed, and the in-world bubble drops its `diceManual` gate (auto fighters show `await {text} · {n}s` overhead too). **Tan manual/auto**: nothing was Vinny-special — `TickDice` is per-controller, so Tan gets the identical click/clock paths + mimic semantics for free.
- **Dice AUTO mode = manual mode with the clock playing the click — literal parity** (user request: "test the auto quantum decision engine rolling the dice to ensure it works as designed in manual mode", then correction: "auto mode does not match manual mode — no 5-second seam, functions don't match"): the quantum skeleton is GONE. Auto now runs `DiceClick`'s exact path: the auto tail of `TickDice` gates on the same fields the click does (`State==HoldPosition ∧ diceAwaitRoll ∧ Time≥diceNextRollAt`), consumes `diceAwaitRoll=false`, drains the identical pending chain (`DiceRampCheck → DiceReconRoll → DiceDeepRoll`), and calls the shared boundary — `DiceManualBoundary` renamed `DiceBoundary(int forceFace)`, auto passes 0 (no pin). One seam clock for both modes: `diceNextRollAt` (+`diceRollCooldownSec` 5s per decision); `diceQuantumUntil`/`diceQuantumSec`/`diceStarveSec` deleted (the skeleton's parallel clock and its starve-END stub — deep recon's own exhaust-END covers the hidden case now). `EnterCombat` arms the first decision in BOTH modes (`diceAwaitRoll=true`, `diceNextRollAt=0` — first boundary/first click legal immediately). The `!diceAwaitRoll` self-heal rearm hoisted out of the manual gate — shared parked-failsafe for a landed turn with nothing armed. **Shared mechanics:** micro-leg step + recon dwell moved out of the `diceManual` gate (strafe/advance legs actually WALK in auto — the skeleton never stepped them); deep-recon sighting-interrupt + `InCover` re-park gates dropped `diceManual` → `DiceActive`. Net: MANUAL off = the engine rolls for you — face → ramp → recon → deep fan → reacquire/exhaust — identical semantics, identical 5s seams, identical posts; the only difference is who resolves `diceAwaitRoll`. Also added `Assets/Editor/CompileCheck.cs` (`SteelCity.Setup.CompileCheck.Run`) — Unity-batch compile hook for when the editor is closed (the fast path stays `csc @compile.rsp` per `docs/core/HEADLESS_UNITY_COMPILE.md`).
- **Dice arbiter MANUAL mode — user-paced turn engine + SEAM window resize** (user direction: "make it user driven turn based — nothing happens until I click the simulated die… a seek cover or strafe pauses and waits for the next roll to determine where"): `CombatController.diceManual` gates the roll on a HUD click instead of the quantum timer. **Turn model:** each click = ONE decision — boundary runs END (ceased-target only; the starve clock is skipped — user-paced) → ramp count-check (a taken top-up consumes the click) → 1d6 face → `DiceExecFace`. **Face wiring (first real moves):** STAND holds (fire rides — `TickExposureFire` unchanged); ADVANCE legs 1.2m toward `AimAnchor`; STRAFE-L/R and COVER-L/R queue a **sub-roll** — the next click resolves the "where": stride = roll×0.5m perpendicular to the threat line (executed via `StepToward` with the full stuck/nudge/collision law); cover pick = roll−1 mod N over Hidden, reachable, side-filtered `EnumerateZones` candidates sorted nearest-first, dispatched through `CommitSearchSeek` (certified stands, routed legs — the move IS the turn; arrival → `SetState(InCover)` → new dispatcher branch re-parks to `HoldPosition` + posts "turn complete"). A dice-dispatched leg aborting now re-parks as a verdict instead of falling through the authored orbit's `ExitCombat` — `else if (DiceActive) resumed=false` guard on the seek-abort path. **HUD:** `SeamPanel` dice block gains a MANUAL toggle + `🎲 ROLL — <await text>` button (text names the pending decision: "first roll" / "stride (1d6×0.5m)" / "which of N spots" / "next roll") + a 6-button pin row (`DiceClick(1..6)` forces the face for branch testing). `DiceSubRoll` surfaces the last sub-roll in the quantum line. **Ramp = its own roll** (user: "when he's done the move and enters reload phase, we roll if he's going to reload — weighted by shots fired"): every completed move (STAND, leg done, seek arrived, degenerate-stand fallbacks) sets `diceRampPending` → next click runs `DiceRampCheck` — literal d6, faces ≤ spent take the top-up (1 gone = reload on a 1; dry = any face), posted as `ramp r/6 vs spent N` either way; the face roll moved out of the boundary into its own click. **Dispatcher-ordering bug fixed**: the `InCover → TickBeat()` branch ran before the manual-dice re-park check, so a completed seek never released the turn (ROLL stayed dead at the arrived cover) — re-park now claims `InCover && diceMoveDone` first. SEAM window reshaped to a wide short band: `min(sw−2m, 800) × 208`, fighters side-by-side in fixed 382px columns, adaptive cap still short of the main HUD's bottom edge + scroll. `DiceClick` refuses clicks mid-turn/mid-reload/mid-seek — the move must land first. **Dice readout in the metrics overlay** (user: "the roll log is hard to decipher — replace the combat info log with a relevant dice log when DICE is on"): `diceLog` is a per-line ring (24 entries, one per decision — `t148.6 #2 f4 COVER-L → ramp 2/6 vs spent 0 — clear`); `CoverMetricsPanel` swaps the inert authored beat bar + engagement-flow chart for a `── dice ──` block (MANUAL/auto · quantum · face · sub-roll · awaiting · verdict + last 10 log lines) and trims the sensor block to field/tested/pick — heights/walls/rejects are authored-chain tuning noise under the roll. Combat telemetry (state, cover, cadence, shots, exposure, lastSeen) untouched — still the relevant combat info. **In-world combat bubbles** (user: "billboards — informative bubbles that display the current action during the sequence, bold and informational, with times and distances"): `DrawCombatBubbles` runs on the always-on overlay path (DrawModeTicker's slot — before the panel-visible gate, so bubbles show with the HUD hidden) projecting a 192×48 box over each fighter's head (resolved via `chunkManager.RenderCam` — the camera the voxel RT actually renders from, which swaps between follow cam and map cam; fixed v1 bug where bubbles projected through a stale cached camera and never appeared): line 1 = live action (amber = executing leg/seek/reload · blue = awaiting), line 2 = the metrics feeding it (dest distance counting down, `→ volId Xm`, `+1 in Ns · full Ns`, `spent N/6`, threat distance). Dice shows `f4 COVER-R — which of 5 spots`/`STAND — holds`/etc; arbiter shows running+verdict; authored shows beat+state. **Movement faces simplified to one-roll-one-action** (user direction): the sub-roll layer is deleted — STRAFE-L/R is now a fixed lateral step (new `diceStepM` Inspector field, default 2.5m) perpendicular to the threat line, ADVANCE steps the same 2.5m forward, and COVER-L/R dispatches immediately to the NEAREST valid Hidden zone on the rolled side (candidates already sorted nearest-first). Turn cycle is now face-roll → move → ramp roll → **SHALLOW RECON** → next face: the recon bearing roll (own click after the count-check) maps d6 → swing-left/rise/swing-right — swings step a 0.55m peek leg off the threat line, rise stands tall in place. Gun holds the anchor (FaceTrack + AimWalk/AimSweep), `lookScan` sweeps the head, and the settle verdict reads `contactSeen` → logged `target spotted` (refreshes evidence) vs `still hidden` (the future DEEP RECON signal). Recon also counts as armed-posture for duck/pose so a rise actually stands. **Recon dwell**: the peek no longer snaps back on arrival — landing starts a `diceReconDwellSec` (default 5s) sweep: IK aim holds the anchor via FaceTrack, lookScan keeps sweeping, and only THEN does the sight verdict post. Bubble shows `recon — eyes on last-known / sweep 4.2s`. **DEEP RECON** (the diagram's TARGET HIDDEN path): a still-hidden recon verdict now queues the deep-recon roll — the die picks the orbit sweep side (1-3 left / 4-6 right) and the existing orbit machinery runs it for real (`ArmSearch` anchors on the belief point, `TryOrbitStep` walks probe hops). Each hop landing re-checks `contactSeen`: REACQUIRED rejoins the turn cycle; an exhausted fan posts `deep exhausted — he was never there` and ENDs combat (`ExitCombat`) — the one chance spent. **3-stop fan → FLANK THE FRONTIER (v2)** (user: "maybe change what the orbit search is entirely — a 3-stage flanking mechanism, come up with a better design than the ring"): the geometric ring's flaw was ordering stops in absolute space the flood couldn't serve — a "perfect" arc point across a wall is a dead dispatch (instant `no corridor` skips, `flood serves 1166/4096` = his pocket only connects 28% of the field). Stage positions are now drawn from `map.Reaches` — cells the flood can actually WALK — scored by bearing-error to the rolled side's ~30/60/90° progression + radial-error vs the standoff, Hidden-state door cells preferred (−6 bonus); a bearing bucket with nothing reachable falls back to a push-in stop (nearest reachable cell to the anchor — close the range, re-look — posted honestly, painted violet). Positions walkable by construction; walls degrade the fan honestly instead of silently skipping. Cover snap still prefers a Hidden stand within `DeepSnapM` of the chosen cell; dead cover legs retry the bare point. Walk in → stand → lookScan at the anchor → verdict; misses chain, the last stop's empty sweep is `deep exhausted` → END. A sighting still ejects mid-anything (REACQUIRED → next roll). **Sighting interrupts the hunt**: `contactSeen` during deep recon now stops the orbit mid-leg or mid-dwell — cancels the seek, re-parks to HoldPosition, posts `reacquired`, re-arms the next MOVE roll (the diagram's REACQUIRED edge). Previously the orbit ran to hop-arrival while the fire channel took the glimpse as a shot — fire still rides everywhere; the hunt just doesn't continue past the answer. **Roll owns ALL movement** (user: "he decides to move on his own — we want the override to reset the entire sequence so we are waiting on a new roll"): the buried-bore relocate in the exposure tick ran every frame regardless of dice ownership — a spotted target + 1.2s of wedged muzzle self-dispatched `TryRelocateToFightingCover` while the panel read `await next turn roll`. Under `DiceActive` it now posts the verdict to the dice log once per blocked episode (`bore buried — can't fight from here`, stamped `MaxValue` so it can't spam) and the next roll decides; the authored relocate path is unchanged. Audit: `TickBeat`/`EnterBeat` already early-return under dice, the arbiter is `ArbActive`-gated, the contact-edge `TryNearestCover` is `!DiceActive`, and `EnterCombat` parks under dice — the bore path was the last un-gated dispatch. **Blocked legs report honestly** (user: "what if he rolls advance into a wall — should faces be gated?" — no gating: the die decides, geometry resolves): `StepToward` returns true on arrival AND stuck-abort (0.6s no-progress + 3 sidestep nudges spent, distance re-verifies); the dice leg completion now distinguishes — arrival posts `move complete`, abort posts `blocked — leg aborted N m` (warn) and still ends the turn into the reload check. Deep-recon bare stops get parity with cover legs: an aborted hop is a SPENT stop (pushes the fan or exhausts), and a wedged recon peek sweeps where he stands. **Dice legs are now corridor-routed** (user: "advance directly from cover into a wall isn't desirable — if we can determine a path forward, that's acceptable, Dijkstra or something"): all dice micro-legs (strafe/advance/recon peek/deep bare stops) step via `StepRouted` — the same Dijkstra flood + chain + string-pull the seek legs walk — so a wall in the way is ROUNDED, not bumped. `allowBare=false` on the dice call: when the flood can't serve the step point the leg refuses at the line and posts `no corridor — can't reach the step point` (warn) instead of walking blind into geometry; authored legs keep the bare-line fallback. **ACQUIRED-target cycle + the 5s seam** (user: "acquired → tried to roll a move → the override kicked right on dice roll … target-acquired should let the dice move him emergently during the firefight, and each roll needs a ~5s cooldown as a seam between decisions"): recon is now the HIDDEN-target phase — when `contactSeen` is latched at the ramp verdict, the cycle is face → ramp → face (emergent repositioning while fire rides); recon/deep pending only arms when he's lost. Sighting-during-the-seam no longer burns rolls: `DiceReconRoll`/`DiceDeepRoll` early-out to `target spotted`/`reacquired` verdicts when contact arrived before the click. `ArmRoll()` centralizes every `diceAwaitRoll` arming and stamps `diceRollCooldownSec` (5s default, Inspector-tunable) — `DiceClick` refuses during the seam; the ROLL button, bubble, and metrics line all show the countdown (`· 3.2s`). Fire rides the whole seam — the pause is for the operator's eyes, not his gun. **Deep-recon fan: stops retry bare + in-world markers + honest abort posts** (user: "deep recon quits after 1 stop — he skips past cover and lands on the last one before lookScanning and ending"): a dead cover leg (`noCorridor` — the certified stand cell can sit outside the flood even when the zone cell reaches) advanced the fan SILENTLY, so stops 1-2 skipped invisible and only the last stop swept. Now `DiceDeepRetryOrNext()`: a dead snapped leg falls back to the bare arc point for the SAME stop (the position is the contract — a dead corridor must not skip it) posting `stop N cover unreachable — walking the bare point`; a bare death spends the stop and posts `stop N unreachable — pushing to stop M`. **3-stop markers**: amber X-ray cubes paint all three fan points at `DiceDeepRoll`; live = green (cover leg) / blue (bare leg), spent dims grey; cleared via `ResetSearch` at fan end. **Stale-anchor bug fixed along the way**: dwell-verdict reacquires now `ResetSearch()` — previously `searchActive` stayed latched so a second deep recon's `ArmSearch` skipped reframing and orbited the OLD last-known. `DiceSubResolve`/`diceSubPending` removed. **Structured dice log**: `diceLog` entries are now `DiceStep` records — rolled / outcome / next-roll plain-english + a kind tag — and the COVER+COMBAT panel renders them color-coded (amber = action dispatched, green = completed/clear, red = warn/END, grey = hold) as `t… ROLLED f4 COVER-L` → `→ seeks nearest cover left → block_36…` → `next: reload check`. Last 6 decisions shown. **Dice-leg at-the-ready pose**: `diceLegActive` legs now request `AimWalk` (gun-forward armed gait, IK riding the tracked anchor) and hold `duck 0` instead of falling to the stationary lane's Idle/duck-fold — strafe/advance reads armed and aiming at last-known, not sliding. **True strafe gait**: `StepToward`'s destination-facing was overriding the turret — strafe legs now write `FaceTrack(AimAnchor())` after it (last write wins the frame) so he steps laterally with his torso on the threat. **Awaiting runtime verify:** START DUEL + tick DICE + MANUAL on a fighter — he should park until clicked; COVER-4 should pause for the sub-roll then walk; unreachable picks park as verdicts, never exit.

- **Dice arbiter PHASE 1 ported into CombatController — `diceArbiter` flag + the skeleton cycle runner live** (per `plan-dice-arbiter-port.md`; user direction: "kick it off … set the old combat systems to off by default to prevent cross contamination"): THE ROLL now runs inside the real motor behind its own per-fighter flag. `diceArbiter` (public bool) + `DiceActive` sibling of `ArbActive`; precedence hard-coded — `ArbActive = seamArbiter ∧ !diceArbiter ∧ !scriptedMode` so the systems can never cross-contaminate a live frame, and the HUD toggles are mutually exclusive (ticking one unticks the other; seamArbiter stays default-off). **Cycle:** `TickDice`/`DiceBoundary` run at the HoldPosition park — a quantum is ATOMIC (runs its full `diceQuantumSec` window while `TickExposureFire` fires freely — opponent events are feed, never interrupts), then the boundary reckons: END checks → **ramp count-check** (real `spent/6` rule already — dry bore = 100%, `StartReload` parks the channel on a taken roll) → **the roll** (literal 1d6). Skeleton executes STAND for every face — park, fire rides, `FaceTrack` holds the anchor. **Feed guards:** heard shots and the contact-sighting loop write `diceLastEvidence` (frame stays fed — never `seamNow`); `seamNow` latches only under `ArbActive`; `TickBeat` early-returns under dice (beats are authored/seam posture). **Entry:** `EnterCombat` under dice skips the authored first-pick → `HoldPosition` + fresh dice fields + face-the-anchor (FCFS frame entry). **END stubs:** ceased target → END, or no contact AND no evidence for `diceStarveSec` (8s) → END-exhausted — the starve clock stands in for DEEP RECON until phase 6; kill-confirm eyeballing lands there too. **HUD:** SEAM row + window carry an exclusive `DICE` toggle; dice mode swaps the radar block for the cycle log (`quantum #N face F (NAME)` + verdict + rolling history via `DicePost`, same format as `SeamDone`). Fields: `diceFace/diceBearing/diceCycle/diceQuantumUntil/diceParkUntil/diceLastEvidence` + `DiceFaceNames[7]` (1 STRAFE-L · 2 ADVANCE · 3 STRAFE-R · 4 COVER-L · 5 STAND · 6 COVER-R). Headless compile clean. **Awaiting runtime verify:** START DUEL with Vinny=DICE vs Tan=ARB (or authored) — Vinny should park, roll a face each ~1s quantum, fire on sight, top-up on the ramp roll, and starve out ~8s after losing him.

- **COMBAT_BEAT_MACHINE.html — new Tab H "ENGAGEMENT — the roll": the simplified directional combat design** (built piece-by-piece with the user — "drastically reduce and simplify everything"): the whole engagement collapses to ONE live frame + THREE organs + FOUR flows. (a) **ENGAGEMENT frame** — combat is a live condition, not a state machine: entered on any threat registration (visible contact OR heard shot); firing is live the entire time (turret model: legs resolve movement, torso+arms IK-aim like a turret, trigger fires on any valid solution — movement is the only open question). (b) **MOVEMENT ROLL** — a literal geometry-agnostic **1d6**: faces 1 strafe-L · 2 advance · 3 strafe-R · 4 cover-L · 5 stand · 6 cover-R (dots numbered like die faces, legend = face key). One cycle = roll → bounded move → stop → count-check → recon. (c) **CYLINDERS** — left/right D-lobes hosting that side's two maneuvers; the 3 internal arrows are a **1d3 bearing pick** (back/lateral/forward) then geometry resolves the concrete target; a dry bearing bumps to the next, all three dry = channel spent (negative memory built into geometry, not flags). Maneuvers carry gait: cover = ducking gait, strafe = standing, advance = upright. (d) **RELOAD ramps** — 6-chamber trapezoids on the return lanes: every completed move reads the cylinder ("N spent, 6−N left") and rolls once — chance = spent/6, 6/6 → 100% mandatory. Reloading is emergent accounting, never scored. (e) **RECON two-stage**: SHALLOW = the rise/peek look (verdicts SPOTTED ↑ into the roll / HIDDEN ↓ graduating downward); **DEEP RECON** below = the orbit search renamed — bounded arc on ONE freshest-evidence anchor (heard shots re-anchor mid-sweep — no anchor queue), visits unvisited hidden stands leg-by-leg (checked = spent), resolves exactly two ways: live target → **REACQUIRED** (green bypass lane, far-left margin, carries the live-synced anchor straight into the roll — no redundant shallow re-check) or nobody → **END** terminator at the frame edge (starvation, not a named verdict). (f) Every element clickable into `beatDetail` with full contracts (data-h keys: frame/legend/fan/recon/deep/hidden/reacq/disengage/dispatch/reload/ramp + 6 maneuver faces); svg children carry pointer-events:all. Decision layer summary: d6 channel + d3 bearing + spent/6 reload — three dice replacing 10-intent argmax + blackboard + probeDeadAt/fireDeadAt/zeroStreak/arbBlocked bookkeeping. (g) **Rules locked with the user** — *quanta are atomic*: a dice cast is one complete move and opponent events can never interrupt it (a mid-move sighting is already answered by the live trigger; a loss edge rides to arrival where the boundary look calls "dark") — only self-side events abort early: blocked leg → bearing spent · self death · authority/possession. *Kill-confirm*: a ceased target does NOT end the frame — the last-seen anchor must resolve to an eyeballed body via deep recon, so END has two flavors: CONFIRMED (body verified) vs EXHAUSTED (swept, nobody — he got away). *FCFS*: the first registered threat owns the frame; a second contact never re-anchors until the frame ends. All five updated contracts live in DETAIL_H (frame/spotted/hidden/deep/disengage).

- **COMBAT_BEAT_MACHINE.html — new Tab G "STACK — the three tiers"** (user request — "improve my understanding … design a diagram to best reflect the hierarchy of the combat system"): the hub-and-spoke tabs show what the machine DOES over time; Tab G is the architectural view — how it's organized. Three horizontal tiers: T1 DECIDE (BLACKBOARD node with input chips + SEAM EVAL hub — scores arbScores[10], argmax dispatches) → T2 ACT (the 4 program families MOVE/PROBE/RELOAD/HOLD, chips = the 10 intents each serves) → T3 EXPRESS (the Beat motor vocabulary — LEGS, RISE, SWING, TOP-UP, ENGAGED, PARKED + a SHARED row for SETTLE and DUCK). Right rail = the OVERLAYS (SHOT / AIM-TRACK / EXPOSURE) cutting through all tiers inside a dashed frame. Bidirectional spokes carry ▲ verdict labels; a green dashed `up` edge from SETTLE to SEAM EVAL marks the honest wrinkle — tier-1 logic lives inside a tier-3 beat. Verdict vocabulary strip at bottom. Every node carries a click-contract naming the real symbols (SeamEval, ArbMoveHidden/Fightable, TryOrbitStep, CommitSearchSeek, EnterBeat, BeatAllowsFire, seamNow/seamBreath, probeDeadAt/fireDeadAt/arbBlocked). Also corrected the stale "arbiter rebuild pending" note on the tempo panel.

- **Seam arbiter ported into CombatController — the Tab-F model driving the real motor** (user direction: "give F a shot … we can live test it and build its own HUD GUI to read his intention in real time — informational only"): `seamArbiter` (public bool, per-fighter) switches dispatch from the authored `afterSettle` chain to a scored seam. `Beat` stays the posture channel (poses, `BeatAllowsFire`, `peekAim` unchanged); the `Settle`/`None` cases + a new HoldPosition poll become THE SEAM — `SeamEval()` scores 10 intents on the live blackboard (contactSeen, TryZoneAt-cover, cylinder fraction, staleness, probeDeadAt, fireDeadAt, orbitLive/orbitDone, pinned) and dispatches the winner's verb through existing machinery: MOVE → `ArbMoveHidden` / `ArbMoveFightable` / `TryOrbitStep` → `CommitSearchSeek` (certified centroids, atomic legs), PROBE → `EnterBeat(Rise)` (refusal falls to Swing internally — the geometry-selected mode; both refusing marks the stand `probeDeadAt`), RELOAD → `TopUp`, StandDeliver → `Engaged` (or parked-hold at HoldPosition — `BeatAllowsFire` already covers the open-ground shot), Watch/PinnedGate → `None` + recheck clocks. Every authored terminal becomes a verdict post: contact@crest/@rim write `contactSeen` + a zero-dwell `SeamEval()` interrupt; zero-contact bumps `zeroStreak` (survives hop arrivals — it IS the transit/recon cadence: `orbitLegRecon` lands as a scored boost, not a schedule); bore-buried marks the face + `fireDeadAt` (suppresses StandDeliver at that stand and steers Engage — breaks the buried → re-deliver → buried loop); leg-abort avoid-lists the zone (`arbBlocked`) and instant re-evals from the HoldPosition park. Orbit bookkeeping (`ArmSearch` / `searchVisited` / `searchProgress` 90° cap) unchanged — OrbitSearch is a dispatch and FinalConfirm is `orbitFinalLook` + Rise with the same exit semantics. `seamNow` interrupt latch: contact edge, heard shot, and blocked legs all bypass the 0.35s `seamBreath` floor. Threat registration still dispatches instantly — the first cover pick IS the zero-dwell eval (`arbRunning` labels it). Scripted mode bypasses entirely (`ArbActive` gate). HUD: `DuelStatusRow` gains a compact ARB line; the full readout lives in the standalone **SEAM window** — `DrawSeamWindow`, a bottom-left `BeginArea` drawn outside the fold system (persistent while other tabs/folds are driven): per fighter — ARB toggle, running program, verdict, blackboard chips, timestamped verdict history, the rasterized 10-axis intent radar (cached `Texture2D`, Repaint-only), and all 10 scores in FIXED order with the argmax marked (stable rows = diffable frame-stepping). Headless csc compile clean.

- **COMBAT_BEAT_MACHINE.html** — real-asset sterile field, Vinny dressed + armed + on stand. cover_arena.stasset (192×48×192 @0.1m, JS port of StAssetReader incl. SKEL tail → named attachment points). Civilian1.json → ClothingSystem "Suit Blue" outfit (regions 3/4/6→126 black fabric) + rest pose (arm groups 2/8 & 3/9 rotated ±90°Z about shoulder pivots, voxel-true) + SW_Model_10 revolver welded to right_hand via full WeaponMount.cs port (grip align + rest basis B + authored attachRotation — unmirrored file space, no +180 comp). Standing on authored cover_stand_a (95.5,2,24.5) facing +Z toward cover_stand_b. file:// CORS guard. LAB hooks: vinny/gun/hand/stand/arenaGrid.

- **COMBAT_BEAT_MACHINE — parity pass: live chart, split traces, equal machines** (user feedback: "stop treating vinny so special" + "live updating per actor + split event log"): Tan upgraded to the full verb set — `armSearch`/`startOrbitHop`/`checkOrbitDone`/`driveSearch`/`fire`/`hitFoe`/`wallEnds` are now agent-agnostic via `foe(g)`; he arms searches on zero contact, runs orbit legs with his own numbered hop markers (per-agent `search.marks`), blind-drops on `dropAfterSec`, takes a real final look, and can kill Vinny (hp 3 symmetric; dead pose; survivor exits to WATCH). Flowchart now shows live occupancy chips per node (grey=Vinny, tan=Tan, node glows, both=yellow). Event log split into per-actor trace columns (VINNY | TAN), `log(who,cls,msg)` routing — world events (stamps/injects) post to both; `setBeat` auto-logs every beat transition as a trace line. Arena rail is sticky (map top → controls → logs bottom) so left-side edits feed a running sim live.
- **COMBAT_BEAT_MACHINE.html — interactive beat/seam flowchart + scenario arena** (user request — a visual instrument seeded with the real combat logic): `docs/systems/COMBAT_BEAT_MACHINE.html`. (a) Clickable flowchart — all 4 states + 7 beats + orbit + exit, edges labeled with the actual verdict text, each node carrying its contract and defaults. (b) Editable tempo/constants table — the real `beatDwell`/fire/movement/search fields; edits feed the arena sim. (c) Mini arena — CSS-shape sandbox running a mirror of the beat machine: walls carry heights (tall refuses the rise, low solves it), the swing pick uses the same wall-end rule, LOS is a real segment-vs-rect test, orbit hops paint bearing-numbered markers, tracers/muzzle flashes fire, and the log narrates in the console's own voice. 5 scenario presets (duel / refused-rise / corner contact / orbit / street spill) + Tan hide/fight + injectable events (shot heard → re-anchor, spotted → 50/50, wall destroyed → invalidate). (d) **Stamp tools** — drag on the arena to spawn tall/low walls or erase; stamped geometry mutates LOS live mid-fight and persists through reset (the sim's stand-in for a voxel write). Duel layout corrected: walls now sit between fighters and stands (they were behind them, giving instant mutual LOS).
- **Swing→Engaged float fixed — refused rise left `riseDuck = −1` poisoning the engaged fold** (playtest: "stand and deliver in this awkward floating pose" — corner contact looked wrong): `SolveRiseDuck()` returns −1 on refusal, and that sentinel survived into `Engaged` — the stationary duck arbiter eased `anim.duck` toward a negative fold, over-standing the rig (pelvis lifted, feet off deck). Rise→Engaged was immune — a solved rise leaves `riseDuck ≥ 0`. Fix: the Rise/Engaged duck target is now `max(0, riseDuck)` — an engaged body never folds below stand.
- **The world owns the expensive geography now — the bake the user's instinct called for** (user research: "should we be baking this data into the map itself so fighters don't need to calculate it themselves"): `WalkLayer` already existed as the world-anchored, write-invalidated shared cache — `groundY`/`floorMat`/`FitOk` were already bulk-filled per build — but the two EXPENSIVE channels still re-probed per field per fighter per scan. Now baked: (a) the **footprint square** — `SqOk`/`SqBad` per world cell, keyed by a quantized spec token (`half`@1cm · `stepUp`@1cm · `bodyH`@0.25m — functionally-identical specs share, a real spec change poisons and re-probes), probed via the same `TryBodySquare` the movers run so certified ≡ executable stays literal; (b) the **edge transitions** — `eg[]` per world cell, 8 dirs × (tested, open), mirrored onto the neighbor under the opposite dir on write (the test is symmetric). `CoverField.EdgeOpen` now: field memo → world `TryEdge` → compute → `SetEdge` (fields keep their local memo so hot paths stay array reads). Invalidation covers the new channels: a dirtied cell clears its own edge row AND the back-pointing dir bit on all 8 neighbors (a changed cell can poison a neighbor's midpoint). Result: the second fighter's field and every subsequent scan serve square+edge verdicts from cache — the Dijkstra floods degenerate to array reads on hot cells; only genuinely-new or just-damaged world cells ever probe. Canonical-tile gate (0.25m) unchanged — non-canonical windows fall back to lazy probing exactly as before. HUD walk-layer readout already surfaces cached/invalidated counters for verification.
- **Clean-inspection baseline — every debug visual defaults OFF** (user request: "set all debug rays and beams and visuals to off — I'll turn on as needed"): the default-true offenders → false: `VisionSensor.showCone`/`showDetectMarks` (per-frame cone + detection beams on both fighters), `CoverSensor.tintWallFacing`/`showImpactMarks`, `VoxelCharacter.showGizmo`, `WeaponMount.showGizmo`/`debugLogs`, `ProjectileSystem.showTracers` (HUD toggle: Combat tab → firearm → tracers — likely the first one to re-enable for duels), `CombatController.yawWatch` (console cost, not a beam — re-enable via inspector when chasing a flap). Sensor `showX` flags, `showSpreadCone`, `showConeFill`, `showTargetRay`, `showSightBeam`, `showDebugRays` were already off-default — untouched. New master gate `DebugHUDManager.fighterBeams` (default off) controls the per-frame body-square/skin/cert/destination beam paint — toggle lives in the cover fold (`detection — CoverSensor` → `fighter beams`), and toggling off runs one clear pass so painted corpses can't linger.
- **Flood edge verdicts memoized per build — the combat perf regression's dominant term** (playtest: "tremendous degradation during combat" after the cert-unification batch): `EdgeOpen` computed `TryColumnTop` + skinned `SquareFree` (~70+ samples) on EVERY call — the Dijkstra flood asks ~12.8k edges per map, twice per undirected pair, and multiple maps flood per scan per fighter (`meMap` every restamp — i.e., every 0.4s scan while data is live — plus `anchorMap` on anchor moves plus re-materialized leg routes). Today's batch also raised the per-sample cost on top (ColumnFree 3→5 heights = +67% on the square term; `Walkable`'s footprint now runs the full `TryBodySquare` instead of the old cheap cell-composed reads). `edgeFlags[cell]` now holds 8 dirs × (tested, open): a computed verdict stamps BOTH directions (the edge test is symmetric) and repeats within the build become array reads — flood maps sharing a build amortize each other's probes. Same lazily-cached contract as the existing `WalkOk/FitOk` cell flags.
- **Swing peek grounded in wall geometry, not the hidden band — wall-side bias removed** (user: "inadequate side peeking logic favoring wall side over open side they can actually see over"): `TryStartSwing` measured the step-out boundary via `CoverField.TryZoneEdge` — the farthest *Hidden* zone member along ±lateral. A threat-shadow metric, not geometry: corner shadows wrapping the wall end overshoot the real corner (long exposed step); clipped/asymmetric shadows undershoot it (`rim+margin` lands still behind the wall → `SwingSideScore` reads Hidden → peek-at-wall); tied scores fell to the stale committed `PeekSide` roll. Now the wall's physical ends come from `sensor.walls` marks on `CoverObstacleId` (cover-facing only, `outDir·zoneOutDir > 0.7`) projected onto the lateral axis; each side's base extends to `max(hiddenRim, wallEnd)` — undershooting geometry is fatal, overshooting the shadow is merely conservative. Tied scores now pick the **nearer** end — the open side wins instead of the roll. Bug `SWING_PEEK_SHADOW_RIM_VS_WALL_END`.
- **Corridor vertex clip + infinite nudge shuttle — the arena corner wedge closed** (playtest: Vinny shot in the back while `swing-p0` yaw-flapped at a volume corner): two stacked defects. (a) `CorridorFree` sampled every **0.12m** — a convex corner vertex bites the swept corridor inside a narrower window than the spacing → a certified leg the mover's ~3cm steps still clipped. Now **0.03m** — a certified leg is literally step-by-step executable. (b) The stuck guard reset on ANY body motion: a ±perp nudge moved him >0.14m → full budget reset → re-wedge → indefinite shuttle. Stuck is now **goal progress** (`wpDist < stuckBest − 5cm`); a sideways shuttle burns the 3-nudge budget → honest `leg aborted`. Second pass on bug `SWING_STEP_OUT_CORNER_CLIP`.
- **Commit to certified ground — `cover unreachable (0.20m out)` verdicts**: all four stand-commit sites (initial pick, relocate, `CommitSearchSeek`, scripted `GoTo`) committed the painted zone centroid verbatim — a centroid averaging certified AND uncertified cells. He'd route to the nearest legal cell then die on the final ~0.2m stretch to a point the skinned square can never host. New `CertifiedStand` snaps every commit through `NearestWalkable`, which now searches ring-1 **and ring-2** (was ring-1 only — wider pockets kept raw points).
- **Map cert ≡ mover — one primitive answers "can this destination host the body"**: new `VoxelCollisionWorld.TryBodySquare` = `SquareFree OR (step-up offers ∧ SquareFree-raised)` — the whole `TryMove` question as one shared primitive. `CoverField.Walkable` consumes it for the footprint term (was a cell-composed `FootprintOK`/`OverlapClear` probe set that could diverge point-for-point); `PossessController` and `CombatController` movers consume the same. `FootprintOK`/`OverlapClear`/`ProbeFloor` retired. `bodyStepUp` dead literal `0.45f → 0.15f` (stamped over by `stepUpMax` at every build — a trap, not live behavior).
