The structural change: **rise and swing dwell merged into `peekAim`** — a blind peek now physically presents the bore at last-known instead of staying stowed ("eye leads" still holds, but the gun paints memory while it probes). The `searchAim`/`swingAim` booleans collapsed into `armOk + peekAim + searchAim`.
- **Converged = held, not sampled** (user: "IK aim during opportunity should wait for actual target acquisition"): `solver.LastAngularErrorDeg` is explicitly one frame stale — a same-frame retarget read the error against the OLD aim point, so a scout-forward-aimed gun could pass the gate and discharge on the acquisition edge while the bore still pointed forward. Added a settle latch: `convergeSince` tracks continuous `Blend ≥ 0.7 ∧ error ≤ aimGateDeg`; the acquisition edge (`!contactSeen → canSee`) resets it, and the trigger needs `fireSettleSec` (default 0.15s) of live convergence. A moving target that pushes error outside the gate drops the latch mid-contact too — honest tracking, not a one-time flag.
- **Blind-fire fix — the trigger now requires a resolved probe** (playtest: Vinny fired during the entry seek and twice mid-transit on pure belief): `TickExposureFire`'s fire gate checked armed/converged/cadence/rounds/bore-clear but never `canSee` — any aim channel holding the gun up (scout `searchAim`, `swingAim` dwell, disarm-hold) could converge on air and drop the hammer. Added `!canSee` to the gate — the doctrine's "aim-without-fire until a probe resolves" is now enforced at the trigger, not just the arm. Opportunity fire survives intact: a mid-leg sighting flips `canSee`, retargets to `vis.centroid`, and authorizes through the same gate. Filed `FIRES_ON_BELIEF_NO_CONTACT` (FIXED).
- **Routed legs: cover-node ↔ corridor** (`BuildRoute`, user: "cover to cover node movement"): the missing layer between hop selection and feet — zones are destination nodes, and a Dijkstra flood over the cover field's own cell grid computes the corridor between them (voxel-solid walkability = the same SamplePoint predicate `MoveWithCollision` enforces, no diagonal corner-cutting, endpoint snap-to-walkable). During a search episode the orbit ring enters the flood as a soft per-cell cost (`routeRingBias`) so the *corridor itself* hugs the arc, not just the destination. The cell chain is string-pulled through voxel-clear corridors into sparse waypoints; `StepRoute` walks them with the existing gait/slide/nudge machinery (fresh nudge budget per segment; a waypoint abort = leg abort → spent zone, re-pick). Applies to every cover seek — orbit hops, park, relocate, entry. `search_leg` became `search_legN`: the HUD draws the real corridor polyline instead of a straight ray.
- **Orbit face-gate sign fix** (playtest: "he gave up" — search exited with zero hops): `CoverField`'s `outDir` is the wall face's outward normal **into the stand area** (`stand = facePos + outDir·depth`), so coherent cover — stand behind the wall, anchor beyond it — reads `Dot(outDir, anchor→stand) ≈ +1`, not −1. The shipped gate required `dot < −0.3`: every genuinely-opposing zone failed it, and backwards-cover zones that would pass it fail `Hidden` anyway → the conjunction was empty → first `TryOrbitStep` exhausted → `ExitCombat`. Gate flipped to `> +0.3`. Also added the exhaustion census: on give-up the 🥊 log now prints per-gate rejection counts (`visited · notHidden · wrongFace · noAdvance · tooClose`), and the ≥90° satisfied exit logs itself (`orbit satisfied: flank n° resolved`) — a quiet abandon is now impossible.
- **Orbit radius is now the HOT PATH, not a fence** (user refinement — "any cover selectable at any depth, the ring shows the ideal path"): the `≥ buffer` rejection is gone — hop scoring hugs the ring instead (`cost = |stand→anchor dist − orbitRadius| + 0.15·travelDist`), so on-ring stands win at any depth near or far. The `search_buf` marker meaning updates: preferred-radius attractor, not the never-closer boundary. The flow-field flood remains the declared upgrade — as an actual *pathing* layer it would make legs route along the ring too, not just score destinations near it.
- **Orbit hops: threat-facing gate + transit/recon cadence** (user refinements): candidates must now satisfy **cover-facing-away** — `Dot(zone.outDir, stand→anchor) < -0.3`, so the hop path is a chain of zones whose protective face opposes the threat, not ground that's accidentally occluded. Hop classes: every `orbitReconEvery`th leg (default 3) is a **RECON** — lands and runs the full peek loop; the rest are **TRANSIT** — land, brief settle, seam re-issues the next step (`transitPending`) without peeking: 2 transit → 1 recon → loop. Panel tags the live leg `·transit`/`·recon`.
- **Lost-contact search collapsed to the ORBIT doctrine** (user-directed: "primitive authored rotations → one rule — reach a ±90° bearing offset to the last-known, hops however far the cover chain leads, never closer than the buffer"): the staged hop1/hop2 machinery is replaced by angle accumulation. `ArmSearch()` (shared by ALL empty-peek verdicts — Engaged drop, zero-contact `Rise`, zero-contact `Swing` dwell — so a never-seen target still triggers it) records the orbit frame: anchor = `AimAnchor` (belief: freshest sighting else the entry report), origin bearing = his bearing around the anchor at the miss, **buffer radius** = the standoff held at the loss, and rolls the CW/CCW side — the only discrete decision. Each Settle-seam `TryOrbitStep()` picks the **cheapest unvisited Hidden zone that advances the arc ≥15°** (fallback pass accepts any forward progress) at ≥ buffer radius — `searchVisited` prevents ping-pong; Euclidean cost is the declared stand-in for the flow-field flood. Legs chain: across the street, around the block, overshoot past 90° legal. An **aborted leg marks its zone spent and re-picks from where he stands** — only arc exhaustion or `progress ≥ 90°` ends the episode (`ExitCombat` → stale awareness). Mid-leg sighting still rolls 50/50 continue-vs-`TryNearestCover` park. Panel: `SEARCH ▸ ORBIT n° ▸ FLANK 90°`. Markers: `search_ref` white = origin bearing, `search_aim` yellow = the 90° goal spoke, `search_buf` amber 16-chord **standoff ring** at buffer radius, `search_pick`/`search_leg`, `search_tgt` post tinted cyan→orange with progress.
- **Swing peek now picks the side that actually BREACHES cover** (user-observed: he side-peeked toward a full wall instead of the corner): `TryStartSwing` evaluated only the rolled/committed side's rim. Now both sides' `TryZoneEdge` rims are scored by the field's own verdict at the stepped destination (`SwingSideScore`: Exposed=2 opens the sightline, out-of-field open=1, Hidden/invalid=0 — stepping there just peeks AT more wall); the strictly-better side wins the commit, ties keep the roll. Log line gains `verdict L#/R#`.
- **New bug filed — `RENDER_FLOATS_ABOVE_GROUNDED_ANCHOR`** (user screenshot): Vinny rendered ~1m airborne in a crouch beside a wall while the `cov_feet` anchor read grounded — pose/render-side lift, not nav. INVESTIGATING; prime suspect is the duck fold reposing voxels without dropping the render root.
- **Search driver widened to EVERY empty peek + search-orbit markers**: the miss that arms the search was only the Engaged drop (`N` empty probes) — a rise or rim-peek that found *nothing* fell back to `TopUp`→re-probe forever, so a target hidden from combat start never triggered the driver. Now `ArmSearch()` is the shared verdict for all three empty-peek exits (Engaged drop, zero-contact `Rise`, zero-contact `Swing` dwell), and the orbit anchor is `AimAnchor()` — **belief** (freshest sighting, else the entry `threatPos` report) — so a never-seen opponent still leaves a point to circle (`LastSeenTarget` is no longer required). **Search-orbit markers** (`DebugHUDManager`, while `vinCc.Searching`) draw the full triangulation off the magenta anchor post — see the orbit entry above for the current set.
- **3-stage lost-contact search — the hop driver ships**: the papered 45°→90° orbit procedure is live end-to-end. An `Engaged` miss arms `pendingSearch` (first miss records `searchOriginBearing` = my bearing around `lastSeenPos`); the **Settle seam** is the branch point — `TrySearchHop` picks a Hidden-verdict zone whose bearing around belief hits the target (hop 1 = current bearing ±45°, hop 2 = origin bearing ±90°, rolled side each), gated by the `searchIntNorm` INT lever (bearing tolerance lerps 60°→15°). `CommitSearchSeek` issues the leg with the same atomic commit shape as scripted relocations; exhaustion or no candidate → `ExitCombat` (stale awareness). Hop legs run the **scout posture**: locally-Hidden ground folds to the local wall's `reqD` (moving along cover), open ground stands `duck→0` + `AimWalk` + the arm presents FORWARD (feet + facing·2.5m — aiming at lastSeen mid-leg would torque the torso toward memory while he walks elsewhere; a resolved probe retargets the muzzle instantly); `dbgSearchLook` (default off) A/B-layers the head sweep over open legs, pre-search setting restored on leg end. Mid-hop sighting = the **opportunity roll**: the acquire edge clears search state and coins 50/50 — continue to the assigned hop cover (opportunity channel owns the shot en route) vs `TryNearestCover` parks at the nearest usable zone and restarts engagement there. Panel shows `SEARCH hopN` while a hop is out. Guarded: `scriptedMode` never arms the driver; `holdInCover` never reaches the miss path.
- **Body-vs-post offset diagnosed to `arriveEps` — tightened 0.18m → 0.06m + three-way destination markers**: with the quality-layer centroids landing squarely, Vinny still rendered visibly off the post. Instrumented instead of guessed: `cov_dst` (thick ORANGE post at `CoverPoint` — the committed destination, drawn whenever combat is non-Idle) + `cov_feet`/`cov_feet2` (green X at `Feet()` — the grid-center nav anchor) + an `off N.m (anchor→cover)` line in the COVER+COMBAT panel (flags `⚠ NOT ON POINT` beyond `arriveEps`). Screenshot verdict: orange coincided with the cyan zone post (destination = centroid ✓), green X sat under his body (anchor = volume center ✓ — the render path was audited clean: `worldOffset = transform.position` per-frame, instances draw at `worldOffset + size·0.5`), and `off` read **exactly 0.18m** — he was stopping at the loosest legal distance. `arriveEps` is now `0.06f` AND arrival is exact: `StepToward` used to return "arrived" the first tick it entered the eps band (could stop mid-band); now entering eps takes the remaining sub-eps `MoveWithCollision(to)` as the final placement — "arrived" means ON the point (collision-checked; a refused last inch still legally arrives at eps). INT picks WHICH point (t=1 = the centroid); placement precision is footwork every actor gets. A genuinely blocked last inch surfaces as a leg abort (`⚠️ … aborted Nm off centroid`) instead of a silent offset. If a residual offset persists, the next suspects in order are the duck fold shifting the rendered mass toward his facing (physically real — `eyeFwdOf` counts it for Hidden) and the zone-post ↔ chosen-cell pair belonging to different step-split zones.
- **Cover centroids moved UP to the quality layer — threat selects, it doesn't draw** (user-directed: "the actor gets the quality and centroid data from the same layer, and threat simply determines which one he selects"): the zone flood ran on the threat-carved `Hidden` set — irregular LOS-carved blobs whose mean centroids landed off-center (elbow posts on corner-wrapped zones). Now zones form on the **quality footprint** (`volIdOf` set, `quality ≥ SeedMin` — the uniform claimed bands), enumerated once per `Build` *before* the threat pass, so zone shape and `ZoneSpot.stand` are threat-independent geometry that can't wobble on belief updates. New per-cell **`outDirOf`** (the owning face's outward normal, written at splat-claim, inherited by gap fills) joins the split key `(volume, class, ¼-duck, face normal)` — a box's front/side bands split at the corner, so every zone is a **rectangle**. The stand itself is now derived **geometrically**, not statistically: per-cell **`wallIdxOf`** (the spec'd Phase-B field) maps each member to its owning wall column, and `stand` = the owner columns' `facePos` extent projected out by the band depth — the face's ground-projected rectangle center — instead of a member-cell mean that raster asymmetry (corner claim-stealing, gap fills, terrace raggedness) could drag off the face. The threat layer gates **legality at query time**: `TryRankInZone`/`TryZoneEdge` rank only `Hidden` members (t=1 = hidden cell nearest the center, t=0 = farthest hidden rim — INT axis unchanged); `TryZoneAt` keeps its band∧Hidden semantics; relocation resolves the zone's **own face** mark (outDir match) for the rise solve. Debug posts draw for EVERY zone — cyan when the stand cell is Hidden under the current threat, dim grey when the physical offer exists but is gated out; with no threat pinned the zones enumerate passively and every band posts. `Vector2.Dot ≥ 0.999` face-match tolerance covers yawed vehicle faces (arbitrary angles merge correctly per face).
- **"Stuck" is no longer "arrived" — legs verify position at completion** (user-observed ~0.3–0.5m off-centroid settles): `StepToward`'s stuck guard returned `true` for both outcomes — reached `dest` vs. stopped moving — so a corner-wrap stall completed legs wherever he wedged, and the `return` leg settled without re-verifying. Now: `MoveWithCollision` slides on the **dominant component first** (fixed X-then-Z kept feeding the wall lip); a stalled leg spends **unstick sidesteps** (±perpendicular 0.28m hops, alternating sides, ≤3, facing follows the nudge) and only an exhausted budget reports done — **callers re-measure distance**. `swing-p2` verified-arrival → `CrouchAndCover` rolls; aborted → `Duck`'s return leg owns the walk home (its arrival rolls). `return` abort logs `⚠️ return leg aborted Nm off centroid` + `LastResult`, stands where it ended (no fake arrival, no infinite grind). `SeekingCover` rides the same `StepToward` (previously inline — no stuck guard, could grind forever): exhausted → `⚠️ cover unreachable` → `HoldPosition` last-stand fallback. `stuckNudges`/side re-armed at every stuck-clock arm (`SetState(SeekingCover)`, `TryStartSwing`, phase boundary, `returnWalked` first step). Follow-up logged in `SWING_RETREAT_SETTLES_AT_RIM`.
- **Settle is a pure breath — the roll lives at arrivals, the turn at moving beats** (user-directed, final boundary): the seam owned NO motion at all — `EnterBeat(Settle)` is just `FaceHold(settleYawDeg,"settle")`, no roll, no new demand. The facing roll moved into `CrouchAndCover` — the function every PHYSICAL ARRIVAL calls (seek landing, `swing-p2` step-back, the off-spot `return` leg; `settleDirty` deleted — the call IS the event). Each arrival's TURN is owned by the beat that moved him: the seek's last move is `FaceHold(settleYawDeg,"arrive")` — arrival is position ∧ facing, `InCover` doesn't begin until he converges (`coverArrived` flag, reset on `SetState(SeekingCover)`); the step-back's turn rides `Duck`'s `FaceCurve(foldFrom→settleYawDeg, FoldT)` — fold-and-turn one motion; the off-spot `return` leg ends in `FaceHold(settleYawDeg,"return")` posture turn (`returnTurning` — runs above the fold/floor gate so a residual dwell can't leave a stale `Track(CoverPoint)` singing while he stands on the point) converging before `Settle` starts. On a return to the same spot the roll **excludes the side he just used** — the other two directions, equally likely; fresh spots roll all three; `settleFace` explicit settings (Back/Left/Right) still override. Since `PeekSide` IS the re-rolled commitment, variation propagates: the next peek steps out whichever side he came back facing.
- **Settles-in-the-open fixed — stale step-guard clock + missing return leg**: after a correct peek he "stepped back" but folded/settled at the zone rim. Two bugs: (1) `StepToward`'s stuck guard (`swingStuckPos`/`T`) stayed armed from step-out — the dwell consumed its 0.6s no-progress budget, so phase-2's first tick read `stuck → arrived` instantly at the rim; the clock now resets at each phase boundary. (2) `Duck`'s completion only tested `folded ∧ floor`, never position — now when the fold lands off-spot (`>arriveEps` from `CoverPoint`), a `return` leg duck-walks him back to the cover centroid before the seam holds (covers both the zero-contact step-back AND contact→Engaged-at-the-rim exits). New bug file `SWING_RETREAT_SETTLES_AT_RIM`; Swing doc section updated.
- **Gotcha #12 — the zombie slerp claim**: `VOXEL_ENGINE_GOTCHAS` gained the entry — one-shot helpers arming perpetual channels (`DebugSnapTo` → `hasTargetRotation` forever), presence-flag watchdogs vs residual detection, and "a snap is a snap."
- **ROOT CAUSE CAUGHT: `EventPlayer`'s zombie rotation claim was fighting the facing driver** — the FORCES monitor showed `settle` pinned at −439°/s sustained (a `Hold` writing ~7°/frame toward a constant can only mean something pushes back +7°/frame every frame). `EventPlayer.DebugSnapTo` armed `hasTargetRotation` on every pit snap; the scripted-facing slerp channel then ran **every frame forever while `running`** (it only cleared on a zero-direction move event), pulling him ~8%/frame toward the snap's facing while the driver held `settleYawDeg` — ~180° apart = the standing jitter. The old binary watchdog was structurally blind to it (it only flagged frames with NO combat write — the driver wrote every frame). **Fixes**: `DebugSnapTo` writes rotation instantly (a snap holds no claim); EventPlayer's slerp channel now **voids `hasTargetRotation` whenever the character's `CombatController` is non-Idle** — combat owns facing, scripted/snap claims die on entry. **Watchdog upgraded to residual detection**: `EXTERNAL Δ` = observed change − Σ combat writes, so external writers can't hide inside a written frame.
- **FORCES tab — live yaw force monitor**: new HUD tab (combat row) drawing one L◀|0.0|▶R bar per yaw writer — EMA-smoothed °/s actually written to the transform, signed (blue = clockwise/R, orange = counterclockwise/L, red = `EXTERNAL` watchdog-caught unattributed writes). `SetYaw` accumulates signed Δdeg per owner per frame; `LateUpdate` folds watchdog-externals + EMA-decays silent writers to zero. Header shows driver state live (`mode · owner · demand ° · yaw · beat · state`), the ▶ marker tags the owner's row the driver currently serves, writers sort by |force| so the fight floats to the top, and the three facing toggles sit on the tab for flip-and-watch. Locked-view mirror included (`LockForces`). The decisive instrument for the driver-on jitter: two rows lit = tug-of-war; only EXTERNAL lit = unattributed writer; a single owner firing = the driver's own demand chasing a moving target (then read the demand °).
- **Facing rewritten as a single driver — decision vs. mechanism separated** (user-directed): the fragmented yaw architecture (a servo lane + beat-owned writes + movement writes, each gated separately — a bisect toggle literally could not cover all write sites; a `[Yaw] ← duck` log fired with every switch off) is replaced by declared modes rendered by ONE function. `FacingMode { Free, Hold, Track, Curve }`; declaration APIs `FaceFree/FaceHold/FaceTrack/FaceCurve`; `TickFacing()` — the LAST call in `Update`, now the only `transform.rotation` writer in the controller — renders the declared mode: `Hold`/`Track` ease at `turnSpeedDeg` (deadbanded |Δ|≤0.5° ⇒ silent), `Curve` projects yaw onto a live t-source. Declarations: `Settle`/`TopUp`/`None` → `Hold(settleYawDeg)` (roll once per spot stays pure decision data — it never touches the transform), `Rise`/`Duck` → `Curve` (coupled to the fold via `RiseT`/`FoldT` reading `anim.duck` live), `Engaged` → `Track(contactSeen ? ThreatFeet : AimAnchor)`, `Swing` steps → `Track(dest)` via `StepToward`, dwell → `Track(AimAnchor)`, `SeekingCover` → `Track(CoverPoint)`, no-cover `HoldPosition` → `Track(AimAnchor)`, `EnterCombat`/`ExitCombat` → `Free`. `EnterBeat` calls `FaceFree()` first — intent never goes stale. Toggles: `dbgFacing` gates THE DRIVER (off = provably zero combat yaw), `dbgMoveYaw` demotes movement declarations to `Free` (strafe-slide test), `dbgEngagedTrack` demotes the Engaged declaration to `Hold(engageYawDeg)`. `YawIntentDeg`/`dbgYawIntent`/`swingStepYawDeg` deleted. Docs synced (ENGAGEMENT_BEATS facing section rewritten, bug-file bisect/kill-switch tables updated).
- **Debug channel kill-switches — bisect the jitter live**: five toggles on `CombatController`, exposed in-game under the COMBAT tab's `channel kill-switches (jitter bisect)` fold (also Inspector-visible): `dbgYawIntent` (whole per-beat facing lane), `dbgEngagedTrack` (the `Engaged` live-track on `AimAnchor` = last-known-pos targeting — the user's prime suspect), `dbgArmSolver` (arm permanently stowed in combat), `dbgMoveYaw` (seek/swing-step yaw — strafe-slide test), `dbgVisionFeed` (swing-dwell cone feed), plus `yawWatch` (logging mute). Solver-side publics (`torsoAssist`, `aimFeelEnabled`) already existed — the bisect matrix is in `ORPHANED_IK_ARM_FIGHTS_SETTLE_FACING`.
- **Belief is event-driven + intent lane deadbanded — the jitter fix**: kill-switch bisect (`dbgYawIntent` off = silent) pinned the wiggle to the `Engaged` live-track easing toward `AimAnchor`, which was rewritten from `vis.centroid` EVERY probe (0.12s) — the centroid wanders as the visible silhouette changes, so the ease chased a moving target (and `torsoAssist` chased the same noisy point). Now `ThreatPos`/`LastSeenTarget` only move on perception EDGES — acquire (miss→hit) snaps to `vis.centroid`, loss (hit→miss) freezes at `lastConfirmed`; mid-contact belief never drifts. `Engaged` tracks the live body transform (`ThreatFeet()` — smooth) not the centroid; blind-engaged uses the frozen belief. The whole `YawIntentDeg` lane is deadbanded (|Δ|>0.5° gate) — converged Settle/TopUp/Duck write ZERO yaw corrections; yaw writes are reserved for beats that own them (Rise/Engaged/Swing). Bonus: `Sensor.Threat` zone re-paints no longer fire per-probe.
- **Rise/Duck transitions now couple turn to fold — one scalar, one motion**: the unfold (`anim.duck`, 3/s) and the turn (`turnSpeedDeg`, 540°/s) were independent eases landing at different times — the "forces fighting" feel in the crouch→stand-turn. Now `EnterBeat(Rise|Duck)` captures `(duck, yaw)` endpoints and each beat projects yaw onto the DUCK's normalized progress: `LerpAngle(from, to, SmoothStep(duckProgress))` — turn-and-stand / fold-and-turn are single motions. `YawIntentDeg` returns null for both (beat-owned, exempt from the servo lane). The deeper path if this still doesn't read authored: a pose-space root-yaw channel committed to the transform at t=1 (root-motion extraction) — noted in ENGAGEMENT_BEATS.
- **Facing intent is per-beat; navigation tug-of-war fixed**: three facing-ownership leaks. (1) `EnterBeat(Settle)` re-rolled `PeekSide`/`settleYawDeg` EVERY seam — the loop swung him ±90°/180° in place per cycle; the roll is now guarded by `settleSpot == CoverPoint` — committed once per cover spot, re-rolls only on relocation. (2) `Duck`/`TopUp`/`None` had NO yaw writer — the post-Engaged fold left him threat-forward until the next seam's re-roll swung him back ("face forward then back again"); new `YawIntentDeg()` maps every beat to its held heading through one `SetYaw` lane — settle-family holds `settleYawDeg`, `Rise` holds `engageYawDeg`, `Engaged` tracks `AimAnchor` live (sightings refresh belief, the body follows what it believes), `Swing` returns null (phases own yaw). (3) `ArmAimSolver.torsoAssist` now skips while the rig locomotes (`EffectiveState` Walking/AimWalk) — root owns heading on the move; the seek's `ikOverrides[0]` toward the target vs `seek` root yaw was the navigation-time tug-of-war. Arm still solves residual on the move (opportunity-fire untouched).
- **Settle wiggle — orphaned IK arm disarmed; yaw instrumentation added**: the settle-time shudder was a joint-space tug-of-war, not root yaw — the disarm gate required `ExposureArmed` (only true when combat itself armed the solver), so any external arm (possess-entry `Arm()`, VISION-tab ARM test, pre-combat carryover) orphaned forever inside combat: `solver.Update` kept solving toward a stale point, `torsoAssist`/`ikOverrides[0]` pulled the torso toward it under `PerturbTarget` sway while the settle ease pulled the root toward `settleYawDeg`. Fix: the arm is beat-owned outright — `if (!wantAim && !holding && solver.IsAiming) Disarm()` every tick; `ExposureArmed` demoted to display flag. Diagnostics: every combat rotation write routed through `SetYaw(who)` (`[Yaw]` owner logs + 0.5s heartbeat) and a `LateUpdate` watchdog (`[Yaw?] EXTERNAL` on root-yaw moves with no combat writer — combat states only; `yawWatch` Inspector toggle). Tracked: `ORPHANED_IK_ARM_FIGHTS_SETTLE_FACING`.
- **WASD double-drive fixed — possess yields to free-cam**: F6 free-look while possessed had both consumers live — `CityMap3D`'s fly was suppressed on `PossessController.Active` while possess still ate WASD (Vinny walked mid-test, camera stayed put). `CityMap3D.CamFree` is public now and `PossessController`'s yield gate widened from `Time.timeScale<0` (F8 only) to `cityMap.CamFree` (F6 freedom OR freeze): possess stays latched (G still shows control) but hands WASD/mouse/fire to the fly and keeps look angles synced for a snap-free resume.
- **Uniform beat tempo — `beatDwell[]` table + HUD TEMPO editor**: `settleSec`/`swingPeekSec` replaced by `public float[] beatDwell` indexed by `EngagementBeat` (all 5s default) — `EnterBeat` arms `beatUntil` universally so EVERY beat holds its dwell floor; exits become `event ∧ timer` (TopUp leaves on `!reloading ∧ floor` — reload still starts instantly, Rise on `converged ∧ floor`, Engaged's exits queue behind the floor while `buriedSince` accumulates, Duck on `folded ∧ floor`, Swing's corner dwell rides `beatDwell[Swing]`). The SCRIPT slot UI (dropdowns/clear-all/cursor) converted to `TEMPO — beat lengths`: a row per beat with `▶ · Ns left` live countdown + `−`/`+` ±0.5s adjusters; `refresh cover spots`/`show` posts + `scr_tgt` beam kept; the manual `scripted` toggle removed (dead-trap without slot-fill UI — `scriptedMode` stays for the programmatic anchor path). `BeatLeft` now reports any active beat's remaining dwell.
- **ThreatPos collapsed to the belief channel + last-seen debug markers**: the per-frame `ThreatPos = ThreatFeet()` live feed is gone — `ThreatPos` is now written at combat entry and on positive sightings only (`vis.centroid` probe write, plus the swing-dwell cone reacquire write). `Sensor.Threat` (cover zones) and `AimAnchor` (settle/engage facing, rise solve) both ride belief, so "seek cover away from the threat" and "engage the threat" target the same remembered place; live feet are reserved for physical verdicts (silhouette probe, vision cone). `AimAnchor` dropped its live-threat middle fallback. New marker pair in `DebugHUDManager` (per-frame): ORANGE `pick_aim` beams track Tan's actual feet (truth) and MAGENTA `last_seen` post + cross + `last_seen_dir` beam (Vinny→belief) show the slerp anchor — they overlap while he's visible and diverge once he hides. HUD's `visionSensor.SetTarget(pickedAim)` is now gated on not-in-combat (controller owns the cone target during combat — same ownership split as `Sensor.Threat`).
- **`Swing` beat — the lateral corner peek is LIVE**: `EngagementBeat.Swing` (=6) enters from the Rise-refusal path (`SolveRiseDuck < 0`, unscripted — the wall can't be fought OVER, so look AROUND it). `CoverField.TryZoneEdge(cellIdx, dirXZ)` returns the zone member farthest along the peek side's lateral axis; `TryStartSwing` resolves side from `PeekSide` (committed side; back-facing rolls at peek time) and sets `swingDest = rim + stepDir·swingMargin` (0.4m — step-out adapts to painted cover width, no fixed distance). Three phases in `TickBeat`: **step out** (`StepToward` mini-steering — MoveWithCollision + yaw at the lateral exit + stuck-guard ~0.6s; cover posture kept) → **dwell** at the corner (`swingPeekSec` 1.6s ≈ one authored lookScan cycle; `AnimState.Looking` sweeps the posed cone while the body turns onto `AimAnchor`; he is physically EXPOSED out there — verdict = `VisionSensor.AnyVisible` on the live-fed threat point OR the aimed `TryGetVisibleVoxels` probe) → **step back** to `CoverPoint` → `CrouchAndCover` → `Duck` → seam (TopUp). Contact short-circuits to `Engaged`. No zone edge → falls through to relocate→duck. Beat lane lights `swing`; `swingMargin`/`swingPeekSec`/`swingDuck` Inspector-tunable. The 45°/90° hop driver that chains peeks into the 3-stage search remains the pending piece. **Playtest fix**: the stationary pose lane was stomping `Looking` back to `Crouching` every frame and holding `targetDuck` (deepest crouch, no aim, static pose) — Swing is now exempt from that lane and owns its poses (Walking steps / Looking dwell); dwell unfolds to `swingDuck` and arms the IK at `AimAnchor` (belief) via `swingAim` — aim-without-fire since the fire gate now carries `BeatAllowsFire` explicitly; `swingStepYawDeg` faces the actual destination, not the abstract side axis (fixes backwards-looking steps on off-axis edges).
- **Cover settle posture faces away — hiding isn't watching**: `CrouchAndCover`'s arrival threat-snap removed; facing is now beat-owned. `EnterBeat(Settle)` rolls `PeekSide` from `settleFace` (Random → −1 left / 0 back / +1 right) and the seam eases yaw to `settleYawDeg` (threat-bearing +180 or ±90); `Rise` eases yaw to `engageYawDeg` (bearing to `AimAnchor` = lastSeen→live-threat→ThreatPos) while unfolding — turn-to-engage rides the rise, probe still waits full convergence. `SolveRiseDuck`/`SolveRiseAtSpot` evaluate at `engageYawDeg` (not the settle-facing yaw — the eye re-anchor was already yaw-parametric). `HoldPosition` keeps the explicit threat-face (exposed last stand, no wall to turn on). Side effects: the vision cone points away during seams (perception honestly lives in the peek), and `PeekSide` is the future `Swing` beat's step-out commitment.
- **INT axis — cover positioning quality is now a per-rig tunable** (spec'd in COMBAT_COVER_STATE → "Cover positioning quality — the INT axis", mapped to `Hoods.xtx` Intelligence 0-255): the centroid fix and the old wall-midpoint bug turned out to be endpoints of one gradient — HOW deep inside a zone a mobster stands is skill expression. `CoverField` keeps per-zone member cells (`zoneMembers`) from the flood; `TryRankInZone(cellIdx, t)` sorts members by dist² to centroid and rank-picks `idx = round((1−t)·(n−1))` — `t=1` = centroid nest, `t=0` = Hidden rim with zero margin ("sticking out"), linear between. Both `TryPickCover`/`TryPickCoverNear` take `intNorm`; `CombatController.coverIntNorm` ([Range 0-1], default 1) is the per-rig Inspector tunable — Vinny vs Tan can run opposite ends today. Deferred extremes/axes: sub-floor INT stepping past the rim into Exposed cells, INT-modulated search-hop precision / seam / fire discipline.
- **FIXED: auto cover picks land on the zone centroid now (markers = destinations)**: `TryPickCover`/`TryPickCoverNear` resolved the picked Hidden cell, then OVERWROTE the destination with the owning wall column's `facePos + outDir·standoff` midpoint — while `EnumerateZones` markers paint the zone's member-cell-nearest-centroid. Actors stopped beside their own marker. `CoverField` now stamps `zoneOf[cell]` during the zone flood (new `ZoneSpot.cell` = centroid member-cell index) + `TryZoneOfCell` + `MarkChosen`: both pick paths map picked cell → owning zone → `point = zone.stand`, `reqDepth`/`cls` from the zone's uniform split key (more honest than nearest-column), and `ChosenCell` repoints to the centroid cell so the chosen post marks where he'll actually stand. Wall-column resolution stays as `PickInfo` diagnostics + the no-zone fallback. Scripted slots already landed on `z.stand` — auto/anchor/relocate/scripted picks now share one contract.
- **Pit opponent mode → three-way (dummy / hide / fight) — Tan can now hide for search testing**: `pitOpponentAI` bool replaced by `pitTanMode` enum; the pit panel + weapons-tab duel rows both read dummy|hide|fight. New `CombatController.holdInCover` flag (set by the harness before `EnterCombat`, never cleared by it): Tan runs the IDENTICAL entry — his `CoverSensor` arms on Vinny's feet, `TryPickCover` rides his own painted field, `SeekingCover` walks him there — then the `Settle`-exit gate (the single choke point every beat route passes through) re-enters the seam forever: no `TopUp`/`Rise`/`Engaged`, arm stowed at `targetDuck`. `BeatAllowsFire` gated on `!holdInCover` — no opportunity fire during the approach or a no-cover HoldPosition fallback. Net: Vinny hunts, Tan folds behind his own picked cover and waits — Vinny's `emptyProbesToDrop` → Duck → settle is precisely the stage-1 lost-contact trigger the papered 3-stage search consumes. Button labels mirror the mode (`START DUEL — Vinny hunts, Tan hides`); status lines show `duel {mode}`.
- **Lost-contact search procedure PAPERED** (`PERCEPTION_AWARENESS.md` → Search & resolution, `ENGAGEMENT_BEATS.md` lookScan hook): the spec's "user-papered; do not invent" 3-step search is now authored. Stage 1 = miss in position (shipped `emptyProbesToDrop → Duck → Settle` loop, `lastSeenPos` fresh); stage 2 = HOP 1 at ~45° bearing around `lastSeenPos` (random CW/CCW — stat-influenced later), seek → settle → peek; stage 3 = HOP 2 at ±90° **from the original bearing** (12→3/9, not cumulative), final peek → `ExitCombat` → duties + stale awareness entry. Peek = two verdict-gated shapes: `Rise` (vertical, existing) + `Swing` (new lateral beat — step out past the zone centroid, body yaw faces the cover edge swung around; gun aims `lastSeenPos` via the arm solver). **Belief vs truth contract**: actions aim at `lastSeenPos` memory — never `ThreatCharacter`'s live pos; reacquisition = aimed probe at lastSeen OR `VisionSensor` cone detection wherever he actually is (catches mid-relocation flankers). Peek dwell runs the Looking-state sweep so the posed cone physically scans the region (`riseTime` = honest detection accumulation).
- **Render scale moved off [R] → Render-tab dropdown — R is reload-only now**: the `RenderChunks` R-key cycle (0.5/0.65/0.75/1.0) is deleted — it double-bound against possess-mode reload (`PossessController` rKey → `StartReload`). `VoxelChunkManager.SetResolutionScale(float)` is the new public target (same body: `manualResolutionOverride` + `EnsureProxyRT` + `propScreenSize` push + log) with `ResolutionScales` exposing the option list; the HUD's `DrawShadowControls` (RENDER tab's live-controls row) gains a `render scale` dropdown using the script-slot expand-inline idiom — button shows the current scale, options set it directly. Stale text fixed: render-tab line `([R] cycles…)` → `(dropdown below)`, KEYS tab's `[R] Cycle render scale` line removed + `[R] Reload` added to the possess line, footer hotkeys drop `[R] Render scale`. NOTE: `FollowCamera` still holds `rKey` for camera height (Q/E distance · R/F height, both modes) — a remaining overlap with reload worth revisiting.
- **NEW LOT: `cover_arena` — the symmetric duel arena in front of the tenement's door** (`Tools/gen_cover_arena.py` → `cover_arena.json` → `cover_arena.stasset` v2). Placed at `block_36` (r3 c5) — directly south of `block_26`'s real `tenement_block_0` (storefront + awning door on its low-Z face, centered x96 — the block axis IS the door axis; `tenement_glass` at block_47 turned out to be an all-glass shell, no door face). Tan at `stand_b` (high-z edge) backs the facade across ~10m of street — misses north-bound chew the tenement front. Double-mirror symmetry (x=96 AND z=96) = a rotationally fair duel — every quadrant offers both fighters the same cover: (a) the **valley wall** at the mid lane — the pit's stepped gradient mirrored: h16 Full ends stepping down h15→h14→h12(crouch)→h9(Low) to an h7 dip dead-center on the door axis (open sightline, every class preserved); (b) 4 **stone flank stubs** per quadrant stepped h14→h10; (c) 4 **red-brick pillars h32 (1.6m)** — eye can't clear standing = true hide-only cover (exercises refuse→relocate — "cover for hiding, not fighting"); (d) 4 **h12 planters** at quadrant centers. Same stand plates + pick-anchor names (`cover_stand_a/_b`, `cover_wall/crouch/mid/deep` + `_n` twins + `firing_*` aliases) — SETUP PIT/START DUEL/pick rows/scripted director all work untouched. `block_45` (old pit) + `block_56` (first placement attempt) reverted to empty_land — one pit in the layout, since `PitBlockId` scans for the first `cover_stand_a` carrier; `cover_pit.stasset` stays on disk, restoring is a one-line layout edit.
- **Firing posture = FULL STAND, and the recon timers are dead** — per user: "single fully executed atomic procedures with only settle seams in between", and "all firing states the same, fully upright — clearly differentiate in-cover vs breaking cover." Two linked changes: (a) `SolveRiseAtSpot` is now a single full-stand verdict (`TryGetEyeAnchor` at duck 0 → eye clears `wallTop+riseMargin`? → bore ray free?) — the multi-duck candidate search is suspended; `riseDuck` resolves 0 or fails. (b) `Rise` is atomic: the beat ends at pose convergence (`|anim.duck − riseDuck| < .02`, the Duck beat's own epsilon) where a FRESH `TryGetVisibleVoxels` probe delivers the verdict — contact → `Engaged`, occluded → `Duck` → seam. `riseHoldSec`/`riseUntil` removed entirely — the old 1.2s clock started at beat entry while the head was still below the lip, so "rose clean — zero contact" could fire before the eye was physically up (the bug the user hit). `emptyProbeSec` → `emptyProbesToDrop` (4 consecutive empty probes ≈ same 0.5s, counted per probe tick — event-shaped). `BeatLeft` no longer shows a Rise countdown. `boreBurySec`/`disarmHoldSec` kept — debounce, not verdicts.
- **Facing-failed cover cells now paint Exposed instead of vanishing** (CoverField phase 2): a seeded cell whose owning face opens toward the threat (`dot(outDir, dirToThreat) ≥ facingDot`) sets `state = Exposed` rather than staying `Untested` — the threat view now colors the SAME footprint as the quality pass (red = can't hide from this direction) instead of bands silently truncating at the facing boundary.
- **Threat feed is now LIVE during combat — `ThreatPos` tracks `ThreatCharacter`'s feet each frame** and feeds `Sensor.Threat`, so zones re-paint at the sensor's scan cadence as the threat moves (was: a stale entry-time snapshot — a walking threat invalidated painted cover silently). Ownership split: pickedAim owns the channel OUT of combat, the controller owns it IN combat — the sync stands down while `State != Idle` so the two never thrash (which would force-refresh the whole field every frame).
- **FIXED: threat channel was gated on the COVER tab — hierarchy now sequential (threat → zones → combat)**: user caught the inversion — `pickedAim → coverSensor.Threat` sync ran every frame but only when the `coverSensor` field was assigned, which only happened inside `DrawCoverTab`. On the COMBAT tab the channel never reached Vinny's sensor → SETUP PIT staged Tan but `0/0 hidden` painted. Fixed two ways: (a) the sync resolves Vinny's sensor fresh each frame (`SpawnedCharacter.GetComponent`, same pattern as the mode ticker) — threat flows regardless of open tab or combat state, and `ExitCombat`'s clear self-heals next frame (threat persists past disengage — awareness isn't combat-owned); (b) reverted the side-lane where `refresh cover spots` armed the threat itself — it now only re-scans + enumerates; an empty list honestly says `no threat armed — SETUP PIT or PICK THREAT paints zones first`.
- **Scripted engagement director — write the cover sequence live, slot by slot**: user's study tool built — a `SCRIPT — engagement director` fold in TEST LOTS with 8 dropdown slots, each picking a cover POSITION by its zone centroid (`refresh cover spots` enumerates every painted zone via `EnumerateZones` — one stand point per gradient section, nearest-first; the refresh arms the sensor's threat first — Tan's feet in the pit — because Hidden zones are threat-relative and don't exist before combat; `show` paints a `cov_{i}` post at each centroid — dim cyan free, slot-colored when assigned). With `scripted — combat waits for slot orders` ON, `START DUEL` enters combat but **he holds at spawn until slot 1 fills** — the user literally writes the sequence on the fly. Each slot commits through `ScriptedGoTo` — identical atomicity to a relocation (cached stand point, beat reset, `SETTLE→TOPUP→SETTLE→RISE→ENGAGED` replay on arrival; slot 1 shows SEEK, later slots RELOCATE). **Advance rule**: filling the next slot is the order — fires when the current stage reaches a verdict (`ScriptStageDone`: first Engaged contact or any Duck entry) — never mid-move. Auto-relocation suppressed in scripted mode (script owns position). Slots persist across iterations — same script re-runs per duel. `cursor` readout + `scr_tgt` feet→target beam while relocating. Compile clean via the project's Bee rsp.
- **Cover positions are ZONES — a stand point is the connected component's centroid**: the first centroid cut (per-face anchor + radius) produced ~7 spots on a 4-step terrace, biased off-band — user saw the posts drift "toward the back" and one per face, not one per gradient section. Rebuilt as `CoverField.EnumerateZones()`: flood-fills Hidden cells into connected components keyed on `(volIdOf, clsOf, reqDepthOf ÷ ¼-duck)` — a stepped terrace yields one zone per step, a corner overlap splits per volume, and each `ZoneSpot.stand` is the member CELL nearest the raw centroid (visually identical — cells are centimetres — but guaranteed inside the paint even for concave zones). Both consumers iterate zones now: `TryRelocateToFightingCover` (zone borrows its owner's nearest face mark only for the wall top, re-validates `TryZoneAt` at the stand, solves `SolveRiseAtSpot` there) and the script-director's `refresh cover spots`. The committed `CoverPoint` is a cached Vector3 — nothing rewrites it mid-seek — so the relocate stays atomic; the earlier "cancels and re-settles" symptom traced to a face-anchor target landing within `arriveEps` of his feet. `LastRelocateDiag` prints `stand@(x,z)` — the centroid he walks to.
- **RELOCATE is a named phase — the initial-seek primitive, reused**: user model ratified: relocation isn't a bespoke branch, it's the same `SeekingCover` the duel starts with — duck-gait move to the new stand spot, armed opportunity channel during the move (he relocates *reloaded* — TopUp runs before any rise attempt), arrival drops into `SETTLE→TOPUP→SETTLE→RISE` identically. New `Relocating` flag (set on re-pick, cleared at arrival/exit): the SEEK lane relabels **RELOCATE** live; `LastRelocateDiag` line carries the why (`→ <volId> face@(x,z) · duck <d>`). Hygiene: `buriedSince`/`loopGate`/`anim.coverContext` reset on re-pick — a buried-verdict relocate can't trip instantly next Engaged, and the pose returns to fresh-seek state.
- **The real muzzle ray is now the bore detector — buried-bore verdict + relocate diagnostics**: user caught the remaining divergence — the rise solve's `eye−sightGap` MODEL can clear while the actual welded muzzle (`mount.MuzzleWorld`, the red debug ray) still clips the lip; his screenshot showed him `Engaged` and "firing" with the barrel into the wall. Three changes: (a) **fire gate** adds `!BoreBuried(aimPoint)` — the real `MuzzleWorld→aimPoint` segment must be free or the shot never spends (no more rounds into brick); (b) **buried-bore verdict** inside `Engaged` — converged (`Blend≥0.7`) + bore blocked for `boreBurySec` (0.4s) = unfightable wall → `bore buried at the wall — real muzzle ray blocked` → same relocation path as a refused rise (arm itself never gates on it — no flap, the verdict changes cover not pose); (c) **`LastRelocateDiag`** — `→ <volId> face@(x,z) · duck <d>` on success, `refused — n face(s) failed` on exhaustion — surfaced as a `relocate` line under `rise` on the F5 panel so the action + reason are legible.
- **Relocation branch — a refused rise now re-picks FIGHTING cover**: user observed the picked wall was too tall to shoot over while a lower section beside it would clear. `SolveRiseDuck` split into `SolveRiseAtSpot(stand, yaw, wallTop, duck, tgt)` — the solve now works at arbitrary stands (the posed eye re-anchors: local offset yaw-unrotated → carried to the candidate spot). `TryRelocateToFightingCover()` on a refused rise: marks the failed FACE by position (one volume carries faces at different heights — the lower parapet stays eligible), scans `sensor.walls` for the nearest face that is BOTH hidden-validated (`TryZoneAt` — same truth the zone paints) AND rise-solvable, then re-targets `CoverPoint`/`targetDuck` (lower wall = deeper `reqDuck` → deeper approach duck-gait) and re-enters `SeekingCover` — the full `SETTLE→TOPUP→SETTLE→RISE` rhythm replays at the new spot. `failedFaces` clears on combat entry; no fightable face → seam gate as before. Corner pop-out (no face anywhere clears) documented as the remaining deferred branch.
- **Flush standoff + lip-riding muzzle (cover gap was the real bore blocker)**: user saw Vinny park ~0.58m off the wall with the bore ray clipping the lip. Two fixes: (a) `CoverSensor.standoff` 0.55 → **0.3m** — the stand spot now puts him flush behind the face (≈half body depth); HUD `cover standoff` slider (pre-fight). Descending rays clip the lip from far back even when the sight-line muzzle clears — standoff was half the "can't shoot" problem. (b) Bore solve: the `muzzleFwd` push could land a marginal-clearance muzzle inside the wall footprint — muzzle Y now clamps to `≥ wallTop + riseMargin` (the gun rides the parapet lip at minimum). **Pop-out firing specced as the next phase**: `can't see over`/`sees but can't shoot` diags = the side-exit trigger (step around the wall's end instead of over); until built an unsolvable rise loops under `loopBeats` — that wasted cycle marks the missing branch.
- **`loopBeats` is now a LIVE seam gate — toggling mid-fight actually breaks/restarts the cycle**: user found the toggle inert — it was only read once at each failure exit's `afterSettle` write, and the **dry exit** (`Engaged→Duck→Settle→TopUp`) never consulted it at all, so the cycle ran forever regardless. New model: `Duck` entry arms `loopGate`; the settle after any duck is the re-cycle gate and decides park-vs-restart **when it drains** (mid-seam toggles take effect; all four duck exits write `TopUp` unconditionally now). Loop OFF = run the rhythm once and park — even mid-cycle. `awaitingLoop` marks a parked gate: live toggle-ON restarts `Settle→TopUp→…` from the `None` beat case. `NextAfterSettle` previews the gated verdict (`next:` shows `None` while parked).
- **Weapon weld rides the pose transition — the rise gun-snap fixed**: user saw the Model 10 teleport to the aim slot on rise while the hand caught up. The `WeaponMount` weld called `PosePoint`/`ComputeGroupRotation` with `transBlend` defaulted to 1 — evaluating the *destination* pose instantly while the renderer dual-evals and lerps over the transition window. The weld now passes the handle's `transStateA/transTimeA/transBlend/transDip/transDepthA` into `PosePoint` (the overload `VoxelCharacter` queries already use) and dual-evals `ComputeGroupRotation` for both states + `Slerp(qA,qB,blend)` — same convention as `PoseToWorld`. Grip position AND gun orientation now ease with the body.
- **The seam is pure — `TopUp` owns the wheel inside the rhythm**: user saw cylinder dots seating during `SETTLE` — the dry auto-reload fired the instant the 6th round left inside `Engaged` and seated through `Duck→Settle`. Two gates: (a) `cylinder<=0 → StartReload` now requires `State != InCover` (Seek/Hold keep opportunistic reloads; inside cover only the beat starts one); (b) the seat tick holds a carryover reload mid-motion during any non-`TopUp` beat — `nextRoundLoad` defers by the pause so `ReloadLeft`/`NextRoundIn` stay honest, manual possess reloads bypass (`!PossessController.Active`). Sequence now: dry → `DUCK` → `SETTLE` drains pure (cyl `0/6`, dots frozen) → `TOPUP` seats all 6 → `SETTLE` → `RISE`.
- **`loopBeats` toggle — failed runs re-cycle the rhythm instead of parking**: the three failure exits (rise refused / rise-empty / sustained-zero Engaged) used to hard-park `afterSettle=None` — the duel went silent after one attempt. Now `loopBeats` (default ON, HUD checkbox `loop beats (re-rise after fail)` in combat mode) routes them to `afterSettle=TopUp` — `Duck → Settle → TopUp → Settle → Rise` cycles until contact or EXIT. The dry-exit already self-looped; this closes the remaining dead ends. `next:` on the settle bar now shows `TOPUP` when the seam re-arms instead of `None`.
- **Muzzle gate → sight-line bore model (why "sees but can't shoot" fired)**: two-step diagnosis. First diag `wall 0.70+0.02 vs eye≤0.76 muzzle≤0.68` — eye cleared 4cm, rest-pose hand pivot failed 4cm → switched to a hand+lift+forward-reach ray. New verdict `sees but can't shoot` exposed the remaining flaw: pushing the muzzle *toward the target* keeps the bore descending — no forward reach fixes a descending ray under a 0.70 top. Final model — **aimed fire aligns the bore to the eye**: the sights sit `sightGap` (0.045m) above the bore, so the presented muzzle = `eye − sightGap`, pushed `muzzleFwd` *horizontally* toward the target (the barrel over the parapet). For the failing wall: muzzle ≈0.715 > 0.70+0.02 → clears at full stand. `TryGetJointWorld`/hand-pivot eval dropped from the solve — one anchor (the eye), same `LosBlocked` world-raycast as the probe. `muzzleVertOffset` renamed `sightGap`.
- **`LastRiseDiag` — rise attempts now explain themselves**: user saw `settle → next: None` with no visible pop-up — the rise had already exited silently. `SolveRiseDuck` now stamps a verdict string on every attempt (`no wall mark on <vol>`, `eye eval failed`, `unsolvable: wall X vs eye≤Y muzzle≤Z`, `cleared at duck D`, `rose clean — zero contact`, `contact → Engaged`), surfaced as a `rise <diag>` line on the F5 panel + `🥊` console log on refusal/expiry. Semantics note: a refused rise means "standing over this parapet is pointless" (eye AND muzzle must both clear — spec), never "can't stand".
- **Settle seam hardened + countdown section + arm-hold anti-flicker**: user saw beat/fire-lane nodes "crash into each other". Fixes: (a) `settleSec` default 0.5→**5s debug** so each seam is watchable; (b) new `── settle ──` panel section: `SETTLE ▓▓▓▓░░ Ns / Ss → next: <beat>` draining bar, surfaces `afterSettle` (the seam's branch point — new `NextAfterSettle` prop); (c) **disarm hold** (`disarmHoldSec` 0.35): stow now requires a *sustained* miss since `LastSeenAt` — a lone dropped probe no longer pump-stows/re-arms every `visInterval`; authority loss (reload start, no weapon, beat channel closed) still stows instantly. `EXPOSED` node smoothed through the same window. (d) Also answers: TOPUP vs RELOAD — TopUp is the BEAT (intent: restore the wheel); Reload is the physical per-round process. TopUp is idempotent — a reload already in flight from dry opportunity shots is just awaited, and a full wheel passes through (the weapons-check placeholder). Shots fired during approach never alter sequencing — only how long TopUp dwells.
- **Rise beat FIXED — two self-inflicted dead-ends**: after the fold fix, Vinny settled/reloaded correctly but never rose. (a) `SolveRiseDuck` evaluated the muzzle in the REST pose — the hand hangs at waist height, so every mid-height wall failed the clearance test → `riseDuck=-1` → Rise skipped forever. Now evals `handGid` in the AIMED pose (state 4) — what the risen arm actually presents; wall match widened to nearest-face-on-obstacle within 2m (0.6m radius could miss the mark). (b) Stationary pose pick chose `Crouching` vs `Idle` by `targetDuck` — but state 5 can only express duck>1.02, so a rise solving below that rendered shallow-crouch forever (eye never cleared → probe stayed 0 → Duck→Settle→None). Now picks on live `anim.duck`. Same contract bug class: intent solved correctly, render couldn't express it. "Every pose must express duck" codified as law in ANIMATION_SYSTEM.md. Compile clean.
- **Duck-scalar contract FIXED — armed poses now fold (cover posture overrides opportunity fire)**: user observed Vinny marching upright to cover "preferring to fire" — root cause: `duckIn` gated `wd()` to Walking-only and `idleIn` gated `icr()` to Idle-only in BOTH evaluators, so `anim.duck` produced zero fold the moment `ExposureArmed` picked `AimWalk`/`Aiming`. Fixed `VoxelCharacterAnimator.cs` (CPU FK — feeds render AND `TryGetEyeAnchor`/`TryGetJointWorld` hypothetical solves) + `CharacterPoseCompute.compute` (GPU instanced path — two sites: rot eval + chain eval): `wd()` now covers gait states (Walking + AimWalk), `icr()` covers stationary states (Idle + Aiming). Per-channel exclusivity preserved: `isAimingState` still owns arm swing/elbow + head aim — legs/body fold, gun stays on target. Net behavior: approach folds toward `targetDuck` while armed → eye sinks below parapet → exposure decays → arm stows naturally (the intended LOS-loss end to opportunity fire); `Engaged` now renders the solved `riseDuck` instead of popping to full stand. Docs synced (ANIMATION_SYSTEM state table + per-group sources, EXPOSURE_ENGAGEMENT doctrine note). Compile clean.
- **Engagement beats IMPLEMENTED — the authored rhythm now owns the arm**: `CombatController.EngagementBeat` (`None/Settle/TopUp/Rise/Engaged/Duck`) + `TickBeat()` runs inside `InCover` only. Arena flow as spec'd: arrival → `Settle` (seam, `afterSettle` = the branch point) → `TopUp` (partial-wheel `StartReload`, or seam pass-through on a full wheel = weapons-check placeholder) → `Settle` → `Rise` (`SolveRiseDuck` — min duck where posed eye AND hand-pivot+muzzleVertOffset both clear the picked wall's top via `TryGetEyeAnchor`/`TryGetJointWorld` overrides; unsolvable → settles and stops, no loop) → `Engaged` (stand and deliver — free exposure gate; dry or `emptyProbeSec` of zero contact → `Duck` → seam loops). `wantAim` ANDs with new `BeatAllowsFire` (Seek/Hold = opportunity channel; InCover = Rise/Engaged only) — a stray probe during `Settle` can never pop the arm. InCover hands `anim.duck` to the beat (`riseDuck` risen / `targetDuck` seams, eased `riseDuckEase`). Third F5 lane `SETTLE ▸ TOPUP ▸ SETTLE ▸ RISE ▸ ENGAGED ▸ DUCK` + `beat <name> · Ns left · riseDuck` line; `BeatLeft`/`RiseDuck` telemetry; every hop emits `Changed`. Tunables: `settleSec .5`, `riseHoldSec 1.2`, `emptyProbeSec .5`, `riseDuckEase 3`, `riseMargin .02`, `muzzleVertOffset .05`. Docs synced (ENGAGEMENT_BEATS as-built deltas, EXPOSURE_ENGAGEMENT gate line, COMBAT_COVER_STATE pointer). Compile clean.
- **`docs/systems/ENGAGEMENT_BEATS.md` — combat phase 3 spec (accepted)**: authored engagement rhythm over the exposure gates — beats sequence intent, gates still decide what's possible. `OpportunityAdvance` (seek = covering fire, not attack) → `SETTLE` (reusable seam primitive + future branch point) → `TOP-UP` (partial-wheel staggered reload / weapons-check placeholder) → `SETTLE` → `RISE` (triangulated pop-up: minimum duck where posed eye AND muzzle both clear the parapet — user caveat, gun isn't clear just because eye is) → `ENGAGED` "stand and deliver" (free fire until dry or sustained zero exposure) → `DUCK` → seam loops. Third lane on the flow chart. Deferred per user: rise-retry cadence + stray-LOS interruption (until Tan mobility), lookScan slot, branch table, per-weapon cylinders, weapons-check flourish, bore-line clearance refinement. Registered in index (Systems Design → 13).
- **One wheel, every trigger — cylinder unified across modes**: the cylinder is physical character state, not combat-AI state. New shared primitives `SpendRound()`/`StartReload()`/`RefillCylinder()`; the reload seat tick moved to the top of `CombatController.Update` above the `Idle` gate so it seats across mode switches. `EnterCombat` no longer refills (carryover is the rule); `START DUEL` calls `RefillCylinder()` on both fighters as the arena loadout. **Possess LMB** now spends chambers (`Cylinder>0 && !Reloading` gate) — dry wheel needs a deliberate **R** reload (manual here, auto in combat). **Weapons-tab FIRE button** gated + debits identically. Panel: flow dots/RELOAD work mode-agnostic; idle shows `reloading — +1 in Ns · full in Ns` or `cylinder empty — press R`.
- **Staggered reload + cylinder-dot wheel**: `reloadDwell` (instant refill after 2.2s) → `roundLoadInterval` (1.5s per chamber): dry → `Reloading`, `cylinder++` per interval + `Changed` per seat, arm dipped until the full wheel (swing-out model — `!reloading` gates wantAim; one-line flip if mid-load fire is ever wanted). Flow chart appends the wheel after `RELOAD`: `●●●○○○` — drains per FIRE, seats amber one-at-a-time during reload, green when chambered. Panel gains `Reloading`/`ReloadLeft` (time-to-full)/`NextRoundIn` readouts; cadence line shows `+1 in Ns · reload Ns left`. `reloadDwell`/`reloadUntil` fully retired; docs synced.
- **Panel rebuild → event-driven (`Changed` contract)**: `CombatController.Changed` + `CoverSensor.Changed` fire on every display-relevant mutation — state transitions routed through new `SetState` (Idle→Seek→Cover/Hold edges can't bypass), pick verdicts, armed edges, shots (`ShotsFired` counter added), reload refill, scans, visibility probes. `CoverMetricsPanel` subscribes via `Rehook` (tracks live bodies, resubscribes on change), flags `dirty`, rebuilds next Update — events never rebuild inside themselves. A 2Hz analog floor runs only while live (combat / possess / probe preview) for honest continuous readouts; idle scene = zero panel rebuilds. FIRE node now lights on the shot event itself — the flicker caveat is gone; `shots n` added to the cadence line.
- **Flow-chart honesty fixes**: `PROBE`/`EXPOSED` now light during setup-phase probe-rays preview (gated on `showTargetViz` so a stale cache can't hold them) — the setup chain correctly stops at ARMED ("can see him, gun not out"). `ExitCombat` clears `LastSeenTarget` and the sensor's `LastVisible` cache (new `CoverSensor.ClearVisibility()`) so idle reports a clean slate — no ghost nodes.
- **Engagement flow chart on the F5 panel + cadence telemetry**: `── engagement flow ──` section draws the combat pipeline as two lanes with nodes lit green when live / grey when stalled (FIRE/RELOAD amber): `IDLE ▸ SEEK ▸ COVER │ HOLD` (movement lane) over `PROBE ▸ EXPOSED ▸ ARMED ▸ CONVERGED ▸ FIRE ▸ RELOAD` (fire chain) — a greyed node marks exactly where the chain stalled, telemetry lines above explain why. `CombatController` gained `LastShotAt` (set on each successful `Fire`), `NextFireIn` (cadence countdown) and `ReloadLeft` (dwell remaining); panel shows `cadence Ns · next shot Ns · reload Ns left`. Panel style now `richText` for node colors.
- **COVER METRICS → COVER + COMBAT panel — centralized readout surface**: `CoverMetricsPanel` retitled and expanded — new `── combat ──` section carries `{State}·{LastResult}`, `cover {obstacle}·{wallClass}·reqDuck`, pick-scan verdict (`obs → pts → valid` + reject reasons), `exposure n/m (%)·armed·cyl n/6` (shows at setup too — `tested>0` = manual probe ran), solver line (`aim err·blend·dist`), `lastSeen`+age. Tan Suit section gains the same exposure/armed/cylinder/lastSeen mirror. The combat tab keeps only controls (START/EXIT, probe-rays toggle, probe/cadence sliders); all state/exposure/lastSeen/scan info labels moved to the panel — single place to read during playtest, survives HUD minimize for snapshots. EXPOSURE_ENGAGEMENT debug-viz section updated.
- **`docs/systems/PERCEPTION_AWARENESS.md` — city-wide perception spec written**: generalizes the shipped cone/probe split — `VisionSensor` = awareness ("does he notice"), `TryGetVisibleVoxels` = geometry ("can his eye reach him"), noticed ≠ shootable. Spec'd: actor registry + per-actor awareness table (detection level, lastSeen, identity tiers) replacing `List<Vector3>` targets; `SfxWaves` hearing channel → shared reactions (turn-to-sound, report, fight roll); witnessed-initiation legality (police react to what each observer's cone saw, not a global attacker flag — self-defense is emergent); back-turned ambush flow (hear → turn → sweep → detect → combat as earned defender status); engagement hand-off (EnterCombat stays explicit, probe stays cone-free); search resolution consumes `LastSeenTarget` + the papered 3-step lookScan pattern → return-to-normal. Explicit non-goals: search pattern content, identity tuning, dispatch policy, cross-week persistence. Registered in DOCUMENTATION_INDEX; EXPOSURE_ENGAGEMENT cross-linked.
- **Phase 2 — Exposure-Based Engagement IMPLEMENTED (crossfire build)**: voxel visibility IS the fire gate now, per the spec. **`VoxelCharacter.SampleSilhouetteWorld`** — one-time `(gid,rest)` sample set (per-group Y-sorted strided picks, `clamp(⌈n/8⌉,2,8)` ≈ 30–60 pts) posed through `PosePoint` under the LIVE instanced pose/transition params + world-mapped via extracted `CellToWorld` (`PoseToWorld` refactored onto it). **`CoverSensor.TryGetVisibleVoxels(threat)`** — `VisibleSet {visible,tested,fraction,centroid,target}` + `LastVisible`/`LastProbeEye`: posed-eye → rays at each sample through `VoxelCollisionWorld` (unobstructed = visible — no self-occlusion, accepted); `showTargetViz` paints green/red `tv_s{i}` beams + cyan `tv_c*` centroid cross. **`CombatController.TickExposureFire`** — first AI fire loop: probes on `visInterval` (0.12s), `wantAim = canSee && cylinder>0 && equipped` → `Arm`/`SetTarget(centroid)`; fire on `Blend≥0.7 && boreErr≤aimGateDeg(14°) && fireInterval(0.9s) && cylinder>0` → `ProjectileSystem.Fire`; **`ExposureArmed`** selects AimWalk (seeking) / Aiming (in-cover) pose lanes; combat only disarms what it armed; cylinder 0 → arm dips `reloadDwell` (2.2s) then refills (the reload beat); `LastSeenTarget`/`LastSeenAt` written per positive probe; `EnterCombat(feet, body)` overloads — `feet+1.3` retired as the aim target. **`ArmAimSolver` posture gate REMOVED** (user ruling: any pose can fire if the IK resolves) — `Crouching/duck>1.02/coverContext` no longer reject `Arm` or force-release; instead `Solve` drops *torso assist* when `duck>1.02` (the yaw un-folds the crouch lean — `IK_TORSO_OVERRIDE` bound), arms-only reach in the deep band. **`DebugHUDManager`**: START DUEL/picks pass the threat BODY (symmetric on AI-duel toggle); combat section shows `exposure n/m (%)`, `cylinder n/6`, `armed`, `lastSeen` + age, `probe rays` toggle + probe/cadence sliders; displayed/manual target tracks the live centroid (`lastSeen` in the gap); **probe rays works at setup too** — combat `Idle` + toggle → the HUD runs a slow manual probe so the beams/verdict preview the firing window before the duel. **Deferred per spec**: AI pop-up trigger, possess pop-up binding, threatEye→posed eye, lookScan search, hit model. E-variant compile clean via the project's Bee `Assembly-CSharp.rsp`. Docs synced (EXPOSURE_ENGAGEMENT → as-built, COMBAT_COVER_STATE Phase 2, AUTOBATTLE, DIFFERENTIATION §9.9, ANIMATION_SYSTEM gate note, COVER_HEIGHTFIELD).
- **`docs/systems/EXPOSURE_ENGAGEMENT.md` — Phase 2 implementation spec written**: `TryGetVisibleVoxels(eye, target)` — ~30–40 posed-silhouette samples (per-group extremes + strided, CPU pose eval, fresh each query) → rays unobstructed = visible; centroid = IK target (retires `feet + 1.3f`); `visibleCount>0` = FIRE gate. User ruling: **any pose can fire if the IK resolves** — posture gate relaxed (solver should drop torso-assist, not refuse, at deep duck — `IK_TORSO_OVERRIDE_STOMPS_CROUCH_LEAN` bounds it). Spec'd the honesty caveat (characters aren't in VoxelCollisionWorld → "visible" = unobstructed-to-sample, no self-occlusion), the first AI fire loop (armed + converged + cadence → `Fire`), `lastSeenTarget` (pos/time/ref — pop-up re-query + later lookScan search), 6-shot cylinder + reload dwell, symmetric Tan-side query, debug viz contract (per-sample beams + centroid + metrics), deferred list (pop-up triggers, possess binding, threatEye→posed eye, hit-model, squad budget). Registered in DOCUMENTATION_INDEX; COMBAT_COVER_STATE Phase 2 links it.
- **FIXED: cover-exit snap — transition freeze read the POST-write duck** — write-order race in the flip frame: the drive writes `anim.duck = 0` on band exit, then `CharacterAnimation.Update` detects the state flip and freezes the outgoing pose with `transFromDepth = duck` — already 0 → the "folded" from-pose evaluated upright → the blend lerped upright→upright and the un-fold snapped. Entry looked smooth only because the pose-swap blend masked the same flaw in reverse. Fix: `prevFrameDuck` snapshot at the end of every Update; the freeze now reads the PRE-write scalar → honest folded→upright un-fold out, genuine upright→folded fold-in in. Single write covers GPU (`_InstanceAnimData.y`) and CPU (`transDuckA`) evaluators. Tracked: `TRANSITION_FREEZE_READS_POSTWRITE_DUCK`.
- **FIXED: possess drive ducked Vinny on the THREAT side of a wall** — two threat-blind paths: (a) ring probes hit any wall face in reach and only gated on axial alignment — a threat-side face is still a wall, so `ProbeD` solved a duck; (b) `TryZoneAt` checked claimed+painted but never `state` — quality pass splats BOTH faces (threat-independent by design), so the threat side's cells reported `Crouch duck1.70` while staying Untested/Exposed in phase 2. Fix mirrors the field's own facing test in both places: ring probes now require `dot(faceNormal, dirToThreat) < facingDot` (open side must point AWAY from the shooter), and `TryZoneAt` returns false on non-`Hidden` cells when the field was `BuiltWithThreat` (new flag; no-threat paint keeps the old claimed-cell behavior for inspection). Result: exposed side → stands, `coverContext` clear, aimable (honest); hidden side → ducks as before. Tracked: `POSSESS_DRIVE_DUCKS_ON_THREAT_SIDE`. Compile clean (E variant).
- **Cover zones → uniform standoff band; combat owns the approach duck (user redesign)**: the requirement-scaled corridor (`standoff × 2..4`, deeper zones reaching further out to fold him early) is gone — `CoverField` now paints a flat `max(standoff, CellSize)` band flush to each face, marking only the stand spot. **Possess**: unchanged code, new footprint — ducks in the band, releases out, simple. **Combat**: `SeekingCover` no longer reads `TryZoneAt` — the approach IS combat posture now: `anim.duck` eases toward the *destination* wall's requirement for the whole advance (new `seekDuckEase` = 2.5/s, tunable to snap), Full-wall picks stay upright, morph walls crab-walk at partial duck. The step also scales by `anim.DuckSpeedFactor` so the slowed gait rate and the slowed transform stay married (deep approach = slow crab-walk, not feet-slide). Arrival/hold still snap `anim.duck = targetDuck`; pick destination was already the owning column's stand-spot. Docs synced (COMBAT_COVER_STATE, ANIMATION_SYSTEM); E-variant compile clean, P-variant still the pre-existing stale-Bee artifact.
- **Combat doctrine doc pass — exposure-based engagement (the crossfire model)**: user clarified the full combat loop — Vinny advances firing while Tan's voxels are visible; duck-in decays exposure smoothly (posed eye sinks → his own wall eats the rays → visible set fades → stow, crouch-idle, `lastSeenTarget` retained); explicit **pop-up** override = the one authored exposure choice → mutual exposure window (his `h(duck)` → stand lights him up on the enemy's identical query — the exposure race); S&W Model 10 = **6 shots** → duck back to reload (crouch-idle until a reload anim exists). Aim target = **centroid of visible posed enemy voxels** (~30–40 samples, rays from posed eye through `VoxelCollisionWorld`) — retires `feet + 1.3f`; `coverContext` stays the posture signal, the FIRE gate becomes `visibleCount > 0`. Parapet fire is emergently headshot-only — accepted (recoil/sway + tiny voxel set keep it honest). Search hand-off: `lastSeenTarget` + `lookScan` sweep = the later visual-search phase (3-step pattern papered). Deferred by design: AI pop-up trigger, possess-mode pop-up control, `threatEye` 0.55 → posed-eye query, hit-model (pure ballistic vs thin probability layer). Docs updated: `DIFFERENTIATION_FROM_ORIGINAL.md` (§9.2 cover → implemented, §9.2c `h(duck)` contract NEW, §9.6 Approach A → implemented, §9.7 status, §9.9 exposure doctrine NEW), `COMBAT_COVER_STATE.md` (Phase 2 spec + stale pre-h(duck) duplicate block removed), `COMBAT_AUTOBATTLE.md` (exposure design principle), `COVER_HEIGHTFIELD.md` (Phase E + the e/h symmetry), `STEEL_CITY_ROADMAP.html` (scalar-cover milestone done, crossfire = NOW, search/popped-trigger queue, footer).
- **Axis-shift sweep — corridor formula + labels**: `CoverField` strip depth was `standoff × (2 + 2·clamp01(reqDepth))` — `clamp01` on duck-space saturated at 1 for the entire deep band and gave morph walls a 3× corridor; now `duck × 0.5` (requirement fraction → 2×..4×, proportional). Readout labels switched to duck units (`duck{}` in PickInfo, `best duck`/`zone duck` in the metrics panel). Audit-verified clean seams: GPU sync writes `anim.duck` → `_InstanceAnimData.x`; WeaponMount weld passes the duck field; `transFromDepth`/`transDuckA` freeze the real scalar for transitions; `DepthToDuck` maps state-5 queries `1+d` / direct-duck for others (morph queries work); VisionSensor eye rides the posed head anchor (auto-follows duck); `isWalkState` covers AimWalk (wd() composes there); no `duck`/`crouchDepth` serialized in `.anim.json` (runtime channel, not asset data). Design note: `threatEye` constant assumes a standing threat — conservative for LOS validation; revisit if symmetric ducked duels matter.
- **Duck-space (0..2) end-to-end — the "fixed crouch" hardcoding decontaminated**: the whole cover pipeline spoke the legacy 0..1 axis (`duck = 1+d` or `duck = 0`), so the stand→shallow **morph band was unreachable** — standing snapped to the full shallow hold with no in-between. Now: `CoverSensor.DuckForTop/Raw` piecewise-inverts `h(duck)` (sub-1 = morph band, over-1 = deep band, >2 = Low); `CoverField` claims cells in duck units with piecewise eye anchors; `PossessController` writes `anim.duck` directly and the pose pick is a pure scalar rule (duck>1.02 → Crouching/Walking state-5; morph rides Idle/Walking where `icr`/`wd` scale the fold); `CombatController.targetDuck` + `CrouchAndCover` use the same rule; `HoldPosition` = duck 1 (old "depth 0" = shallow hold); `CrouchLabel`/metrics show morph/deep bands; `crouchDepthSpeed` field deleted; `ArmAimSolver` gate unchanged (coverContext carries verdict, duck>1.02 = deep-band backstop, sub-1 debug duck still aims = sentry). E-variant compile clean; P-variant fails on pre-existing stale-Bee-artifact errors (its .rsp predates CoverSensor.cs — unrelated). Docs synced.
- **Live ring solve owns cover depth; baked zone d demoted to fallback** — the zone's `reqDepthOf` is baked at scan time vs the owning wall's *global* top + cell floor — on gradient/stepped walls it disagreed with the live column at his spot, and `zd >= req` let the stale number win (ring said 0.2, blend showed 0.70). Zone now owns posture only (`DriveCrouch`); when the ring finds a crouch wall its live `DepthForTopRaw(wallTop, supportY)` stays the requirement. Baked `zd` fills in only when the ring is blind. Note for tuning: the measured crouch band is `0.50→0.40` — the authored C1→C2 head heights span ~10cm, so most solvable walls land in the deep half of the d-axis; widening that band is an authoring job (deeper C2 head), not code. Compile clean.
- **Cover ring probes rewritten: horizontal face-find + axial gate** — the fixed-radius down-cast ring measured column tops at a constant `pr` from his feet, so (a) hugging a wall put the probe BEHIND it → sampled the far side → cover verdict vanished right where it should be strongest, and (b) diagonal probes landed on wall end-caps past the painted zone's lateral edge → he ducked outside the paint. Now: 8 horizontal rays at knee height find each direction's face at its real distance (max `standoff+0.25` reach), `dot(dir,−normal) ≥ 0.85` rejects grazing/diagonal hits, then `TryColumnTop` samples just inside the face. Close = correct, past-the-end = rejected, reach = real. In-zone deep corridors (up to `standoff×4` ≈ 2.2m for deep walls) are painted intent — the paint is the posture contract there. Compile clean.
- **Cover doctrine retuned per user direction** — (a) **Full walls gate IK aim too**: new `anim.coverContext` flag written by whoever owns the live verdict (possess drive's `detected` = ring OR painted zone any-class; combat's `CrouchAndCover`/`ExitCombat`; cleared on possess Release). `ArmAimSolver` gate = `Crouching state || duck > 1.02 || coverContext` — a Full wall covers standing but he still can't see over it: covered is covered. (b) **Full cells paint quality = 1.0 flat** — `qw = 0.85 + 0.15·slack` removed; surplus wall height is not a grade. (c) **IMMEDIATE cover posture**: gap latch (0.35m dropout hysteresis) and the 3/s `coverDriveEase`/`crouchDepthSpeed` drains deleted — `anim.crouchDepth` writes snap to the zone requirement the same frame, in and out. The painted cell IS the posture: color says what he does. (d) Possess pick demotes aim behind ANY cover: Full-cover cells pick `moving ? Walking : Idle` (was AimWalk/Aiming pose with gated IK — raised gun pointing nowhere). Verified: all 82 .cs compile clean.
- **Crumple-gait rollout: movement now discriminates the duck channel** — the pick rule is `ducked + moving → Walking pose` (wd deltas ride the gait) / `ducked + stationary → Crouching pose` (authored hold). (a) `PossessController`: cover pick branched on `moving` — WASD-ing behind a wall now plays the authored walk-duck instead of the hold-pose slide; combatHold crouch inherits the same rule. (b) `CombatController`: SeekingCover always picks `Walking` — painted crouch zones duck via the scalar, not a state swap; `CrouchAndCover` unchanged (stationary = hold). (c) `ArmAimSolver` posture gate → `EffectiveState == Crouching || duck > 1.02f` — the 1.02 matches the pick's `crouchDepth > 0.02` exactly (alias duck = 1+d), catching ducked-WALKING movers the state check missed; sub-1 debug duck still composes with aim. (d) Possess cover readout scalar-gated (shows during cover-walk). Bonus emergent: `crouchMoveFactor` scales ground speed and `DuckSpeedFactor` scales gait rate off the same scalar — feet stay married to terrain. Verified: all 82 .cs compile clean.
- **Possess hold-exit re-arm race FIXED** — walking out of a combatHold cover spot fired a single `solver.Arm()` on the flip frame while `EffectiveState` was still `Crouching` (the new pick is requested later in the same Update, and `crouchDepth` still reads holdDepth → pose stays Crouching until the drive eases it out). The posture gate rejected the one-shot and nothing retried — he stood into the `Aiming` pose with no IK. Now a per-frame retry (`!combatHold && solver.enabled && !IsAiming → Arm`) lets the gate refuse while ducked-in-cover and land the first frame the pick leaves Crouching — also yields the pop-up/duck cycle for free on re-entry. Tracked: `docs/known_issues/runtime/POSSESS_HOLD_EXIT_ARM_ONESHOT_RACE.md`.
- **UNITY PORT: scalar/channel animation model lands in the runtime** — the HTML-validated system now drives CPU + GPU evaluators uniformly. (a) **THE `duck` scalar 0..2** on `CharacterAnimation` replaces the 0..1 `crouchDepth` field as THE silhouette axis — `crouchDepth` survives as a write-alias (cover depth d → duck 1+d) so `CombatController`/`PossessController` keep their depth semantics. Idle evaluates the authored hold (`crouching→crouchDeep` absolute pose, `icr`); Walking folds crumple deltas (`walkDuckMid→walkDuckPose`, `wd`) under the gait — never seed walk-duck from authored crouch (absolute strides pin legs). Legacy state 5.x reads `duck−1` as its old depth axis — cover queries (`TryMeasureTopY`/`TryGetEyeAnchor` state-5 overrides) map `1+d` → identical poses. (b) **Integrated `gaitTime`** (`+= dt·walkSpeed·DuckSpeedFactor`, surviving state changes) replaces derived `animTime % (dur/speed)` phase on both evaluators + `PosePoint`/`PoseVoxels`/`PoseVoxelsToBuffer` wrappers (−1 sentinel falls back to `animTime×speed` for legacy callers; test rigs pass their already-integrated clock). (c) **Duck→speed coupling** — `duckSpeedScale`/`duckSpeedMin` (0.35): `factor = lerp(1→min, duck/2)` slows the gait rate only — the preview of the move-speed curve. (d) **Look-scan channel** — `lookScan`/`lookScanBlend` layers the `looking` yaw sweep over ANY state's head (CPU `ikOverrides.Count==0` gate, GPU `ikArmOn` gate — yields to aim). (e) **`bodyForward`** — Z torso offset on all four endpoints, body+head+arms (post-rotation model space, legs planted). (f) **Data**: `CrouchingData` +`bodyForward`/`headTilt`, `AnimParamsData` +`walkDuckMid`/`walkDuckPose` (absent → modest symmetric-fold defaults, NOT authored crouch). ASP packing 19→**27** float4s ([18].zw = crouch bodyForward, [19-22] = D1, [23-26] = D2); `_InstanceAnimData[i*3+2]` now `(duck, transDuckA, gaitTime, lookScanBlend)` — free slots, no stride change. (g) **Hotkeys remapped to the scalar model**: `C`/Num5 = duck cycle (stand→1→2→1) on the CURRENT pose — no state change; `L`/Num2 = look-scan toggle; `I`/`X`/Num0 release zeroes duck. (h) `TryMeasureTopY` samples 4 gait phases (worst-of-gait top) so moving-duck heights bound the bob. (i) `DebugHUD` duck slider 0..2 + duck-speed floor + scan toggle. Verified: **all 82 project .cs files compile clean** via Unity 6.0.3 Roslyn csc. `VoxelProxyRaymarch.shader`'s reference eval left untouched (dead path per its own comment). Docs updated: `ANIMATION_SYSTEM.md` (states table → scalar model, ASP/instance layout, per-group sources), `TEST_WEEK.md` (pad map + three axes), `COMBAT_COVER_STATE.md` (alias note).
- **Possess hold-exit re-arm race FIXED** — walking out of a combatHold cover spot fired a single `solver.Arm()` on the flip frame while `EffectiveState` was still `Crouching` (the new pick is requested later in the same Update, and `crouchDepth` still reads holdDepth → pose stays Crouching until the drive eases it out). The posture gate rejected the one-shot and nothing retried — he stood into the `Aiming` pose with no IK. Now a per-frame retry (`!combatHold && solver.enabled && !IsAiming → Arm`) lets the gate refuse while ducked-in-cover and land the first frame the pick leaves Crouching — also yields the pop-up/duck cycle for free on re-entry. Tracked: `docs/known_issues/runtime/POSSESS_HOLD_EXIT_ARM_ONESHOT_RACE.md`.

- **New authored Civilian1 deployed** — user hand-tailored all four duck/crouch endpoints and exported; baked into `DEFAULT_PARAMS` (C2 deep: modelLower 17, lean 0.88, kneeL 1.80, stride 1.79 lunge · D2: bodyForward 5.5, bodyLower 8.5, lean 1.03, asymmetric legs — torso-drop+forward "hunched scurry" style) and into `CROUCH_BASE_STANCE` + `mirrored` preset swaps. File copied to `StreamingAssets/voxel_characters/Civilian1.json` (old → `.backup.json`); voxels identical, `sitting` refilled by the C# defaults pass, `walkDuck*`/`bodyForward` inert until the runtime port. Authored copy kept at `VoxelAssetStudio/Civilian1_authored.json`.
- **Duck→Speed coupling** — toolbar `Duck→Spd` toggle + Min slider (default 0.35): `animSpeed` is now effective = `base × lerp(1→min, duck/2)`, previewing the in-game move-speed falloff vs crouch depth. Readout shows effective speed.
