- **`gaitTime` integrated clock** — gait phase now accumulates `dt × speed` instead of `animTime mod (cycleDur/speed)`: speed changes are rate changes, never phase teleports (the "pose rewrite" pop). Call sites unchanged (wrappers swap the clock); state-switch no longer restarts the gait mid-blend. Scan/sweep oscillators stay on wall-clock `animTime`.
- **`bodyForward` endpoint field** — Z-axis torso offset, twin of `bodyLower`, on all four endpoints (`C1/C2/D1/D2 Body Fwd`, ±15 vox). Post-rotation model-space shift applied to body+head+arms — throws mass forward over the knees for deep squats.
- **Endpoint slider ranges widened** — knee/elbow −0.5→3.0 (knees-to-chest + hyper-extension headroom), stride/arm ±2.5, modelLower 25, bodyLower 20, lean/headPitch ±1.5, headTilt ±1.0.
- **Panel authoring order** — endpoint/param sliders now render above the walk-keyframe editor; `C1/C2 Body Lower` restored (was dropped in the collapse).

- **IK Aim head tracking** — `ikAim.headTrack` (0..1, default 1): head rotation solved via `basisDelta` (bind-forward +Z → target dir, same construction as the arm solve) in the pre-body fold frame, blended over the state's head pose. Aiming takes eyes — vision cone sweeps onto the aim point while any state/duck plays.
- **IK Aim torso pitch assist** — `ikAimTorsoPitch()` folds the target's elevation × `torsoShare` into `bodyLean`, so the torso assists vertically as well as in yaw. `torsoTwist` stays a tunable blade constant (0 = pure solve).
- **Look Scan channel** — `lookScan = {enabled, blend}` layers the authored `animParams.looking` head oscillation over ANY state's head pose (IK headTrack wins while aiming). BASE CROUCH + Look Scan = crouched alert sentry with no new state.
- **STATE COLLAPSE** — the grid shrank to 5 real states (Idle, Walking, Sit, Down, T-Pose) + the museum aim trio. Retired: Looking(2)→lookScan channel; Crouching/CrouchDeep(5/5.1)→duck endpoints; Duck Axis(5.2)→the walkDuck scalar; Crouch Walk(12)→Walking×Duck. Legacy states still evaluate for saved JSON/transitions.
- **Dual duck endpoints** — new `walkDuckMid` section lands at duck 1 (defaults = authored BASE CROUCH shallow), `walkDuckPose` lands at duck 2 (defaults = authored deep). `wd(k) = lerp(mid,far,duck-1) × min(1,duck)`. Stance presets retargeted: sh→mid, dp→far. Verified: duck1/duck2 reproduce authored endpoints field-for-field.
- **Crouch Hold on Idle** — held crouch pose on Idle via `icr()` over `crouching`/`crouchDeep`. DEFAULT_PARAMS.crouching/crouchDeep updated to authored BASE CROUCH (toxic defaults eliminated); stance presets dual-write both endpoint pairs.
- **Shared `duck` scalar** — idle hold and walking duck read ONE bound scalar (0–2). IMPORTANT: the endpoint sets are different data — `crouching`/`crouchDeep` = authored poses for the stationary hold; `walkDuckMid`/`walkDuckPose` = crumple deltas under the gait (defaults restored to symmetric folds — authored absolute values were pinning legs against the cycle). Stance presets → crouch endpoints only. Height parity `h(duck)` is a tuning target: match walkDuck `modelLower` to the held pose.
- **Look Scan = yaw only** — pitch dropped from the scan channel; head pitch belongs to the pose underneath.
- **IK Leg debug channel REMOVED** — the foot-target IK experiment (ikLeg/ikLegSolve/ikFootPoint/gizmos/UI) pulled out entirely per user direction; foot planting stays an authored-endpoint problem, not a solve.
- **`walkDuckPose` now owns the whole level-2 pose** — extended beyond limb deltas with `bodyLean`, `bodyLower`, `headPitch`, `headTilt` (defaults keep the deep-crouch look: lean 0.67, head −0.26). Body channel on Walking now uses `duckT = walkDuck/2` against the authored endpoint instead of borrowing crouch endpoints — duck=2 IS the authored pose, authored live via 13 Walking-panel sliders.
- **`walkDuckPose` authored endpoint section** — the duck far-side is now an authored param section (`animParams.walkDuckPose`: leg/arm/elbow deltas + absolute `modelLower`), editable via sliders on the Walking panel at Duck=2, exported per-character through the generic section merge/diff — NOT hardcoded constants. `legFold = walkDuck/2` ramps deltas to the endpoint at duck 2. Editing it never touches `walkKeyframes` — base gait uncontaminated by construction.
- **Walk Duck unified to one 0–2 axis** — Depth + Duck Body merged into a single Duck slider (same shape as state 5.2's duckAxis): 0–1 crumples toward the shallow recipe scaled in, 1–2 lerps shallow→deep at full strength. `bodyDepth` derives the endpoint lerp on Walking (`crd` helper); crouch states unchanged. `legFold = min(1.5, walkDuck)` keeps deepening the knee fold through the deep half; `modelLower = legFold·0.35·Cr`.
- **Walk Duck crumple fix** — `modelLower` alone dropped the whole model through the floor (free walk legs have no fold to catch it). Now a symmetric knee fold on the walking limbs (`knee +0.55`, `thigh +0.30` at duck=1) drops the hips while `modelLower` scales to 35% (`DUCK_LEG_KNEE/DUCK_LEG_STRIDE/DUCK_MODEL_FRAC` constants). Gait still mirrors on top — the fold is a symmetric baseline, not a role.
- **Walk Duck channel on Walking state** — new `walkDuck` global (0..1) scales the crouch BODY channel (modelLower/bodyLower/bodyLean/headPitch, still depth-lerped) while `walkKeyframes` owns the limbs — legs free to mirror under a crouched body, unlike `duckwalk` where legs stay pinned to asymmetric crouch bases. Walking panel gets Depth + Duck Body sliders. Compare both ownership models directly.
- **`duckwalk` gait — mirrored walk on crouch stance** — the upright walk cycle verbatim (`autoMirror:true`, same ±0.4/0.8 kf deltas) riding the crouched base; bob/shift damped. Replaced the authored `lurch` (kept in git history if wanted back). Verified: clean at shallow; one flagged frame at deep kf2 (L −109°/−64° over-reach on the lunged base).
- **`lurch` gait added — two-legged low walk** — both legs engaged in alternating locomotion (R swings through at kf0 while L drives; L lifts/replants at kf2 while R bears weight), authored with per-leg amplitudes around the asymmetric base. Verified clean at BOTH stance endpoints. Pair with `hunched` stance for the body-over-knees mobster read.
- **Gait catalog rebuilt as lead-follow (autoMirror OFF)** — mirroring deltas over the asymmetric authored stance makes the planted L leg absorb stepping-leg numbers and hyper-extends (shin −89°). All 5 gaits re-authored with fixed leg roles across 4 keyframes (REACH→SETTLE→DRAG→RELOAD): L = planted lead with small drive oscillation, R = stepping leg. `sneak`/`shuffle`/`stalk`/`scuttle`/`march` retuned; `DEFAULT_PARAMS.crouchWalk` now equals `sneak`. Every keyframe verified against the pose math — no bad zones; `march` lifts L at DRAG deliberately. `ready` stance flagged TODO for weapon-conditional arm deltas (AIM_WEAPON_PRESETS/anchors).
- **Stance catalog rebuilt from authored pose** — the old catalog was calibrated against `DEFAULT_PARAMS` (a different pose entirely). Now `CROUCH_BASE_STANCE` = verified authored replica and every preset derives via `stanceDerive()` showing only its deltas: `BASE CROUCH` (reference), `mirrored` (true L/R swap), `hunched`, `upright`, `low`, `tall`, `relaxed`, `ready`. All 8 verified through the pose math — every leg lands in PLANT/TUCK, no bad zones. Catalog rule in code: never seed from `DEFAULT_PARAMS`; keep `kneeBendL ≈ legStrideL` when varying the plant leg.
- **Preset pick persists in dropdown** — `appendPresetSelect` was resetting to the placeholder after `buildParamControls()` rebuilt the panel; `presetPicks` now records the last pick per label and re-selects it, so the Stance/Gait dropdowns show what was applied (both Stance pickers share the key — stay in sync across states). Verified: BASE CROUCH reproduces the authored pose exactly; SQUAT confirmed to be the tool-defaults pose (looks wrong because it overwrites authored values).
- **BASE CROUCH replica preset + authored-pose truth discovered** — the authored crouch saved in `Civilian1.json` differs from the tool's `DEFAULT_PARAMS` entirely: real authored = **PLANT+TUCK** (L `legStride +1.34/knee 1.34` → thigh −77° fwd + shin ~0 flat; R `−0.51/1.05` → tucked) deepening to fwd-lunge+TUCK (`modelLower 11/14.5`, lean .2/.67, arms −.23/−.31, elbows .49/.49→1.23/.84). `DEFAULT_PARAMS.crouching` is a different all-tuck pose (lower 4/8) — flagged, not silently changed. New `'BASE CROUCH'` preset replicates all 24 authored scalars — verified byte-for-byte by a diff script (REPLICA: EXACT). Prior `squat` relabeled "tool defaults (NOT the authored pose)". This is why picking `squat` looked wrong: it was overwriting the authored pose.
- **CROUCH CATALOG re-verified by computed pose math** — built an articulation inspector (thigh = −legStride through the sign=−1 wrapper, shin = thigh + kneeBend) and ran the authored crouch + every preset/gait keyframe through it. Authored template = **TUCK** (thigh +66° back, shin +132° doubles back) + **KNEEL** (thigh 0°, shin +80° flat behind) — no forward-planted leg; `modelLower` drops the body onto the folded legs (user's hypothesis confirmed). User's +1.13 fix = a second valid family, **PLANT** (thigh forward + knee≈stride → shin vertical). Fixed: `kneel` now front-PLANT + rear-TUCK (real take-a-knee); gait forward-stride deltas capped (folded shins no longer swing forward — was the lifted-foot artifact); articulation-family comment + doc §8 rewritten with the verified semantics. All shipped values now classify into named families.
- **CROUCH CATALOG recalibrated to authored articulation** — first pass produced "sitting-on-calves" poses (both thighs forward + both knees maxed). Every stance preset now keeps the authored template: planted front leg (thigh-forward ≈ knee bend) + folded trail leg (thigh vertical-or-BACK — positive legStride). `kneel` trail leg now swings back (+0.15/+0.20) instead of forward (−0.4/−1.2); `even` moderated to thigh≈knee angles; gait knee deltas capped to swing-lift (0.45–0.55) since the stance already carries ~1.2–1.6 rad of bend. Articulation-family rule documented in the catalog comment.
- **CROUCH PRESET CATALOG (HTML)** — compositing surface: `CROUCH_STANCE_PRESETS` (5 named stances writing both `crouching`+`crouchDeep` scalars: `squat`/`even`/`hunch`/`upright`/`kneel`) x `CROUCH_GAIT_PRESETS` (5 named gaits replacing the whole `crouchWalk` section: `sneak`/`shuffle`/`stalk`/`scuttle`/`march`). `appendPresetSelect` dropdown rows: Stance picker on all crouch states (5/5.1/5.2), Gait+Stance pickers on Crouch Walk so the combo tunes from one panel. Picks are deterministic full-section writes; manual slider edits afterwards still export.
- **IK AIM arm side corrected to Unity-rendered right** — the tool's "L/R Arm" labels (gid 2 = "L", gid 3 = "R") were authored in the UN-mirrored view, so gid 3 renders as the LEFT arm under the Unity-view mirror. `Arm: right` now owns **gid 2/8** (what Unity draws as the right arm), `left` = 3/9; the torso blade sign and elbow-hint X flip per side. User-verified: the raised arm was reading as left in Unity view.
- **Joint Axis/Sign panel on all states** — extracted the param-row renderer (`appendParamRow`) and moved the rig-mapping rows out of Idle/Walking into a shared `RIG_AXIS_PARAMS` block rendered by `appendVisionControls`, so every state (incl. T-Pose/Down) shows the same axis/sign controls.
- **IK AIM refined: single pistol arm + torso blade** (user: dual-arm aim looked "zombie"). Channel now defaults to a single pistol arm; Torso blade added: `ikAim.torsoTwist` default −0.2 — the authored single-pistol "blades left shoulder back" stance — composed into the same body transform the chain applies (and that `ikAimSolve` folds the target through, so aim stays true under the blade); `torsoShare` yaw-tracks off-center targets (Unity's torso-assist term). Arm picker + twist/share sliders in the debug block.
- **IK AIM DEBUG harness (HTML)** — simulates the `ArmAimSolver` channel in `character_animator.html` so the IK-only aim doctrine is visible before Unity ports it. `ikAimSolve` mirrors `Solve()`: law-of-cosines elbow placement in the `elbowHint` plane (elbows tuck down), forearm's cumulative rotation lands the hand axis on target, and the body transform folds the target into the pre-body frame so a crouched lean still aims true. The solved rotations REPLACE the group's `ownRot` on gids 2/3/8/9, blended by `ikAim.blend` (parity with `Slerp(base, ik, blend)` into `ikOverrides`) — over ANY state (excluding T-Pose/Down), which is the point: simple poses cycle while arms track. Hand point = most-distal forearm voxel from the shoulder pivot (cached per model). New "IK Aim (debug)" param block: enabled checkbox, blend/dist/height(0=auto chest 0.58·H)/reach sliders, red target sphere + aim beam gizmo. `ANIMATION_SYSTEM.md` §8.4 records the harness.
- **HTML PROTO: unified duck axis (state 5.2) + Crouch Walk (state 12)** — first implementation of the `ANIMATION_SYSTEM.md` §6c/§8 contract, HTML-only (Unity port pending visual validation). (a) **Duck Axis preview state 5.2**: a `duckAxis` 0–2 scrub rides the two existing mechanisms — `rebuildAnimatedMesh` maps duck<1 → `heldBlend{from:Idle, weight:duck}` (the held A/B eval now branches inside `posedPosTransition`, weight-driven not timer-driven — the runtime's missing stand→shallow segment), duck≥1 → `crouchDepth = duck−1`. Param panel renders a "Duck" slider (0–2) for 5.2; crouch states 5/5.1 and state 12 keep the Depth slider (now also rebuilds while paused). (b) **CrouchWalk state 12**: new `animParams.crouchWalk` section (same schema as `walkKeyframes`, authored ~half amplitude, cycle 1.4s); `getWalkPose` refactored into shared `getKfCyclePose(wkf,time,speed,slot)` with per-set cache slots + `getCrouchWalkPose` wrapper. `computeGroupRotation` adds `isCrouchWalkState`: body channel = `Cr` crouch scalars at `crouchDepth` (lean/lower/head) AND limb baselines fold to depth; keyframed gait rides additively per joint — `sign·(Cr(base) + kf)` for arms/legs/elbows/knees, kf `forearmTwist`; bob/shift sourced from the crouchWalk set. `buildWalkKeyframeUI` generalized to `buildKfEditor(container, wkf, title)` — state 12 gets the full kf editor + Depth preview; gait inspector, pivot gizmos, vision cone, and both bob/shift apply sites extended. Old assets inherit `crouchWalk` from `DEFAULT_PARAMS` via the section merge; `diffAnimParams` exports it generically. (c) `ANIMATION_SYSTEM.md` amended: §2 state table (5.2/12), §4 group-table note (additive composition), §5 `crouchWalk` schema row, §6c planned→prototyped status, §8 locked contract — ONE state (no deep/aim variants), ONE gait set, **aiming = IK channel not a state** (doctrine recorded), Unity-port checklist incl. the four other pose-engine copies (`voxel_editor.html`, `character_pipeline.html`, `character_pose_engine.js`, `generate_pipeline_js.py`). Bracket-balance verified; no JS runtime available for syntax check — browser check pending.
- **B2 LIVE: gradient-wall testbed + possess-mode cover drive** (user design: "enter possess after BEGIN DUEL — Vinny's crouch logic stays active, folding standing → crouch → standing as the wall top sweeps"). (a) `gen_cover_pit.py`: the 4-box ladder is REPLACED by one continuous wall in the same lane (z92-99): full segment h16 (0.80m top) → taper h15..h8 in 1-vox steps → null tail h6 (0.30m). At Vinny's measured heights (0.62/0.50/0.45 + 0.02 margin) the crouch band is tops ~0.57-0.62m — h12 is THE crouch step (d≈0.4) and it sits dead on the x94 duel axis; h13+ Full, h11- Low — the ~1-voxel band width is the spec-predicted quantization, now legible on a walkable wall. Pick anchors re-pointed: cover_wall→full, cover_crouch→h12 (the crouch pick), cover_mid→h11 Low edge + cover_deep→null tail (expected-fail picks that exercise the classifier's reject path); north `_n` twins kept. Stasset rebuilt + binary-verified (v2 tail, 10 attachmentPoints). (b) `PossessController` cover drive: per-frame 8-dir ring probe of adjacent column tops (`TryColumnTop`, radius 0.5m) → `DepthForTop(top, supportY)` on the most protective column (lowest d) → eased `anim.crouchDepth` (`coverDriveEase` 3/s; d≤0 Full → stands, 0<d≤1 crouch at d, d>1/no-column → release). Depth also scales move speed (`crouchMoveFactor` 0.45 — deep crouch shuffles) and flips the pose pick to `Crouching` while depth>0.02; a latched test pose still wins. Vinny's `CoverSensor` is auto-attached + `Refresh(true)`'d on possess so heights are measured. `crouchDepth` resets on release; live `cover: crouchDepth N.NN` readout under the crosshair; `coverDrive` inspector toggle. **FIX (same pass): possessing Vinny blinded the threat render** — `CombatController.Update` exits combat while possessed and `ExitCombat` nulls `sensor.Threat`, and every threat-keyed paint path gates on `HasThreat` (verdict field, heat, LOS, candidates). `PossessController` now captures `coverSensor.Threat` into `pinnedThreat` at possess and re-pins it per frame while active — enemy context survives the combat exit so threat V-modes stay live under manual control. **FIX (playtest): Vinny crouched in possess mode with no combat** (user: "nowhere near the arena wall" — the drive was threat-agnostic, so cars/curbs/raised pads counted as cover). Drive now gated on `coverSensor.HasThreat` per doctrine — crouching only exists under a threat context (mid-duel possess keeps it via pinnedThreat; cold possess never crouches). **NEW `CoverMetricsPanel`** (user ask — a dedicated read-only surface for cover metrics, right-center of screen, for snapshot debugging): F5 toggles, one cached label rebuilt ~5Hz, `GUI.depth=-100` so it draws over the HUD and survives minimize. Sections: Vinny pos/pose/depth + POSSESSED flag · combat State+LastResult · threat armed/pinned · sensor heights (stand/shallow→deep + FALLBACK flag) + supportY + wall class counts + cell stats + top reject reasons · possess-drive line (supportY, best d→req) + 8-dir probe ring (top+class per direction) · Tan Suit combat state. Drive statics exposed on `PossessController` (DriveActive/ThreatPinned/DriveBestD/DriveReq/DriveSupport/ProbeTop/ProbeD); `CombatController` gained `CoverWallClass`/`TargetCrouchDepth` getters. Panel attaches beside DebugHUDManager in `CityMap3D.Awake`. **Probe ring now records hit identity** (user: "depth 1.00 at odd times" — snapshots showed `best d 1.00 → req 1.00` with all-pad probe tops, pointing at non-wall columns): drive probes cast `world.Raycast` directly (same ray as `TryColumnTop`) capturing `VoxelRayHit.volumeId`/`point` — `PossessController.ProbeVol[8]` names what each probe saw (`t`=terrain/`v`=volume tag in the ring), and `DriveBestDir` reports the winning direction + its top + volume on the drive line. Repro-and-snapshot will name the phantom column directly. **ROOT CAUSE FOUND (playtest)**: panel showed all 8 probes hitting `block_45_building` pad (top 0.20) yet `best d 1.00` — `DepthForTop` returns `Clamp01(d)`, so a too-low column (true d>1) clamps to exactly 1.0 = deepest usable crouch → the ground itself claimed him. New `CoverSensor.DepthForTopRaw` (unclamped — d≤0 Full / 0..1 Crouch / >1 Low) feeds the probe ring; clamped `DepthForTop` stays for pose-feeding callers. Cataloged: `docs/known_issues/runtime/COVER_DEPTH_SOLVE_CLAMP_HIDES_REJECT.md`. **COVER QUALITY layer now paints the actual gradient** (user: "quality should match the wall 1:1, stepping down until score 0"): `CoverField.Build` splatted a binary class weight (Full=1.0/Crouch=0.7) — a smooth height ramp was structurally impossible in the layer. `qw` is now `1 − 0.5·reqDepth` — Full stays 1.0, the crouch band descends 1.0→0.5 by depth cost (deep duck = worse but never invalid), and the drop to unpainted/0 lands exactly at the Low boundary where the classifier already rejects. The dilated halo still washes visuals only (<0.5 never validates). **CROUCH-DEPTH PIN diagnosed + rig-ease unlatched** (user: "if red = full cover, depth should be 0 not 1"): panel showed `req 0.00` yet `depth 1.00` frozen — two candidate pins found: (a) `CharacterRig`'s debug depth-ease ran UNGATED by `_controllable` — a latched Num5/Alt+C ease fights the possess drive at identical 3f/s rates and pins crouchDepth mid-range; now cancelled the frame the rig loses control (`!_controllable → depthEaseActive=false`), and (b) the Components foldout's `cc.enabled` kill-switch leaves `State` frozen (Update never runs ExitCombat) — a disabled cc reads `InCover` forever as a label. Panel now prints `CC DISABLED — state frozen`, `ts` timescale, and `rig controllable · depth-ease →target` so the next snapshot names the pin directly. **COMBAT PICK resolves the LOCAL wall, not the volume worst-case** (user: non-possess Test Week → Enter Combat, he walks to the red/Full zone but crouches): `CoverSensor.TryPickCover` read class/depth from `volDepth/volClass[volId]` = the deepest column anywhere in the volume; the gradient wall is ONE volume, so the taper's crouch step dictated the pose for a cell behind the full segment. Now takes the nearest non-Low wall of the same volume to the picked cell's stand-spot (aggregates are only the fallback); `TryPickCoverNear` already did this. Known remaining: field LOS validation still uses the volume worst-case eye (`volTop`) — conservative, tracked for Phase B `wallIdxOf`. **Quality scale widened so red = Full only**: the 1.0→0.5 range saturated the heat ramp (a crouch@0.44 column painted 0.78 = red, indistinguishable from Full). Now `SeedSpan 0.8` (Full 1.0 → deepest duck 0.2), halo `HaloDecay 0.15`, validation floor `SeedMin 0.19` (named consts in `CoverField`). **WALL CLASS NO LONGER DEPENDS ON WHERE VINNY STANDS** (user: "vinny's position is changing the cover quality of solid objects"): `CoverSensor.Scan` classified every wall against one `supportY` under the owner's feet — street vs pad shifted all classes 0.1m. Now per-wall local support at the stand-spot (`LocalSupport`, cached 0.1m grid). Cataloged `COVER_CLASS_DEPENDS_ON_OWNER_SUPPORT.md`. **Field paint no longer collapses as cover radius grows** (user: raising radius changes the scores/paint): the field is a fixed 64² grid so `CellSize = 2r/64` (0.25m at r8 → 0.75m at r24 → 2m at r64) while splat discs were a fixed 0.55m, so at coarse sizes each wall hit 0–few cell centres (plus/cross shapes, lost cover) and the 1-cell halo ballooned. Splat radius is now `max(standoff, 0.75·CellSize)` (always ≥1 cell), halo is skipped when CellSize>0.5m, and the panel prints `field rNm · 64² · cell X.XXm · COARSE`. Wall quality values are unchanged by radius; only grid resolution differs. A true constant-resolution field at large radius needs a bigger field texture (FIELD_TEX_RES) — open. **Quality = ONE height scale + nearest-column ownership** (user: "red behind stepped-down cover is wrong; red=Full=stand, lighter as it steps down through the crouch interpolation, until too low"): (1) `CoverField.Splat` let the MAX of overlapping 0.55m discs win, so a tall neighbour painted red in front of a lower step — cells are now owned by the NEAREST wall column, and Low columns claim cells with quality 0; (2) scale is Full 0.85–1.0 (by headroom above standing), Crouch 0.80→0.20 by required depth, Low 0 — Crouch can never read as Full. Build gets `standH` (`HStand`). Geometry caveat: at Vinny's measured heights the crouch band is ONE 0.05m voxel step (rise 0.45–0.62), so the gradient through crouch has a single step; a smoother ramp needs a taller-resolution wall or other character heights. **Possess no longer overwrites the combat duck** (user: "when I enter possess mode depth gets overwritten"): (a) the drive's 0.5m probe ring landed ~6cm short of the wall — combat parks him `standoff+½vox ≈ 0.56m` from the face, so every probe hit bare floor (`best d 9.5 → req 0`); ring radius is now `max(coverProbeRadius, standoff+0.25)`; (b) `CombatController`'s possess-exit zeroed `crouchDepth` — it now preserves the depth across the exit so the drive eases from the real value instead of popping him up. **Possess inherits the combat pose** (user: "combat mode should be dictating all poses" — on G he stood up and aimed): `TryPossess` unconditionally `solver.Arm`ed the gun and the pose picker fell to `Aiming`. Possessing straight out of `InCover` now sets `combatHold` — combat's depth/pose (crouch, or Idle for Full-stand) holds with the gun DOWN until he walks >0.25m from the cover spot, then the wall-driven drive takes over and the IK arms. Also fixed the drive ring being centred on the volume CORNER (`transform.position`) instead of his centre (`WorldCenter`). **Shallow crouch is a crouch** (panel: `pick … rise 0.55 → Crouch d0.00` yet `pose Idle` after possess): pose was chosen by `crouchDepth > 0.02`, so a Crouch-class wall needing depth 0 (shallow already hides him) rendered as standing. Hold now keys off combat's `CoverClass` (`holdCrouch`), the possess drive keys off the probe's class (`ProbeFull` = top−floor > `HStand`, `DriveCrouch`), and the panel's F/C/L letters use the same rule; panel prints `combat hold` instead of the misleading "no threat context". **Crouch depth display disambiguated** (user: depth reads 0.00 both crouched and standing): depth is the shallow(0)↔deep(1) pose-blend axis and is meaningless standing. Panel + crosshair now print `standing (no depth)` vs `crouch SHALLOW (0.00) / blend 0.44 / DEEP (1.00)` (`PossessController.CrouchLabel`). Finding: on the 0.05m-voxel gradient wall deep (1.0) is unreachable — the only wall rises are 0.45(Low)/0.50(d≈0.4)/0.55+(shallow). **Cover pick = closest viable cover** (user doctrine: "quality is informational — use the IMMEDIATE cover; better-cover seeking is an intelligence-gated later pass"): field score changed from `quality × proximity-blend` to `proxWeight·proximity + (1−proxWeight)·quality`, and `proxWeight` default 0.6→0.85 (1 = pure closest, 0 = pure best). Quality stays fully computed/painted — it just no longer dominates the pick. **CoverMetricsPanel GC trim**: profiler's top GC contributors listed the panel — it allocated a `GUIContent` + re-measured on every OnGUI Repaint; content/height are now cached per 5Hz text rebuild. Ambient profile at 120 FPS target: CPU 4.23ms / GPU 3.00ms of an 8.33ms budget, 80 draws, 18.4k tris. **Uniform cover zones + per-column LOS validation** (user: "detected cover should cleanly/uniformly dictate standing or crouching zones; higher cover was getting unfair treatment over the nearer low cover"): (a) disc splats replaced with `SplatRect` — every wall column paints a column-width × `standoff*2` rectangle behind its own face, identical footprint for every class, so "closest viable" compares equal areas and zone edges align with wall steps; (b) cells now store their OWNING column's silhouette (`eyeTopOf`/`eyeFwdOf`) — Full-zone cells must hide a standing eye, crouch cells validate at their own reqDepth. Previously `volTop` fed every cell the volume's WORST-case crouch top, so a Full zone could mark Hidden where standing was actually exposed. `volTop` dictionary removed from CoverSensor. **Zones dictate posture + gradient-depth zones + hug-the-wall picks** (user design: "no quality in the pick; low cover gets a bigger ground area; Vinny proceeds THROUGH the zone to rest against the wall — crouching sooner and staying down as he proceeds"). (a) `proxWeight` default 0.85→1.0 — pure closest-viable, quality contributes nothing to the pick score. (b) Zone depth scales with required duck: `standoff*(2+2·reqDepth)` — Full stays ~1.1m, deep crouch corridors reach ~2.2m out; lower cover literally claims him from further away. (c) Cells now store the owning column's `clsOf`/`reqDepthOf` → new `CoverSensor.TryZoneAt(pos)` returns the zone's posture verdict — used by CombatController (SeekingCover crouch-walks through crouch zones, walks upright elsewhere — "crouch sooner, stay down, arrive at the wall") and PossessController's drive (deepest of ring-hit vs zone-verdict wins; `ZoneCls`/`ZoneD` exposed on the panel). (d) Both pick paths now return the owning column's STAND-SPOT as the destination — he walks through the zone and rests against the wall instead of stopping at the zone's near edge. **Gap-tolerant cover zones** (user: seams in a consistent wall stand Vinny up): once posture reads the painted zone, any unpainted slit (a Low column claims its strip with quality 0; also seams/notches) became "no cover". Fixes: (a) `CoverField.CloseGaps` — unpainted runs <= 0.3m between painted cells (row + column passes) are filled from the less protective neighbour; (b) possess drive dropout latch — with NO ring/zone detection the last crouch holds until he travels 0.35m (distance, not time); a positive Full verdict releases immediately. **Floor relief no longer counts as a wall** (user: oval anomalies in zones — suspected the small steps): `SolidVolumeSet.EmitWalls` measures span from the volume's bottom (ground slab included), so the pit's 1-voxel marker pads / curb / stand plates (0.15m span) passed `wallFloor` 0.15 and became Low wall columns that claim cells at quality 0, punching holes + halos in real zones (marker pads sit 0.05–0.2m in front of the wall face). `CoverSensor.Scan` now skips any column with `spanTop − localSupport < wallFloor` — a wall must stand wallFloor above the floor behind it. Real low walls (e.g. the 0.30m null tail, rise 0.20) still classify Low. **Per-cell cover verdict (dynamic crouch over uneven floor)**: `CoverField` now solves each cell's class / required depth / heat / validation eye against THAT cell's own floor (per-cell ground probe, cached in `floorOf`, reused by the threat pass) instead of inheriting the wall column's single stand-spot verdict. A curb or debris under a cell raises him → rise shrinks → deeper duck (dimmer paint) or unpainted when past the crouch band; a dip can promote Crouch→Full. `TryZoneAt` therefore returns the per-cell depth, so possess drive + combat approach deepen/straighten their crouch over uneven ground automatically; paint and posture stay in agreement. Strip depth still follows the column's own reqDepth. `Build` gained a `depthMargin` param. **Crouch-band heat follows wall rise, not pose depth** (user: a stepped-down wall painted one flat yellow): required depth is 0 across the whole shallow band (rise 0.52–0.62m), so every step in it got the same `0.8−0.6·d` quality. Crouch cells now use `0.2 + 0.6·clamp01((rise − (hDeep+margin)) / (stand − (hDeep+margin)))` — continuous with height, topping out just under Full's 0.85. Pose logic still uses depth (no authored pose exists between standing and shallow crouch). **Cover radius default 8→4m** (`CoverSensor.radius`). **Pick wall math now visible**: `WallMark.support` (floor Y at the wall's stand-spot) + `CoverSensor.PickInfo`, printed on the panel as `pick: wall top · floor · rise (stand/crouch heights) → class dN` so "wall vs floor" is readable from a snapshot. Compile clean — headless csc, 0 errors. **NEW CANONICAL DOC: `docs/systems/ANIMATION_SYSTEM.md`** (user: "unify these documents for total pipeline truth — animating a crouch walk cycle will be born from these changes"). One end-to-end reference replacing the fragmented coverage: HTML authoring surface (states incl. pseudo-state 5.1, diffed `animParams` export, carriedFields) → consolidated `<name>.json` → runtime file map → the TWO blend mechanisms (timed dual-eval transitions vs the `crouchDepth` parametric axis) → GPU/CPU/JS parity contract + `_InstanceAnimData`/`_InstanceIKData` layout → silhouette `TryMeasureTopY` → the `h(d)` cover contract → gotchas/parity checklist. Pins the **stand→shallow gap** (only a timed transition exists; planned fix = unified `duck∈[0,2]` axis reusing the dual-eval machinery) and specs the **crouch-walk state contract** it births (body = `Cr` scalars at depth, limbs = keyframed gait, worst-phase silhouette measurement, triple-port parity). `DOCUMENTATION_INDEX.md` gained the entry + keywords; `VOXEL_GROUP_ANIMATION.md` + `Assets/docs/systems/CHARACTER_ANIMATION_PIPELINE.md` marked superseded-by banners (both predate crouchDepth/transitions/RequestPose).

- **LOCKED/UNLOCKED HUD architecture — the 40fps tab cluster solved** (user design: "lock → 120fps on any tab, unlock to tune, lock back to verify"). Root cause chain, instrumented end-to-end: `hud` self-profiler showed OnGUI script cost ≈ **1ms/frame at 44fps** — the C# widget eval was never the wall; the cost is the **native IMGUI draw pass** (mesh/text playback billed to EditorLoop, scales with drawn content, dies when minimized). Fix = the architecture the user proposed, implemented correctly: **locked tabs render as ONE cached label rebuilt ~4Hz** (the pattern the text tabs already prove at 120fps). Live read-only state mirrors per tab — Combat/Cover/Vision/TanSuit/Demolitions/Sfx/MatRes/Clothing each dump their key state (component enabled flags, tuning values, scan stats, det%, picks, duel mode) so changes are **verifiable at full framerate while locked** — read-only `GetComponent`/`.Instance` paths, so locked mode can't spawn singletons or drive scans. `LOCKED`/`edit` button on the sections row + **[L] hotkey**; defaults locked. Instrumentation kept: `hud Xms/frame · N ev · peak` line in the sections row, plus `lite` suppression toggle for future native-pass A/Bs.
- **PERF: HUD section foldouts — widget-count control for the 40fps tab cluster** (user report: SFX/Clothing/Combat/Cover/TanSuit/Demolitions/MatRes = 40fps, BuildLog 100, text tabs 120). Per the `IMGUI_HUD_FRAME_TAX` doc, the cost is **widgets × GUI events** (4–6+/frame) — the slow tabs are exactly the live-interactive ones the original fix left uncached (Combat ~150+, Clothing ~100 dynamic rig/instance buttons, MatRes ~60 table rows). New `SecFold(key,title)` = persistent per-session foldout headers; a collapsed section costs 1 toggle instead of N controls. Wrapped every heavy section: Combat (lots/picks/target/status/combat/aim/fire/tuning), Cover (threat/detect/viz/scan), TanSuit (components/cover/vision/combat), Demolitions (picks/damage/bench — collapsing bench also kills its 3 unconditional extent beams), SFX (shot/glass/waves), Clothing (the two ~53-button selector rows), MatRes (table/ops/blast). Global `open all`/`close all` buttons under the tab rows; sections default open (UX unchanged) until the user collapses. **Companion fixes in the same pass**: (a) `TanSuitRef` — Tan Suit resolved at most 1/sec; `DrawCombatTab` called `FindCharacter` → `FindObjectsByType<CharacterRig>` **3× per event** = ~15 full scene scans/frame while open. (b) `ResolveSfxListener` — the null-retry path was still an unbounded `FindFirstObjectByType` per event when no listener exists; now retry-gated 1s. (c) Combat's target-source block restructured: `hasPos`/`hasTgt`/`tgtWorld` resolve unconditionally (fire controls read them) — only the *display* folds. Headless csc: clean.
- **FIX: SFX tab open = instant 40fps cap** — `DrawSfxTab` ran `FindFirstObjectByType<AudioListener>()` **inline on every OnGUI event** (~2-4+ events/frame → a full scene scan each) — the documented "8 scans per IMGUI pass" bug class, unfixed in this one spot. Listener now cached on the existing 1s `clothingListRefreshTimer` cadence + resolved once lazily when the cache is empty. Companion: `SfxWaves.showWaves` defaulted TRUE — every emitted report wave = 112 `SetCustomBeam`/frame (same beam-flood class as the ring/cone regression) — flipped default off; the SFX tab toggle opts in.
- **REGRESSION SOLVED: debug beam emission flood was the 120→40 wall** (confirmed: FPS 120 / 7.81ms at scale 1.00 with all emits off). Root cause = `PathDebugRenderer` emits every beam segment as `CommandBuffer.DrawMeshInstanced` into the voxel RT — per-frame submission of ~1.3k tiny instanced draws (the Stats "Batched Draw Calls" tell), resolution-independent — which is why R did nothing and `WaitForGPU` read 80% while "GPU Time" said 3ms. Tan Suit's emitters were the invisible half — HUD tabs resolve Vinny only. Cataloged: `docs/known_issues/runtime/PERF_DEBUG_BEAM_EMISSION_FLOOD.md`. Now controllable per-character via TANSUIT + all-off defaults.
- **PERF ISOLATION: all detection debug emits default OFF + new TANSUIT tab** (user: "Tan Suit still had his rings and stuff on" — root cause: COVER/VISION tabs resolve `SpawnedCharacter` only; Tan Suit's own components carried the default-on flags forever). Defaults flipped: `CoverSensor.showRing` (reverts the earlier default-on), `VisionSensor.showCone`, `VisionSensor.showTargetRay` — clean baseline = zero beams/rings/cones until asked. New `Tab.TanSuit` (enum appended — serialized tabs safe; row 2 after Vision) resolves `FindCharacter("Civilian_02")` and exposes per-component `enabled` kills (`CoverSensor`/`VisionSensor`/`CombatController`/`ArmAimSolver` — `enabled=false` stops Update entirely, `OnDisable` already cleans viz+globals), ATTACH buttons when missing, an ALL-VISUALS-OFF quick button, his full detection surface (cover radius/standoff + rescan + all viz toggles + live stats; vision range/half-angle/rise/decay + cone/target-ray/wedge toggles + det%), and his logic (combat state readout, ENTER COMBAT → Vinny or → picked aim, EXIT COMBAT, move/crouch-ease sliders, duel mode toggle — same `pitOpponentAI` flag). Companion fix: `VisionSensor.ConeOwner` static — only the designated sensor pushes the shared `_VisConeA/B` globals (every sensor used to write per-frame, last-writer-wins → the mode-7 cone could follow Tan Suit). Vinny's VISION tab claims ownership when inspected (`if (ConeOwner == null)`); Tan Suit's tab has an explicit "owns V-mode cone globals" toggle; `OnDisable` releases ownership cleanly.
- **PERF A/B lever: `VoxelChunkManager.poseComputeDisabled`** — inspector toggle that gates `useComputePose` per frame: skips the CSClear+CSPose dispatches entirely and binds `sharedVoxelBuffer` (characters render rest-pose). Resolution-independent GPU work isolated without a stash.
- **PERF: ambient cover scan gated on consumers + radius 14→8** (user: "significant degradation"). Profiler showed scripts at 2.4ms — detection was never the wall (GPU-bound: `WaitForGPU` 80%, Unity's "GPU Time" counter doesn't see the custom RT raymarch pass) — but the 0.4s column walk still ran idle. `CoverSensor.Update` now scans only when a consumer exists: armed threat, any viz toggle, cover V-modes 4-6, or the COVER tab open (`sensor.Refresh()` rate-limited drives it while inspected). Combat picks were always demand-driven (`Refresh(true)` before `TryPickCover`) so they're unaffected. The ring got *better* as a side effect — `DrawRing` now paints from the live position every frame instead of lagging at `lastScanPos`; it's a radius indicator, not scan data.
- **V-mode 7 = VISION CONE — the true cone display** (user: wedge "renders underneath" the world). Root cause is a pipeline split, not a setting: `PathDebugRenderer` beams inject into the voxel view's own color RT via CommandBuffer, but the wedge is a normal scene `MeshRenderer` (`Sprites/Default`) — depth-tested against raymarched depth → occluded by every voxel surface. Fix = the shader channel: new `IterVisMode.VisionCone` paints the sight wedge on hit surfaces via `_VisConeA`/`_VisConeB` globals (apex.xz, XZ-normalized dir, cosHalf, range, alpha, enabled) — pushed per-frame by `VisionSensor.PushConeGlobals`, cleared on disable. Angle+range sector test at `worldHit.xz` with a radial edge-fade — shows WHERE the wedge lands; per-target LOS occlusion stays in the sensor's rays by design. V-cycle now mod 8; banner shows `range · ±angle · det%`. Sprite wedge gated behind `showConeFill` (default off) — kept for fallback/debug; rim beams unchanged under `showCone`. Also fixed `eyeFallback` 1.55→0.55 (Vinny-scale). Bonus perf lever in the same pass: `CoverSensor.radius` default 14→8m — a quarter of the disc's column count at Vinny scale (user reported detection scan degradation).
- **Viz defaults: detection ring on by default, vision cone fill → orange.** Ring-vs-cone confusion fixed by color + always-on: `CoverSensor.showRing` defaults true (cyan 28-seg beam circle at the scan radius — it's `AddComponent`'d at runtime so the initializer applies), and the `VisionSensor` wedge fill is a constant orange `(1, .55, .1, .16)` — the rim beams still carry the idle→detecting→seen alertness progression.
- **PHASE A LIVE: cover classification rebuilt on measured silhouette-top `h(d)`** (`COVER_HEIGHTFIELD.md`). Eyes are gone from the height path entirely: `VoxelCharacter.TryMeasureTopY(state, depth)` runs each pose group's highest rest voxel through the same `PosePoint` FK chain the renderer uses — `H_stand`/`h₀`/`h₁` are real posed numbers, cached per (state,depth), and re-authoring a pose re-tunes cover automatically. `VoxelCollisionWorld.TryColumnTop(pos, probeUp, probeDown)` down-probes terrain+volumes for the solid column top under the feet — **standing on voxels is additive** (curbs/rubble/crates raise the whole silhouette). `CoverSensor.Scan` now classifies `Covers(w, supportY + HStand)` → Full / `Covers(w, supportY + h₁)` → Crouch / else Low, and `DepthForTop` solves `d = (top − margin − supportY − h₀)/(h₁ − h₀)` on measured heights. Retired constants deleted: `crouchEye`/`standEye`/`EyeOffset`/`EyeOffsetDeep` (fields, proxies, HUD sliders); `volEye`→`volTop` (req-depth silhouette top + fwd lean), `CandEye`→`CandTop`. Vinny-scale re-tunes: `wallFloor` 0.25→0.15m, `depthMargin` 0.10→0.02m, `threatEye` 1.3→0.55m (a Vinny-sized shooter). The eye anchor still feeds `EyeFwd`/`EyeFwdDeep` (the pose's forward lean — LOS probe sits over the toes) and stays the vision sensor's emit point — clean separation of concerns. HUD Detection block now prints `silhouette top: stand H · crouch h₀ → h₁ (+lean)` + `support: Y` with a `FALLBACK (no anim data)` flag via `HeightsMeasured`. Headless csc check: 0 errors in all touched files.
- **SPEC: cover detection rebuild — measured silhouette-top `h(d)`** (`docs/systems/COVER_HEIGHTFIELD.md`). Vinny's canonical height established: **62 vox @ 0.01 = 0.62m** (eye ≈0.54m) — the world is Vinny-scaled, so human-scale constants are the bug class to eliminate. Design: measured `H_stand`/`h₀`/`h₁` (posed model top via FK, cached), `ColumnTopY` ground probe (standing on voxels = additive height), `wallIdxOf` cell bookkeeping (retires the volId pin), Step layer (step-up nav from `colBase`/`colSpan`), digit-atlas height blit painted on surfaces in possess mode. Phases A–D with checkpoints; arena ladder re-scopes to a ~0.35–0.75m band pending measured values.
- **FIX: the "2.0m wall" was really 1.0m — cover-pit generator authored at the wrong voxel scale** (user: "why isnt it transmitting the data that a wall is 'tall'?"). Detection was correct end-to-end — `EnsureColumns` walks every (x,z) column of the registered array and the wall box's column is 20 contiguous voxels — but `gen_cover_pit.py` assumed 0.1m/voxel while the whole city places blocks at `voxelSize=0.05` ("2x-upscaled" convention, forced in `CityMap3D`). 20 vox × 0.05 = exactly the reported `top+1.0m`; every ladder box was half-height (deep 0.4 / mid 0.5 / crouch 0.65 / wall 1.0m). Verified against the raw `.stasset` bytes (column solid y0-19). Fix = doubled `LADDER` heights (16/20/26/40 vox → 0.8/1.0/1.3/2.0m), `voxelSize` field 0.05, comments re-derived; stasset rebuilt + binary-verified (columns 16/20/26/40). No C# changes needed. Cataloged: `docs/known_issues/runtime/COVER_PIT_VOXEL_SCALE_MISMATCH.md` — flags the same 0.1m-assumption risk for `gen_shooting_range.py`/`generate_empty_land`.
- **FIX: Vinny crouched behind the Full wall instead of standing** — stale debug pose hold. Any NumX pose latched before clicking a pick sits at PRIORITY_DEBUG (50) and silently refuses every `PRIORITY_COMBAT` (30) request — including `Walking` on entry (crouch-slides to the cover) and the new `Idle` stand behind Full walls. `EnterCombatCore` now `ReleasePose(-1)`s on entry: a pick means "run the sim fresh". Debug keys pressed after entry still win normally. Compile clean.
- **FIX: debug pose keys dead during combat** (user: "im behind the mid wall trying to set poses and it wont let me override"). `EnterCombat` set `rig.Controllable = false`, and `CharacterRig.HandleInput` early-outs on that flag — the entire pose panel was offline exactly when combat inspection matters. The rig only ever read pose keys (no locomotion), and `PRIORITY_DEBUG` already outranks `PRIORITY_COMBAT`, so the disable was purely self-inflicted. Removed the suppress/restore; pose keys now work mid-combat (a debug pose at pri-50 cleanly overrides the pri-30 combat hold). Also: `CharacterAnimation.DebugOwnsPose` gates `CombatController`'s per-frame depth ease — a debug-held pose owns the crouch-depth channel too, so `Num5`-deep doesn't get dragged back to the wall's `reqDepth` until the hold releases. Compile clean — 78 srcs, 0 errors.
- **FIX: every pit pick resolved to deep crouch + Full walls still crouched** (two defects, one seam). (a) `CoverField.Splat` claims each cell for the *highest-quality* wall and ties go to whichever splatted first — adjacent boxes' discs overlap, so cells nearest `cover_mid`/`cover_crouch` were owned by `cover_deep` → `volDepth` reported 1.0 for neighbors. `TryPickCoverNear` now resolves the *wall* by proximity first (nearest non-Low `facePos`), reads its `volId`/`reqDepth`/`cls` directly, then pins `PickNear` to that volume's Hidden cells (`requireVol`). (b) `CrouchAndCover` unconditionally crouched — a Full wall's reqDepth 0 still produced a shallow pose. New `volClass` map + class reported by both pick paths; `CombatController.coverWallClass` drives stand-vs-duck: Full → `RequestPose(Idle)` (stands hidden), Crouch → ducked at reqDepth. Cataloged: `docs/known_issues/runtime/COVER_PICK_SPLAT_OVERLAP_WRONG_VOLUME.md`. Compile clean — 78 srcs, 0 errors.
- **FIX: in-game deep crouch rendered upright — IK torso assist was erasing the authored lean** (user diagnosis: "turning off IK fixed it"). `CrouchAndAim` armed `ArmAimSolver` on cover arrival; torso assist writes a pure-yaw `ikOverrides[0]` every frame and `ApplyIKOverride` nlerp-*replaces* `bodyRot` — the 0.67-rad crouch lean was wiped while legs kept their bends. Design gate per doctrine **crouched = taking cover = not aiming**: solver `Arm()` rejects while `EffectiveState == Crouching` ("cannot aim while crouched (in cover)"), `Update()` drops all IK channels instantly on crouch entry (`ReleaseInstant`, shared with blend-out teardown); `CombatController` → `CrouchAndCover()` no longer arms on arrival/hold (keeps yaw-face for the future pop-up). Aiming moves to the planned exposed phase (peek → ≤6 shots → duck → reload). Cataloged: `docs/known_issues/runtime/IK_TORSO_OVERRIDE_STOMPS_CROUCH_LEAN.md`. Compile clean — 78 srcs, 0 errors.
- **FIX: pit pick row was invisible on the normal path** — the buttons sat inside `!clickedPit` in TEST LOTS, which is false exactly when the pit block is clicked (the standard entry). Extracted `PitCoverPickRow` and rendered it unconditionally in TEST LOTS AND in the clicked-pit combat branch (after START DUEL / EXIT COMBAT). Compile clean.
- **Cover pit rebuilt as a 4-box depth ladder** (user spec — test each duck band deliberately). The two mirrored 3-crate clusters are replaced by ONE shared row mid-lot (z92-99), ascending tops: `cover_deep` 0.8m (reqDepth ~1.0) / `cover_mid` 1.0m (~0.5) / `cover_crouch` 1.3m (0) / `cover_wall` 2.0m (Full, no duck). Dark-wood lip on both faces — either fighter can use either side; marker pads south+north carry 8 pick anchors (`cover_*` / `cover_*_n`). gen_cover_pit.py updated + stasset rebuilt (10 attachmentPoints in tail).
- **Pick-a-box path** — `CoverField.PickNear(anchor, 2.5m)` = nearest Hidden cell to the anchor (same validated field, not a teleport); `CoverSensor.TryPickCoverNear` reports reqDepth; `CombatController.EnterCombat(threat, anchor)` overload via shared `EnterCombatCore` — anchor-miss returns false with "no valid cover near that anchor" instead of silently holding. `LastResult` now reports `depth N.NN` on every pick.
- **HUD**: TEST LOTS gains a `deep / mid / crouch / wall` button row — resolves the anchor via `TryGetBuildingPointLocal`, keys threat on Tan's live feet, works mid-combat for A/B sweeps. COMBAT_COVER_STATE.md updated. Compile clean.
- **Num5 toggles shallow <-> deep crouch** (debug pose panel): first press enters crouch at depth 0 as before; pressing again WHILE crouched flips `crouchDepth` target 0 <-> 1. Depth DRIFTS at 3/s (CombatController's settle rate) — state stays Crouching so no pose transition fires; the ease owns the channel only until reached, then releases so combat depth targeting isn't contested (any non-crouch pose or release also frees it immediately). Mid-ease presses reverse the target. `Alt+C` shares the toggle. Keys tab updated. Compile clean.
- **Depth axis joins the transition snapshot** (bug: Crouching <-> Crouch Deep snapped instead of morphing — user diagnosis was exactly right, "the depth is snapping"). The freeze captured (state, time) but `crouchDepth` stayed a live global, so when a state change also moved depth (5 -> 5.1 pins depth 0 -> 1) the frozen A-eval re-rendered at the NEW depth = both blend ends identical = invisible transition. `transDepthA` now freezes with the pair: HTML `posedPosTransition` swaps `crouchDepth` alongside state/time for the A-eval; C# `CharacterAnimation.transFromDepth` -> `InstancedCharacter.transDepthA` -> `animData[i*3+2].y` (free slot); compute kernel A-eval reads `.y`; CPU `PosePoint`/`PoseToWorld`/`TransParams` carry it (sentinel -1 = fall back to live depth for hypothetical evals). Compile clean — 78 srcs, 0 errors.
- **Civilian1.json gains its deep-crouch endpoint** (user-authored — "Deep Crouch.json" export had the deep pose written into `crouching` because the tool had no second destination): surgically merged the authored block into `animParams.crouchDeep` — Civilian1 now carries BOTH endpoints (`crouching` modelLower 11 = shallow; `crouchDeep` modelLower 14.5, bodyLean 0.67, headPitch -0.26, elbow tucks 1.23/0.84 = the authored deep duck). Temp export file safe to delete.
- **"Crouch Deep" in the animator's state library** (user ask — a separate authoring destination so deep poses never overwrite `crouching` again): new `STATES` entry id **5.1** — inside the existing `>4.5 && <5.5` crouch band in every eval (HTML/C#/both shaders) so it poses as crouching for free; `selectState` pins `crouchDepth` (5->0, 5.1->1) so each button shows its own endpoint, and the Crouching param panel + depth slider respond to the band check. Workflow: click Crouch Deep -> tune the DEEP rows while viewing the deep pose -> export writes `crouchDeep` into the character file.
- **Adaptive crouch depth — cover height picks the duck** (user design — "a crouch that over-corrects for a deeper duck if the cover is lower; a range of 'short cover' inside a threshold"): crouch is now a *third pose-space axis* — `crouchDepth` 0..1 lerps every authored scalar `crouching -> crouchDeep` inside the pose eval, so the author ships two static endpoints and the engine owns the range. `AnimParamsData.crouchDeep` (same `CrouchingData` shape) + `Missing()` fill clones the shallow block when a .anim.json predates it = depth inert on old assets. GPU: `_InstanceAnimData` stride 2->3 float4s (3rd = crouchDepth), `_AnimStaticParams` 15->19 (`[15]` deep bodyLean/headPitch/armSwings, `[16]` deep legStride/kneeBend, `[17]` = deep bodyLower/modelLower + shallow elbow pair, `[18]` = deep elbow pair); both pose evals (CharacterPoseCompute kernels AND VoxelProxyRaymarch inverse-transform frag path) lerp at the crouch reads — and the proxy picked up the crouch-elbow branch it never had (CPU/GPU parity). `_InstanceOffsets` anim half `.w` carries depth for the inline path. CPU: `ComputeGroupRotation`/`PosePointCore`/`PosePoint` take `crouchDepth`; `TryGetJointWorld`/`TryGetEyeAnchor` gain `animDepthOverride` (hypothetical evals default 0, live queries inherit `ic.crouchDepth`); `WeaponMount` muzzle solve passes live depth. `CharacterAnimation.crouchDepth` public field -> handle each frame.
- **CoverSensor solves depth per wall**: `EyeOffsetDeep`/`EyeFwdDeep` = posed painted-eye anchor evaluated at depth 1 — the usable-cover floor is now *authored anatomy* (walls whose top clears the deepest eye are duckable; below it = genuinely unusable). `QueryCoverWalls` open-side band widened to the deep eye; `WallMark.reqDepth = DepthForTop(spanTop)` = `(top - depthMargin(0.1m) - baseY - EyeOffset)/(EyeOffsetDeep - EyeOffset)` clamped 0..1 — linear because the anchor lerps like the pose. `CoverField.Build` takes `volEye` (volId -> eye height+forward at that volume's required depth) so the threat->eye LOS ray validates each cell at *its* wall's actual duck — a low wall only passes if the deep-crouched eye clears it. `TryPickCover` gains `out float reqDepth` (max depth over the picked volume's walls).
- **CombatController eases into the duck**: picks store `targetCrouchDepth`, `Update` `MoveTowards` it at `crouchDepthSpeed` (default 3/s ~0.33s to full) — the settle into a deeper duck rides the same eval, no snap; `ExitCombat` resets to 0. HoldPosition (no cover) targets 0 = shallow.
- **Authoring + preview**: `character_animator.html` — `crouchDeep` default block (deeper modelLower 8, thigh/knee/lean authored), all 11 crouch reads go through `cr()` depth-lerp, Crouching state shows SHALLOW rows + "DEEP "-prefixed endpoint rows + a **Crouch Depth** preview slider; both import paths clone shallow->deep for legacy files (Unity parity). `DebugHUDManager` Clothing tab adds a **crouch depth** slider under Pose transition (Num5 + drag = live preview). headless csc clean.
- **Cover pit testbed lot** (user design — "double-sided firing range; Vinny on one side, Tan Suit opposite, two low wooden cover boxes between them; phase-0 win = Vinny moves for low cover and aims"): new authored asset `voxel_buildings/cover_pit.stasset` (192×48×192 via `Tools/gen_cover_pit.py` → `json_to_stasset.py` — same pipeline as the range). Contents: asphalt pad + stone curb, **two mirrored 3-crate clusters** (short crouch-class crate flanked by two tall full-class crates — user layout rev2 exercises both cover tiers), dark-wood lip strips on each crate's stand-facing side ("the side you hide behind"), and painted attachment points `cover_stand_a` / `cover_stand_b` straight across the lot centerline (layout rev3 per user render — straight rows, centered stands, 2.0m nav gaps; earlier revs were diagonal/staggered), resolved through `TryGetBuildingPointLocal`. `firing_position`/`firing_target` aliases added (range's own lane-alias trick) so the **Target Practice order treats the pit as a first-class destination** — skip-walk spawns Vinny on stand_a facing stand_b.
- **Symmetric duel (phase-1 spec — "both stand idle at plates, guns at side; on START both advance to their low cover")**: `SETUP PIT` now exits any live combat on both rigs and forces `Idle` pose before teleporting (deterministic re-runs). `START DUEL` always runs Vinny's `EnterCombat` keyed to Tan's live feet; an **opponent toggle** (`target dummy` default / `AI duel`) decides whether Tan fights back — user request: Tan starts as a passive dummy while Vinny's cover+aim is dialed in, and flipping to AI duel makes both advance to their own cover, crouch, and aim across the lot. Dummy mode stands down any live combat on Tan. `Civilian_02` receives the combat stack (`CoverSensor`+`ArmAimSolver`+`CombatController`) at spawn via new `CityMap3D.AttachCombatStackWhenReady` — the Gotcha-#10 canonical-attach rule applied to a second rig; `VisionSensor` remains Vinny-only. `city_layout.json`: `block_45` (all-empty land adjacent to the shooting-range block) is now "Central Block 45 [COVER TESTBED]" hosting the lot; `cover_pit` building_type added.
- **Combat tab pit branch**: `HandleBlockClicked` now opens the Combat tab on any block carrying `cover_stand_a` (not just `firing_position`). Pit blocks replace the lane selector with a **SETUP PIT** button — Vinny teleports to stand A facing B via `EventPlayer.DebugSnapTo` (same path as range lanes, with a down-raycast feetY), Civilian_02 snaps to stand B facing A via the new `SnapCharacterTo` helper (generic corner-offset teleport for non-EventPlayer rigs), and the orange threat cross arms on Tan's feet so `CoverThreat`/`CoverExposure` V-modes go live pre-duel. In-position readout for Tan mirrors the existing Vinny check. The ENTER COMBAT button becomes **START DUEL** on pit blocks: snapshots Tan's live feet position (sensor threat semantics = ground point; `threatEye` lifts it to chest) and runs the shared `cc.EnterCombat` path.
- **Cover field → V-key vision mode** (from prior session, same session's theme): the field bakes into a packed `Texture2D` (R=score G=quality B=verdict A=proximity) pushed as shader globals; `VoxelProxyRaymarch.shader` `VOXEL_DEBUG_VIEW` samples it at `worldHit.xz` so heat paints the voxels themselves. V cycle: IterationHeat → StepChannels → MissWaste → **CoverQuality** (passive, works threat-free) → **CoverThreat** (composite + white pick flash) → **CoverExposure** (teal/red/slate verdict map) → off. Persistent bottom-center banner names the mode + live stats. All beam-type cover viz (ring/footprints/wall bars/candidate posts/LOS rays/ground quad) defaults off — the field is the single visual language; beams remain as deep-debug toggles.
- **Gotcha #10 documented** (`docs/systems/VOXEL_ENGINE_GOTCHAS.md`): tabs inspect, don't construct — runtime components attach at canonical spawn (`WaitForCharacterSpawn`), dependencies Get-or-Add or fail loudly. Audit found a latent crash (`CombatController.solver.Arm()` unguarded after Get-only) — now Get-or-Add.
- **Gotcha #11 documented**: `out var` inside `&&`-chained `TryGet` calls stays unassigned on short-circuit — split the calls when the result is branched on later (hit live in the pit detection code, fixed before compile).
- **"Selection ceremony" anti-pattern flushed** (user design call — second recurrence of mode-gated tooling): every `SimState.Dwelling` check that gated *tool actions* removed — `HandleBlockClicked` opens testbed Combat tabs on anchor blocks in ANY sim state, `MoveCharacterToLane` lane-snaps anytime, IK AIM/fire controls gate on a resolved target context (`freeFire || hasPos`) instead of dwell. Rule recorded in `docs/systems/TEST_WEEK.md`: gate on missing *things* not *modes*; disabled controls must state their reason; tools self-resolve context (anchor-probe discovery). `Dwelling` stays a sim concept — the Test Week hold-open — never a debug key.
- **Pose transitions = parametric snapshot→target engine** (user design — "first pose is whatever we set it to, final is the authored pose"; replaces hard-cut state changes): on every effective-state change `CharacterAnimation` freezes the outgoing pose as (state, frozen animTime) and uploads a dual-eval descriptor — `transBlend` rides `animData.w`, `(stateA, timeA, dip)` rides the free `.yzw` of the posed-offset float4 (no buffer growth). `CharacterPoseCompute` extracts its per-voxel eval into `EvalPosedPos` and, while 0<blend<1, evaluates BOTH poses and lerps voxel positions — no quaternion-chain surgery, works from ANY source state incl. T-Pose (bind = empty eval) and mid-walk (frozen cycle phase). `transDip = -sin(pi·t)·amp` adds the bob-flavored settle (peaks mid-transition, recovers at end). CPU parity: `PosePoint`/`PoseToWorld` dual-eval + slerp so eye-anchor/muzzle queries stay truthful mid-transition (hypothetical `animStateOverride` evals deliberately skip blending). 4 knobs on `CharacterAnimation` (enable/duration/dip/ease) + live sliders in Clothing tab under the rig selector. v1 limits: proxy-raymarch DDA eval and CPU `PoseVoxels` mesh path stay unblended (fallback paths only). headless csc clean (all Assets/Scripts).

- **Numpad = pose panel** (user request — always-available pose override without the Windows menu-beep): Alt+letter dings because it is the OS menu-accelerator chord (WM_SYSCHAR -> no mnemonic -> DefWindowProc beep) — unconsumable from managed code. Numpad digits carry no menu semantics and no camera/WASD conflict, so the same 11 states now map to a physical pad grid: 7/8/9 = T-Pose/Down/Sit, 4/5/6 = Aim/Crouch/Flinch, 1/2/3 = Walk/Look/AimWalk, Num0/NumEnter = release, Num. = AimSweep; Num+/- keep anim speed. Same SetState PRIORITY_DEBUG latch — poses hold over combat/locomotion until released. Keys tab shows the grid. FollowCamera FOV bindings removed entirely (user never used them — =/-/Num+/Num- were double-firing anim speed AND camera FOV; the pose panel now owns +/- exclusively).

- **Alt+pose hotkeys had no receiver** (user hit Alt+C → Windows menu beep, Vinny stayed Idle): `CharacterRig.HandleInput` gates on `_controllable && animHotkeys`, but `animHotkeys` was an Inspector-only serialize field defaulting false — nothing in code ever set it, so the advertised "rig selected in Clothing tab" path was dead (selector set `Controllable` only). Fixed: public `AnimHotkeys` property; primary rig gets it true at spawn (`CityMap3D` — Vinny answers Alt+pose out of the box); Clothing-tab selection now transfers it (`AnimHotkeys=false` on deselected rigs) so poses can target Tan too. Possess path unchanged — `Controllable=false` while possessing still gates rig input, so no double-handling.
- **Short crates were silently non-cover** (user caught it in Exposure mode — tall crates verdict-painted, shorts showed only slate wash): the 0.8m crate on the 0.2m pad tops at 1.0m — the posed crouch-eye band resolves ~1.0m and `Covers()` requires `eye < spanTop` → boundary miss → `WallClass.Low` → no splat → no cells → nothing to verdict. Raised `SHORT_H` 10→13 (1.1m body, 1.3m column top — clears the band, stays under `standEye` 1.35m → still Crouch-class). Rule captured in COMBAT_COVER_STATE: author cover ≥0.2m over the eye band, not marginally.
- **Pit setup de-gated → always-on TEST LOTS menu** (user hit "state lock" — SETUP PIT silently no-op'd outside `SimState.Dwelling`, was `GUI.enabled=dwelling`-greyed, and only existed after clicking the pit block *during* dwell): `SetupCoverPit` drops the dwelling check entirely (debug teleport, works any sim state) and logs loudly on failures instead of silent returns. New **TEST LOTS** card at the top of the Combat tab — the pit is *discovered* by probing every laid block's attachment points for `cover_stand_a` (`PitBlockId`, cached, re-derived on city rebuild) — SETUP PIT + opponent toggle + START DUEL + Tan status all work with zero ceremony: no order, no dwell, no block click. Clicked-pit branch keeps only fire-aim resolution + a "controls in TEST LOTS" pointer; its combat-section START DUEL dedupes against the card. `FindCharacter` helper — CharacterRig path with raw `GameObject.Find`+`GetComponent` fallback (a rig without a `CharacterRig` no longer silently skips the Tan snap). `StartPitDuel` extracted — TEST LOTS card and clicked-pit combat section share one duel path.
- **Cover-field viz → multi-agent union** (user-reported bug + design call): with Tan Suit carrying a `CoverSensor` (symmetric duel stack), every sensor was racing on a single `_CoverFieldTex`/`_CoverFieldParams` shader global — last-scan-wins made the disc **flicker between Vinny's and Tan's fields**, and the radius slider looked dead (half the frames were the other sensor's fixed radius). Fixed per user direction — "2 lights overlapping, same data, no competing": all fields now upload into a shared `Texture2DArray` (`_CoverFieldTexArr`, 4 slots, one slice per contributing sensor) + per-source `_CoverFieldParamsArr`; the proxy shader **unions** every slice at each hit's world XZ. Eclipse rule: strongest/verdict-priority sample wins (mode 4 → max quality; modes 5/6 → chosen > hidden > exposed > invalid), untested disc-edge wash survives via max-alpha. Consequences: **no flicker** (sources never touch each other's slot), Vinny's radius slider resizes only his disc while Tan's persists, and in AI-duel mode **both agents' verdict fields and white picks can paint simultaneously** — duel debugging sees both decisions. Slot released OnDisable; >4 contributors simulate but skip paint (warned once). Contributors must run `fieldRes=64`.
- **`target_practice` → `test_week` rename** (user call — the order outgrew the range and became the official debug-harness entry point): orderType string, button label ("Test Week"), serialized fields (`testWeekDwells`/`testWeekSkipWalk` with `FormerlySerializedAs`), sim hold/resolve logic, and all comments renamed. Concept captured in new `docs/systems/TEST_WEEK.md` — the order is the door; authored attachment points route each clicked block to its testbed UI (range lanes, cover pit, debris lot). Compile clean.
- Compile clean (Unity 6000.3.18f1 batchmode, 0 errors). Pit runtime validation pending.

---

### Damage-gradient char tint — blast wounds bake scorch into the voxels themselves
- **The feature** (user design): survivors adjacent to doomed cells get darker toward the wound — "damage" as a gradient, not new materials. Implemented as a 4-bit field in the voxel word's **runtime-only bits 16-19** (`VoxelDamageMath.DamageShift/Mask`): `0` clean · `1-7` char (darken) · `8-15` glint (lighten, reserved for crack webbing). Bits 16+ were chosen over the on-disk reserved region (9-15 = shape/rotation) deliberately — the u16 `.stasset` format can never reach them, so tint can't collide with authored data and never serializes into an asset.
- **`ApplyDamageGradient`**: multi-source 6-connected BFS seeded by the just-removed cells; ring r gets level `depth-r+1` (darkest at the wound, fading over `charDepth` cells); max-merge so overlapping blasts only deepen. `keepFrac` hash-dither for sparse webbing; dithered cells still propagate the front. Called in `Detonate` (after `Blast`+`FindDetached`) and `ApplyPlanSpan` (flash span) — tint writes ride the same dirty-span upload.
- **Whole-lifecycle tint** (user requirement — settlement must look natural): detached cells are tinted *before* `SeedFalling` snapshots `vox`, so in-grid rain carries char; flyers get a hash-dithered `debrisChar` nibble at emission (pair-anchored hash → domino debris scorches uniformly) which `AddFlyer` folds into the draw color; `fly.vox` handoff writes the tinted word into rubble. Grid → rain → flyer → rubble: one continuous tint.
- **Shader**: `(packed >> 16) & 0xF` after the palette+chunk-tint lookup in `VoxelProxyRaymarch.shader` AND `MobSimVoxelRaymarch.compute`; `1-7` multiplies toward black by `_DamageChar`, `8-15` lerps toward white by `_DamageGlint` (globals pushed per-frame from the VDS fields). Tints `baseColor` before the glass gate → glinted glass reads frosted for free.
- **Radial falloff** (user refinement — "char should fade from the epicenter, not stencil uniformly"): `ApplyDamageGradient` accepts a blast-center + radius + `radial` strength; each front cell's level scales by `1 - radial·min(dist/radius, 1)` — near-epicenter wound edges char hardest, fading progressively to nothing at the blast rim (a far wound-adjacent cell reads "barely damaged," and far faces of thick solids that only catch bleed-through rings go ~clean). `EmitFlyersRadial` applies the same scale to debris levels — near-center debris flies darkest. New slider `radial char fade` (default 0.8; 0 = the previous uniform-ring look).
- **Knobs** (Range → Demolition): `damage tint` toggle, `char depth` (1-8 cells, default 4), `char strength` (0.65), `radial char fade` (0.8), `debris char` (0.6 = 60% of ejected cells charred, levels scale 1-7 by blast proximity). Glass shots emit glint debris.
- **Selftest `[21]`** (7 sub-cases): ring levels, material preservation, max-merge (weak pass can't repaint, strong deepens), glint mode, **air-moat blocking** (char never air-hops), dither propagation (skipped cells still carry the front to depth), corner-seed bounds (3 shells = 19 cells), nibble round-trip through reserved bits. **ALL PASS (155,703 checks)**, headless csc clean.
- **Bullet impact decals** (user request — wound-adjacency applied to firearms): `OnShotResolved` no longer drops non-glass hits — `ApplyImpactTint` writes the hit cell a max char "pit" (level 6-7, seed-varied), then seeds it into the same `ApplyDamageGradient` BFS so a faint dithered halo rings it (`bulletScorchDepth` 2, `bulletScorchKeep` 0.75 for ragged spall). **No removal — the wall keeps its voxel; the scar is pure tint.** Glass still punctures/cracks exactly as before. Knob: `bullet impact decals` toggle + `scorch depth` under the damage-tint block. Selftest `[21](j)` covers the survives-seed pattern (core keeps 7, halo rings 2→1→0).
- **Blast char → exposure field** (user refinement — "stronger gradient from both ends, S-curve not linear; distance + facing + path absorption; windows transmit to sills"): blasts switched from the surface BFS to `ApplyDamageExposure` — each solid cell within `radius + charReach` computes `level = round(7 · (1 − charRadial·smoothstep(dist/range)) · charAbsorb^solids)`. `smoothstep` gives the S-curve (near-max hold at the origin, steep taper at the rim); `MarchSolidCount` counts intervening voxels on the straight blast→cell ray (uniform per-cell cost — **material resistance deliberately excluded**, exposure is geometry). Emergent for free: facing surfaces char, shadowed/back sides stay clean, depth-into-material decays by absorption, and **air transmits** — a blown opening scorches what it reveals (selftest `[22](b)`: cell behind an opening chars harder than behind solid wall at equal distance). New slider `char absorb/cell` 0.62; `charDepth` renamed `charReach` (it's now a margin past the doom zone); `charRadial` now drives the S-curve strength (0 = absorption-only). Bullet decals keep the BFS — contact marks ≠ radiated energy. Selftest `[22]` 6 sub-cases; **ALL PASS (155,728)**, csc clean.
- **Data note**: all 36 `.stasset` files scanned — zero upper bits set; `VxShape`/`VxRotation` are dead helpers in both shaders. The format reservation stands documented; runtime bits 16-19 sidestep it entirely.
- **Glass glint mode** (opt-in A/B — retires the cracked-material swap): `TouchGlass` survivors can now keep their **own glass material** and carry the fracture as a bright damage nibble (glint 10-13, hash-varied, max-merged so re-shots only brighten). `Puncture` gained a `glintMode` param driven by VDS's `glassGlint` field (default **false** — classic 134 halo remains until the look is validated). Pane bookkeeping needs no change: `UploadVoxelEdits` keys on the dirty cell's *current* material, so glinted survivors (still material 112) get the shared damage-pane id and render as their own frosted surface. HUD toggle `glass glint (retire cracked material)` under the tint block. Selftest `[22](g)`: 0%-removal 3×3 pane puncture → 1 AIR + all 8 ring cells glint 10-13 keeping material 112, zero material-134 writes. **ALL PASS (155,732)**, csc clean. If the glint scar wins the eyeball test, material 134 can retire and `crackedMaterial` becomes a fallback constant.
- **HUD separation-of-concerns pass** (user report — "damage and shooting shouldn't depend on demolition"): the Range tab's shared damage/debris controls were gated behind `pickedBlast` — invisible until a blast target was picked. Hoisted into an always-visible **Damage &amp; debris** section: `damage tint` + all children (char reach/strength/S-curve/absorb, debris char, `glass glint` + a new `glint strength` slider, bullet decals + scorch depth), `debris collides with voxel world`, `same-material pairing` + chance (shots pair too — `EmitFlyersShot` reads `pairCells`), `debris settles into volume`, and the RESET DAMAGE button. **Demolition** keeps only blast-scoped controls: geometry sliders, occlusion, collapse, shock front, `debris radial launch velocity` (blast-only — `EmitFlyersShot` launches along `shotDir`), and DETONATE. New `<b>Free-fire &amp; picks</b>` header marks the pick row section. csc clean.
- **Render tab scale readout**: `Resolution: WxH` now also shows `scale X.XX ([R] cycles 0.5/0.65/0.75/1.0)` — RT size is GameView×camera-rect×scale, so the line now diagnoses "small window vs low scale" at a glance. DynRes is hard-locked at 1.0 in Awake (committed since Aug — confirmed it was NOT a regression).
- **`DevBuild.cs` (Steel City → Build Dev Player)**: one-click `BuildOptions.Development` player to `Build/Dev/` — needed because the editor profiler can't answer perf claims (URP GPU markers unsupported; `UpdateScene`/`WaitForLastPresentation` noise). Investigating Demolitions/MatRes 40fps plateaus vs Combat's 120: plateau frames are CPU-blocked on `GfxDeviceD3D12.WaitForGPU` (~20ms) with scripts at ~2ms — looks like editor repaint overhead, not game cost. The dev build test decides whether it's real.
- **BuildLog tab + `BuildDiagnostics`** (user request — "a tab that shows everything the build sees"): new `Assets/Scripts/Sim/BuildDiagnostics.cs` — a 256-line ring-buffer event log + ordered key/value state table, always-on in editor AND player. `Application.logMessageReceived` capture routes the **entire Unity log stream** into the ring (warnings/errors type-tagged), so in a dev build the BuildLog tab replaces digging through `Player.log`. Instrumented: VCM Awake (device/RPCaps/shader+kernel resolution), `InitProxyRender` (which load path resolved each shader), `RegisterSector`, `sectorMaterial` keyword state, `RenderChunks` per-frame state (path taken, RT dims, camera rect/enabled/mask/target, content counts, proxyList + sectors-drawn, skip reason), `VoxelRenderBridge` (callback firing, overlay active, texture dims), `CityMap3D.Awake`/`GameUIController.Start` milestones. New `Tab.BuildLog` on row 2, rendered via the cached-text path (4Hz rebuild — one label per repaint, IMGUI-cheap).
- **COVER tab + `CoverSensor` — cover detection v2, awareness-as-a-sense** (user design — "a mobster always aware of what's around him before a threat exists"): `CoverFinder` retired; new `CoverSensor` component on Vinny runs a periodic passive scan (0.4s interval / 0.4m move) independent of combat state. Detection model is now **column-profile based**: each registered volume lazily builds per-footprint-column `colBase`+`colSpan` (first solid voxel + contiguous run — an open-cockpit canopy floats above the eye band and can't masquerade as cover). `QueryCoverWalls` emits wall columns with an open side; classified Full (covers standing eye) / Crouch / Low. With a threat set (`sensor.Threat` ← shared PICK AIM point), only walls whose open side faces away from the threat generate stand spots (deduped by spot-spacing grid), validated by ground probe → free-space → authoritative threat→crouch-eye LOS ray. New `Tab.Cover` (row 2): PICK THREAT + CLEAR, detection sliders (radius/standoff/crouch eye/full cover/spot spacing/facing dot), per-layer viz toggles (radius ring, local-frame footprints, class-colored wall columns, non-facing dimming, candidate pins, threat→eye LOS rays), live scan stats with reject-reason counts. `CombatController` cover params became sensor proxies; EnterCombat = `sensor.Threat` + force-scan + `TryPickCover`. F8 freeze doubles as inspection mode: WASD-fly/MMB-orbit/RMB-pan/wheel all live while frozen (EventPlayer + possess yield camera authority; WASD deltas were previously swallowed by the frozen follow-lerp). csc clean.
- **Pose-derived eye + `VisionSensor` — the VISION tab sense** (user design — "the pose is the source of truth for perception; the Looking animation IS the scan"): `VoxelCharacter.TryGetJointWorld(gid, out pos, out rot, animStateOverride)` pushes any joint pivot through the same FK chain the voxels render with (`animStateOverride` evaluates a pose at t=0 as if in that state — `JointHead = 1`). `CoverSensor`'s eye band now reads the **posed crouch head height** every scan — the `crouchEye` slider demoted to fallback; Cover tab shows the live source (`eye band: X.XXm — read from posed crouch head`). Re-authoring the crouch (modelLower etc.) auto-tracks the band — detection can't disagree with the rendered silhouette. New `VisionSensor` component: live eye+forward from the posed head joint (cone physically sweeps with the Looking state's head yaw — scan animation = mechanic), perception = range + yaw half-angle + `VoxelCollisionWorld.Raycast` LOS (same occlusion law as cover — hidden from bullets = hidden from gaze), detection accumulates 0..1 while in-cone (riseTime) and decays outside (decayTime) → sweep gaps produce intermittent exposure windows. New `Tab.Vision` (row 2): PICK TARGET (reuses the shared aim pick as "the thing he's looking at"), range/half-angle/rise/decay sliders, cone + eye→target ray toggles, live detection % status; cone beams color by alertness (pale = watching → amber = detecting → red = detected). csc clean.
- **Selection panel un-buried** (user couldn't find it): the Selection block lived inside the hidden `#adv-panel` (mirror-toggled). Extracted to a standalone `#sel-panel` (ruler-panel pattern) — auto-shows while the Select tool is active OR any selection exists, hides with ui-hidden, and gained a gesture-hint footer (Shift=voxel / Ctrl+drag=box / W+click=region / Q+click=group / click=flood / Alt=add).
- **Vision ground-up rebind — eyes centroid spine + wedge viz (Unity + animator)**:
  - `CharacterAsset.TryGetRegionCentroid(name)` + animator `rebuildRegionIndex` — **bbox midpoint** of the painted span (not mass mean — a mean drifts onto the heavier side; an eyes band 2 voxels tall should sit on the seam). Lazy cache over parsed `Regions`/`RegionDefs`. **Index space both sides** (voxel index = render center, `(min+max)/2`) — an earlier cell-center convention rendered the anchor +0.5 voxels off, so even-width spans sat on a voxel instead of the seam (root cause of the persistent "off center" orb). Status line now prints the span bounds with `.5 = seam · .0 = voxel`.
  - `TryGetEyeAnchor` takes `animStateOverride`/`animTime` (same hypothetical-pose API as `TryGetJointWorld`) → `CoverSensor` now reads the **posed crouch eye anchor** — height (`EyeOffset`) AND forward lean (`EyeFwd`: the crouch's bodyLean + proud offset put eyes in front of the feet axis). Candidate LOS test point = `spot + up*eyeH + dirToThreat*eyeFwd` — the same point the vision cone emits from, so cover is evaluated against where the eyes really land when he ducks. `crouchEye` slider remains the no-anim-data fallback; HUD eye-band line shows `+N.NNm fwd — posed crouch eye anchor`.
  - **In-game test poses**: **Alt + mnemonic** latches a `PRIORITY_DEBUG` pose override — works on the **possessed** character AND on the **rig selected in Clothing tab's Character Control** (unpossessed). `CharacterRig`'s letter keys are now Alt-gated (bare letters fought the map camera's WASD — the original deprecation reason). `Alt+L` Looking, `Alt+I` Idle, `Alt+W` Walking, `Alt+J` AimWalk, `Alt+A` Aiming, `Alt+C` Crouching, `Alt+F` Flinching, `Alt+D` Down, `Alt+T` T-Pose, `Alt+S` AimSweep, `Alt+B` Sitting, **`Alt+X` release**. Possessed pose shows beside the crosshair.
  - **Animator round-trip fix (data loss)**: animator export only wrote anim/voxel fields — it silently dropped `assetType`, `itemParts` (weapon voxels!), `itemPartDefs`, `attachmentPoints` (hand mounts!), `attachRotation`, `bgColor`, and `"handedness":"unity"` on save — the last one silently un-marked the promoted rig → `WeaponMount`'s +180° unity-hand roll compensation skipped → **gun backwards in palm at idle** (IK masked it by re-solving every frame).
